12	# DateRangeField
13	
14	Inline two-field range input **without a popover**. Wraps two DateField instances with shared validation.

162	### 1.6 Full Machine Implementation
163	
164	```rust
165	/// Machine for the DateRangeField component.
166	pub struct Machine;
167	
168	impl ars_core::Machine for Machine {
169	    type State = State;
170	    type Event = Event;
171	    type Context = Context;
172	    type Props = Props;
173	    type Messages = Messages;
174	    type Api<'a> = Api<'a>;
175	
176	    fn init(props: &Self::Props, env: &Env, messages: &Self::Messages) -> (Self::State, Self::Context) {
177	        let locale = env.locale.clone();
178	        let ctx = Context {
179	            value: match &props.value {
180	                Some(v) => Bindable::controlled(v.clone()),
181	                None => Bindable::uncontrolled(props.default_value.clone()),
182	            },
183	            active_field: None,
184	            min: props.min.clone(),
185	            max: props.max.clone(),
186	            messages: messages.clone(),
187	            locale,
188	            disabled: props.disabled,
189	            readonly: props.readonly,
190	            required: props.required,
191	            name: props.name.clone(),
192	            start_name: props.start_name.clone(),
193	            end_name: props.end_name.clone(),
194	            ids: ComponentIds::from_id(&props.id),
195	            has_description: false,
196	            has_error_message: false,
197	            force_leading_zeros: props.force_leading_zeros,
198	        };
199	        (State::Idle, ctx)
200	    }
201	
202	    fn transition(
203	        state: &Self::State,
204	        event: &Self::Event,
205	        ctx: &Self::Context,
206	        _props: &Self::Props,
207	    ) -> Option<TransitionPlan<Self>> {
208	        if is_disabled(ctx) { return None; }
209	
210	        match event {
211	            Event::FocusStart => {
212	                Some(TransitionPlan::to(State::StartFocused).apply(|ctx| {
213	                    ctx.active_field = Some(ActiveField::Start);
214	                }))
215	            }
216	
217	            Event::FocusEnd => {
218	                Some(TransitionPlan::to(State::EndFocused).apply(|ctx| {
219	                    ctx.active_field = Some(ActiveField::End);
220	                }))
221	            }
222	
223	            Event::BlurAll => {
224	                Some(TransitionPlan::to(State::Idle).apply(|ctx| {
225	                    ctx.active_field = None;
226	                }))
227	            }
228	
229	            Event::SetRange(range) => {
230	                let range = range.clone();
231	                Some(TransitionPlan::context_only(move |ctx| {
232	                    ctx.value.set(range);
233	                }))
234	            }
235	
236	            Event::StartValueChange(date) => {
237	                let date = date.clone();
238	                Some(TransitionPlan::context_only(move |ctx| {
239	                    match (&date, ctx.value.get().as_ref().map(|r| &r.end)) {
240	                        (Some(start), Some(end)) => {
241	                            ctx.value.set(Some(DateRange::normalized(
242	                                start.clone(), end.clone(),
243	                            )));
244	                        }
245	                        (Some(start), None) => {
246	                            // Only start set; store partial (no complete range yet)
247	                            // Range will complete when end is also set.
248	                        }
249	                        (None, _) => {
250	                            ctx.value.set(None);
251	                        }
252	                    }
253	                }))
254	            }
255	
256	            Event::EndValueChange(date) => {
257	                let date = date.clone();
258	                Some(TransitionPlan::context_only(move |ctx| {
259	                    match (ctx.value.get().as_ref().map(|r| &r.start), &date) {
260	                        (Some(start), Some(end)) => {
261	                            ctx.value.set(Some(DateRange::normalized(
262	                                start.clone(), end.clone(),
263	                            )));
264	                        }
265	                        (None, Some(end)) => {
266	                            // Only end set; store partial.
267	                        }
268	                        (_, None) => {
269	                            ctx.value.set(None);
270	                        }
271	                    }
272	                }))
273	            }
274	        }
275	    }
276	}
277	```

279	### 1.7 Connect / API
280	
281	```rust
282	#[derive(ComponentPart)]
283	#[scope = "date-range-field"]
284	pub enum Part {
285	    Root,
286	    Label,
287	    StartField,
288	    Separator,
289	    EndField,
290	    Description,
291	    ErrorMessage,
292	    HiddenInput,
293	}
294	
295	/// API for the DateRangeField component.
296	pub struct Api<'a> {
297	    /// The state of the component.
298	    state: &'a State,
299	    /// The context of the component.
300	    ctx: &'a Context,
301	    /// The props of the component.
302	    props: &'a Props,
303	    /// The send function.
304	    send: &'a dyn Fn(Event),
305	}
306	
307	impl<'a> Api<'a> {
308	    // ── AttrMap getters ──────────────────────────────────────────────────
309	
310	    /// Attributes for the root element.
311	    pub fn root_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {
312	        let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();
313	        let [(scope_attr, scope_val), (part_attr, part_val)] = Part::Root.data_attrs();
314	        attrs.set(scope_attr, scope_val);
315	        attrs.set(part_attr, part_val);
316	        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Id, self.ctx.ids.id());
317	        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Role, "group");
318	        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::LabelledBy), self.ctx.ids.part("label"));
319	        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Data("ars-state"), self.state_name());
320	        let mut described_by = Vec::new();
321	        if self.ctx.has_description { described_by.push(self.ctx.ids.part("description")); }
322	        if self.ctx.has_error_message { described_by.push(self.ctx.ids.part("error-message")); }
323	        if !described_by.is_empty() {
324	            attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::DescribedBy), described_by.join(" "));
325	        }
326	        if self.ctx.disabled {
327	            attrs.set_bool(HtmlAttr::Data("ars-disabled"), true);
328	        }
329	        if self.ctx.readonly {
330	            attrs.set_bool(HtmlAttr::Data("ars-readonly"), true);
331	        }
332	        if self.ctx.required {
333	            attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::Required), "true");
334	        }
335	        attrs
336	    }
337	
338	    /// Attributes for the label element.
339	    pub fn label_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {
340	        let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();
341	        let [(scope_attr, scope_val), (part_attr, part_val)] = Part::Label.data_attrs();
342	        attrs.set(scope_attr, scope_val);
343	        attrs.set(part_attr, part_val);
344	        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Id, self.ctx.ids.part("label"));
345	        attrs
346	    }
347	
348	    /// Attributes for the separator element.
349	    pub fn separator_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {
350	        let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();
351	        let [(scope_attr, scope_val), (part_attr, part_val)] = Part::Separator.data_attrs();
352	        attrs.set(scope_attr, scope_val);
353	        attrs.set(part_attr, part_val);
354	        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::Hidden), "true");
355	        attrs
356	    }
357	
358	    /// The separator text between start and end fields (e.g., " – ").
359	    /// The adapter renders this as the text content of the Separator element.
360	    pub fn separator_text(&self) -> String {
361	        (self.ctx.messages.separator_text)(&self.ctx.locale)
362	    }
363	
364	    /// Screen reader description of the full date range (e.g., "March 1 to March 15").
365	    /// Returns `None` if no complete range is selected.
366	    /// The adapter can use this as an `aria-description` on the root or as a live announcement.
367	    pub fn range_description(&self) -> Option<String> {
368	        let range = self.ctx.value.get().as_ref()?;
369	        let start = format_date(&range.start, &self.ctx.format, &self.ctx.locale);
370	        let end = format_date(&range.end, &self.ctx.format, &self.ctx.locale);
371	        Some((self.ctx.messages.range_description)(&start, &end, &self.ctx.locale))
372	    }
373	
374	    /// Build DateField Props for the start date input.
375	    pub fn start_field_props(&self) -> date_field::Props {
376	        date_field::Props {
377	            id: self.ctx.ids.part("start"),
378	            value: self.ctx.value.get().as_ref().map(|r| Some(r.start.clone())),
379	            min: self.ctx.min.clone(),
380	            max: self.ctx.value.get().as_ref().map(|r| r.end.clone()),
381	            disabled: self.ctx.disabled,
382	            readonly: self.ctx.readonly,
383	            aria_label: Some((self.ctx.messages.start_label)(&self.ctx.locale)),
384	            force_leading_zeros: self.ctx.force_leading_zeros,
385	            ..Default::default()
386	        }
387	    }
388	
389	    /// Build DateField Props for the end date input.
390	    pub fn end_field_props(&self) -> date_field::Props {
391	        date_field::Props {
392	            id: self.ctx.ids.part("end"),
393	            value: self.ctx.value.get().as_ref().map(|r| Some(r.end.clone())),
394	            min: self.ctx.value.get().as_ref().map(|r| r.start.clone()),
395	            max: self.ctx.max.clone(),
396	            disabled: self.ctx.disabled,
397	            readonly: self.ctx.readonly,
398	            aria_label: Some((self.ctx.messages.end_label)(&self.ctx.locale)),
399	            force_leading_zeros: self.ctx.force_leading_zeros,
400	            ..Default::default()
401	        }
402	    }
403	
404	    /// Attributes for the description element.
405	    pub fn description_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {
406	        let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();
407	        let [(scope_attr, scope_val), (part_attr, part_val)] = Part::Description.data_attrs();
408	        attrs.set(scope_attr, scope_val);
409	        attrs.set(part_attr, part_val);
410	        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Id, self.ctx.ids.part("description"));
411	        attrs
412	    }
413	
414	    /// Attributes for the error message element.
415	    pub fn error_message_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {
416	        let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();
417	        let [(scope_attr, scope_val), (part_attr, part_val)] = Part::ErrorMessage.data_attrs();
418	        attrs.set(scope_attr, scope_val);
419	        attrs.set(part_attr, part_val);
420	        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Id, self.ctx.ids.part("error-message"));
421	        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Role, "alert");
422	        attrs
423	    }
424	
425	    /// Attributes for the hidden input element.
426	    pub fn hidden_input_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {
427	        let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();
428	        let [(scope_attr, scope_val), (part_attr, part_val)] = Part::HiddenInput.data_attrs();
429	        attrs.set(scope_attr, scope_val);
430	        attrs.set(part_attr, part_val);
431	        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Type, "hidden");
432	        if let Some(ref name) = self.ctx.name {
433	            attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Name, name);
434	        }
435	        let iso_value = match self.ctx.value.get() {
436	            Some(range) => format!(
437	                "{}/{}",
438	                format_iso(&range.start),
439	                format_iso(&range.end),
440	            ),
441	            None => String::new(),
442	        };
443	        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Value, iso_value);
444	        attrs
445	    }
446	
447	    /// Attributes for a separate hidden input carrying the start date.
448	    /// Only rendered when `start_name` is set on Props.
449	    pub fn start_hidden_input_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {
450	        let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();
451	        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Type, "hidden");
452	        if let Some(name) = &self.ctx.start_name {
453	            attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Name, name);
454	        }
455	        if let Some(range) = &self.ctx.value.get() {
456	            attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Value, format_iso(&range.start));
457	        }
458	        attrs
459	    }
460	
461	    /// Attributes for a separate hidden input carrying the end date.
462	    /// Only rendered when `end_name` is set on Props.
463	    pub fn end_hidden_input_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {
464	        let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();
465	        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Type, "hidden");
466	        if let Some(name) = &self.ctx.end_name {
467	            attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Name, name);
468	        }
469	        if let Some(range) = &self.ctx.value.get() {
470	            attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Value, format_iso(&range.end));
471	        }
472	        attrs
473	    }
474	
475	    // ── Convenience getters ─────────────────────────────────────────────
476	
477	    /// Get the selected range.
478	    pub fn selected_range(&self) -> Option<&DateRange> {
479	        self.ctx.value.get().as_ref()
480	    }
481	
482	    /// Whether either field is currently focused.
483	    pub fn is_focused(&self) -> bool {
484	        !matches!(self.state, State::Idle)
485	    }
486	
487	    /// Get the name of the current state.
488	    fn state_name(&self) -> &'static str {
489	        match self.state {
490	            State::Idle => "idle",
491	            State::StartFocused => "start-focused",
492	            State::EndFocused => "end-focused",
493	        }
494	    }
495	}
496	
497	impl ConnectApi for Api<'_> {
498	    type Part = Part;
499	
500	    fn part_attrs(&self, part: Part) -> AttrMap {
501	        match part {
502	            Part::Root => self.root_attrs(),
503	            Part::Label => self.label_attrs(),
504	            Part::StartField => self.start_field_props().into(), // delegated to DateField
505	            Part::Separator => self.separator_attrs(),
506	            Part::EndField => self.end_field_props().into(), // delegated to DateField
507	            Part::Description => self.description_attrs(),
508	            Part::ErrorMessage => self.error_message_attrs(),
509	            Part::HiddenInput => self.hidden_input_attrs(),
510	        }
511	    }
512	}
513	```

515	## 2. Anatomy
516	
517	```text
518	DateRangeField (en-US)
519	└── Root                                  role="group"  data-ars-scope="date-range-field"
520	    ├── Label                             "Trip dates"
521	    ├── StartField (DateField)            role="group"  aria-label="Start date"
522	    │   ├── Segment (Month)               role="spinbutton"  aria-label="Month"
523	    │   ├── Literal "/"                   aria-hidden="true"
524	    │   ├── Segment (Day)                 role="spinbutton"  aria-label="Day"
525	    │   ├── Literal "/"                   aria-hidden="true"
526	    │   └── Segment (Year)               role="spinbutton"  aria-label="Year"
527	    ├── Separator                         " – "  aria-hidden="true"
528	    ├── EndField (DateField)              role="group"  aria-label="End date"
529	    │   ├── Segment (Month)               role="spinbutton"  aria-label="Month"
530	    │   ├── Literal "/"                   aria-hidden="true"
531	    │   ├── Segment (Day)                 role="spinbutton"  aria-label="Day"
532	    │   ├── Literal "/"                   aria-hidden="true"
533	    │   └── Segment (Year)               role="spinbutton"  aria-label="Year"
534	    ├── Description                       "Select check-in and check-out dates"
535	    ├── ErrorMessage                      role="alert"
536	    └── HiddenInput                       type="hidden"  value="2025-06-01/2025-06-15"
537	```
538	
539	| Part           | Element                 | Description                                                    |
540	| -------------- | ----------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------- |
541	| `Root`         | `<div>`                 | `role="group"` — groups both fields; `aria-labelledby` → Label |
542	| `Label`        | `<label>`               | Shared label for the entire range                              |
543	| `StartField`   | _(DateField)_           | Start date segments (delegated to DateField)                   |
544	| `Separator`    | `<span>`                | `aria-hidden="true"` — visual separator (" -- " or "to")       |
545	| `EndField`     | _(DateField)_           | End date segments (delegated to DateField)                     |
546	| `Description`  | `<div>`                 | Optional help text; referenced by `aria-describedby`           |
547	| `ErrorMessage` | `<div role="alert">`    | Validation error text; announced immediately                   |
548	| `HiddenInput`  | `<input type="hidden">` | ISO 8601 range value for form submission                       |
549	
550	## 3. Accessibility
551	
552	### 3.1 ARIA Roles, States, and Properties
553	
554	| Part       | Role/Attribute     | Details                                                      |
555	| ---------- | ------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------ |
556	| Root       | `role="group"`     | `aria-labelledby` points to Label                            |
557	| StartField | --                 | Delegates to DateField; each segment has `role="spinbutton"` |
558	| EndField   | --                 | Delegates to DateField; each segment has `role="spinbutton"` |
559	| Root       | `aria-disabled`    | Present when disabled                                        |
560	| Root       | `aria-readonly`    | Present when readonly                                        |
561	| Root       | `data-ars-invalid` | Present when validation fails                                |
562	| Root       | `aria-describedby` | Wired to Description and ErrorMessage part IDs               |
563	| Root       | `aria-required`    | Present when required                                        |
564	
565	### 3.2 Keyboard Interaction
566	
567	| Key        | Action                                                          |
568	| ---------- | --------------------------------------------------------------- |
569	| Tab        | Move focus from last segment of StartField to first of EndField |
570	| Shift+Tab  | Move focus from first segment of EndField to last of StartField |
571	| ArrowUp    | Increment focused segment value                                 |
572	| ArrowDown  | Decrement focused segment value                                 |
573	| ArrowLeft  | Move to previous segment (within current field)                 |
574	| ArrowRight | Move to next segment (within current field)                     |
575	
576	## 4. Internationalization
577	
578	### 4.1 Messages
579	
580	```rust
581	#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
582	pub struct Messages {
583	    /// Label for the start date field.
584	    pub start_label: MessageFn<dyn Fn(&Locale) -> String + Send + Sync>,
585	    /// Label for the end date field.
586	    pub end_label: MessageFn<dyn Fn(&Locale) -> String + Send + Sync>,
587	    /// Text displayed between start and end fields.
588	    pub separator_text: MessageFn<dyn Fn(&Locale) -> String + Send + Sync>,
589	    /// Description of the range for screen readers.
590	    pub range_description: MessageFn<dyn Fn(&str, &str, &Locale) -> String + Send + Sync>,
591	}
592	
593	impl Default for Messages {
594	    fn default() -> Self {
595	        Self {
596	            start_label: MessageFn::static_str("Start date"),
597	            end_label: MessageFn::static_str("End date"),
598	            separator_text: MessageFn::static_str(" \u{2013} "),
599	            range_description: MessageFn::new(|start, end, _locale| {
600	                format!("{} to {}", start, end)
601	            }),
602	        }
603	    }
604	}
605	
606	impl ComponentMessages for Messages {}
607	```
608	
609	- Label text and separator are consumer-overridable via Messages.
610	- `data-ars-state` values are stable API tokens, not localized.
611	- RTL: segment ordering within each field follows locale conventions (delegated to DateField).
612	
613	## 5. Form Integration
614	
615	- Hidden `<input>` submits the current range in ISO 8601 interval format (`YYYY-MM-DD/YYYY-MM-DD`).
616	- `name` attribute is set from Props.
617	- Reset restores `default_value`.
618	- Validation states (`valid`, `invalid`) reflected via `data-ars-invalid` on Root.
619	- `aria-describedby` wires to Description and ErrorMessage parts.
620	- `aria-required` set when `required` is true.
621	- Disabled/readonly propagation from form context per `07-forms.md` S15.
622	
623	## 6. Library Parity
624	
625	> Compared against: React Aria (`DateRangePicker` field-only aspects).
626	
627	React Aria does not have a standalone `DateRangeField` component separate from `DateRangePicker`. The closest equivalent is the inline portion of `DateRangePicker` (two `DateInput` slots without the popover). ars-ui separates the range field into its own component for composability.
628	
629	### 6.1 Props
630	
631	| Feature               | ars-ui                | React Aria                  | Notes                          |
632	| --------------------- | --------------------- | --------------------------- | ------------------------------ |
633	| Controlled range      | `value`               | `value` (RangeValue)        | Equivalent                     |
634	| Default range         | `default_value`       | `defaultValue`              | Equivalent                     |
635	| Min/max               | `min`, `max`          | `minValue`, `maxValue`      | Equivalent                     |
636	| Disabled              | `disabled`            | `isDisabled`                | Equivalent                     |
637	| Read-only             | `readonly`            | `isReadOnly`                | Equivalent                     |
638	| Required              | `required`            | `isRequired`                | Equivalent                     |
639	| Locale                | `locale`              | -- (context)                | Equivalent                     |
640	| Name                  | `name`                | `startName`, `endName`      | React Aria uses separate names |
641	| Force leading zeros   | `force_leading_zeros` | `shouldForceLeadingZeros`   | Equivalent                     |
642	| Allows non-contiguous | --                    | `allowsNonContiguousRanges` | React Aria feature             |
643	| Start/end names       | --                    | `startName`, `endName`      | Separate form field names      |
644	
645	**Gaps:** None.
646	
647	### 6.2 Anatomy
648	
649	| Part          | ars-ui                   | React Aria                 | Notes                          |
650	| ------------- | ------------------------ | -------------------------- | ------------------------------ |
651	| Root          | `Root`                   | `DateRangePicker`          | Equivalent                     |
652	| Label         | `Label`                  | `Label`                    | Equivalent                     |
653	| Start field   | `StartField` (DateField) | `DateInput` (slot="start") | Equivalent                     |
654	| Separator     | `Separator`              | --                         | ars-ui explicit separator part |
655	| End field     | `EndField` (DateField)   | `DateInput` (slot="end")   | Equivalent                     |
656	| Description   | `Description`            | `Text` (slot)              | Equivalent                     |
657	| Error message | `ErrorMessage`           | `FieldError`               | Equivalent                     |
658	| Hidden input  | `HiddenInput`            | --                         | ars-ui form integration        |
659	
660	**Gaps:** None.
661	
662	### 6.3 Events
663	
664	| Callback     | ars-ui                                | React Aria      | Notes      |
665	| ------------ | ------------------------------------- | --------------- | ---------- |
666	| Range change | `StartValueChange` / `EndValueChange` | `onChange`      | Equivalent |
667	| Focus        | `FocusStart` / `FocusEnd`             | `onFocusChange` | Equivalent |
668	| Blur         | `BlurAll`                             | `onBlur`        | Equivalent |
669	
670	**Gaps:** None.
671	
672	### 6.4 Features
673	
674	| Feature                    | ars-ui | React Aria |
675	| -------------------------- | ------ | ---------- |
676	| Two-field range input      | Yes    | Yes        |
677	| Range normalization        | Yes    | Yes        |
678	| Cross-field tab navigation | Yes    | Yes        |
679	| Hidden form input          | Yes    | No         |
680	| Separate form names        | No     | Yes        |
681	
682	**Gaps:** None.
683	
684	### 6.5 Summary
685	
686	- **Overall:** Full parity.
687	- **Divergences:** React Aria bundles the range field into `DateRangePicker`; ars-ui separates it for composability. ars-ui adds a `Separator` part and hidden form input.
688	- **Recommended additions:** None.
689

697	    #[test]

699	        let (state, ctx) = Machine::init(&Props::default(), &Env::default(), &Default::default());

707	        let (state, ctx) = Machine::init(&props, &Env::default(), &Default::default());
708	        let plan = Machine::transition(&state, &Event::FocusStart, &ctx, &props).unwrap();
709	        assert_eq!(plan.target, Some(State::StartFocused));
710	
711	        let plan = Machine::transition(&State::StartFocused, &Event::FocusEnd, &ctx, &props).unwrap();
712	        assert_eq!(plan.target, Some(State::EndFocused));
713	
714	        let plan = Machine::transition(&State::EndFocused, &Event::BlurAll, &ctx, &props).unwrap();
715	        assert_eq!(plan.target, Some(State::Idle));
716	    }
717	
718	    #[test]

724	        let start = CalendarDate::new_gregorian(2025, nzu8(3), nzu8(15));
725	        // Simulates setting start > end; normalized should swap
726	    }
727	
728	    #[test]

730	        let props = Props { disabled: true, ..Props::default() };

732	        assert!(Machine::transition(&state, &Event::FocusStart, &ctx, &props).is_none());

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, ars_core::HasId)]
pub struct Props {
    pub id: String,                                    // Component ID
    pub value: Option<Option<CalendarDate>>,         // Controlled value
    pub default_value: Option<CalendarDate>,         // Uncontrolled initial
    pub calendar: CalendarSystem,                     // Gregorian, Japanese, etc.
    pub granularity: DateGranularity,                // Currently only Day
    pub min_value: Option<CalendarDate>,             // Min allowed date
    pub max_value: Option<CalendarDate>,             // Max allowed date
    pub disabled: bool,                               // Interaction blocked
    pub readonly: bool,                               // Editing blocked
    pub required: bool,                               // Form required
    pub auto_focus: bool,                             // Focus on mount
    pub label: String,                                // Visible label
    pub aria_label: Option<String>,                  // Accessible label
    pub aria_labelledby: Option<String>,             // Label reference ID
    pub aria_describedby: Option<String>,            // Description reference
    pub description: Option<String>,                  // Help text
    pub error_message: Option<String>,               // Validation error
    pub invalid: bool,                                // Invalid state flag
    pub name: Option<String>,                         // Hidden input name
    pub segment_order: Option<Vec<DateSegmentKind>>, // Custom segment order
    pub force_leading_zeros: bool,                   // Force "03" vs "3"
}

        Self {
            id: String::new(),
            value: None,
            default_value: None,
            calendar: CalendarSystem::Gregorian,
            granularity: DateGranularity::Day,

            description: None,
            error_message: None,
            invalid: false,
            name: None,
            segment_order: None,
            force_leading_zeros: false,
        }
    }
}

## Summary Report: Recent Composite Component Implementations

Based on my exploration of the codebase, here are the key findings for implementing `DateRangeField`:

### 1. RangeCalendar Implementation (Commit 134f9571)

**File:** `/Users/ericson/Workspace/Rust/ars-ui.worktrees/impl-284/crates/ars-components/src/date_time/range_calendar/mod.rs`

**Range Coordination Mechanisms:**

- **`DateRange` handling**: The spec imports `DateRange` from `ars_i18n` (line 29). Normalization happens via `DateRange::normalized(anchor.clone(), hovering.clone())` (lines 638, 1252), which automatically orders start/end.

- **`anchor_date` + `hovering_date`**: Two-step selection model—first click sets `anchor_date`, subsequent hover previews via `hovering_date`, second click normalizes and completes (lines 1251–1289).

- **Validation in `Context`**:
  - `is_in_range()` (line 615): Checks confirmed range
  - `is_in_hover_range()` (line 635): Preview range during pending selection
  - `is_anchor()` (line 648): First-click marker
  - `range_is_allowed()` (line 660): Enforces `allow_single_date_range`, `min_range_days`, `max_range_days`

- **No `ActiveField` enum** in RangeCalendar itself—it manages `anchor_date` state directly instead.

- **Normalization helper** (line 778): `sanitize_external_range()` validates incoming controlled values against span/bounds constraints.

### 2. Component Composition Pattern

**DateTimePicker is the primary example** (it composes DateField + TimeField + Calendar):

**File:** `/Users/ericson/Workspace/Rust/ars-ui.worktrees/impl-284/crates/ars-components/src/date_time/date_time_picker/mod.rs`

**How Child Delegation Works:**

The machine does NOT directly instantiate child Props—instead, the **`Api` struct exposes methods** that compute child Props on-the-fly (see `DatePicker` example below).

- **DatePicker approach** (lines 1690–1707): The `Api::calendar_props()` method constructs a `calendar::Props` struct by copying relevant fields from its own `context` and forwarding predicates:
  ```rust
  pub fn calendar_props(&self) -> calendar::Props {
      calendar::Props {
          id: format!("{}-calendar", self.ctx.ids.id()),
          value: Some(self.ctx.value.get().clone()),
          min: self.ctx.min.clone(),
          max: self.ctx.max.clone(),
          disabled: self.ctx.disabled,
          readonly: self.ctx.readonly,
          is_date_unavailable: self.props.is_date_unavailable.clone(),
          ..calendar::Props::default()
      }
  }
  ```

- **No Props composition in the core state machine**—delegation happens in the adapter layer (Dioxus/Leptos implementations) that embeds the child component directly.

- **Event bridging**: Child state changes arrive as parent events (e.g., `Event::SelectDate { date }` in DatePicker from the Calendar).

### 3. Test Structure for Range/Composite Components

**RangeCalendar test location:** `/Users/ericson/Workspace/Rust/ars-ui.worktrees/impl-284/crates/ars-components/src/date_time/range_calendar/tests.rs`

**Layout conventions:**

- **Unit tests inline**: Test functions directly in `tests.rs` using `#[test]` macros (line 50+).

- **Snapshot tests**: Uses `insta::assert_snapshot!` (line 14 import). Test naming: `snapshot_*` prefix (though RangeCalendar doesn't use this convention; see DateTimePicker tests for examples).

- **Snapshot fixture directory**: `/Users/ericson/Workspace/Rust/ars-ui.worktrees/impl-284/crates/ars-components/src/date_time/range_calendar/snapshots/`
  - Files named: `ars_components__date_time__range_calendar__tests__{test_name}.snap`
  - Example: `ars_components__date_time__range_calendar__tests__cell_trigger_anchor.snap`

- **Helper functions** (lines 18–40): 
  - `date()` / `range()` constructors for test data
  - `props()` / `service()` / `service_with()` for fixture setup
  - `snapshot_attrs()` / `attr()` for assertions

- **DateTimePicker tests** (better snapshot example): `/Users/ericson/Workspace/Rust/ars-ui.worktrees/impl-284/crates/ars-components/src/date_time/date_time_picker/tests.rs`
  - Header comment (lines 1–5) explicitly states: "Test names that begin with `snapshot_` use `insta::assert_snapshot!` and commit golden output under `snapshots/`."

### 4. Module Registration Pattern

**File:** `/Users/ericson/Workspace/Rust/ars-ui.worktrees/impl-284/crates/ars-components/src/date_time/mod.rs`

```rust
//! Date and time component machines.

/// Calendar machine.
pub mod calendar;

/// DateField machine.
pub mod date_field;

/// DatePicker machine.
pub mod date_picker;

/// DateTimePicker machine.
pub mod date_time_picker;

/// RangeCalendar machine.
pub mod range_calendar;

/// TimeField machine.
pub mod time_field;
```

**Pattern**: Simple `pub mod xxx;` declarations in category `mod.rs`. No re-exports needed for internal composition—composition happens in adapter code.

### 5. DateRangePicker & ActiveField Status

**DateRangePicker does NOT exist in implementation yet** (only in spec).

**`ActiveField` is defined in the spec, not code:**

**Spec location:** `/Users/ericson/Workspace/Rust/ars-ui.worktrees/impl-284/spec/components/date-time/date-range-picker.md` (lines 117–124):

```rust

**For DateRangeField**, the spec (lines 61–62) states it **reuses** `ActiveField` from DateRangePicker:

Since both use the same enum and both compose two DateFields, a natural home would be **a shared module** (e.g., `crates/ars-components/src/date_time/range_field_shared.rs` or similar), OR define it inline in the first composite that uses it (DateRangePicker) and re-export from DateRangeField's code if both exist.

**Current architecture:** No shared enum module exists yet. Define `ActiveField` in whichever component you implement first, or create `crates/ars-components/src/date_time/range_fields.rs` (plural) as a shared module.

### Key Takeaways for DateRangeField Implementation:

1. **Composition pattern**: Store both DateField values in `Context` (not Props), expose `start_field_props()` and `end_field_props()` methods on `Api` that compute child Props on demand.

2. **Range coordination**: Mirror RangeCalendar's `range_is_allowed()` + validation logic; use `DateRange::normalized()` for bidirectional safety.

3. **ActiveField tracking**: Use `Option<ActiveField>` in `Context` to track which field has focus (Start/End); transition state on `FocusStart`/`FocusEnd` events.

4. **Test layout**: `tests.rs` inline + `snapshots/` subdirectory with `insta::assert_snapshot!` calls. Follow DateTimePicker's naming convention (test names prefixed `snapshot_*`).

5. **Module registration**: Add `pub mod date_range_field;` to `/Users/ericson/Workspace/Rust/ars-ui.worktrees/impl-284/crates/ars-components/src/date_time/mod.rs`.

6. **ActiveField definition**: Create or inherit from shared location; DateRangeField spec already references it as coming from DateRangePicker, so define it there first (or in a dedicated shared range-field module).

## Summary of Type Definitions and File Locations

### 1. **DateRange** 
**File Path:** `/Users/ericson/Workspace/Rust/ars-ui.worktrees/impl-284/crates/ars-i18n/src/calendar/helpers.rs`

**Crate:** `ars-i18n`

**Definition:**
```rust
/// Inclusive date range.
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct DateRange {
    /// The inclusive range start.
    pub start: CalendarDate,

    /// The inclusive range end.
    pub end: CalendarDate,
}
```

**Methods:**
- `DateRange::new(start: CalendarDate, end: CalendarDate) -> Option<Self>` — Creates a range when `start <= end` within the same comparable calendar. Returns `None` if start > end or calendars/eras don't match.
- `DateRange::normalized(first_date: CalendarDate, second_date: CalendarDate) -> Option<Self>` — Creates a valid range by automatically ordering the two inputs. Swaps them if needed so the earliest date becomes `start`. Returns `None` if calendars/eras don't match.

- `day: u8` — 1-based day of month

18	/// The leap-year branch uses the normal Gregorian 400-year rule:
19	/// divisible by 4, except centuries that are not divisible by 400.

33	        _ => 30,
34	    }

173	pub(crate) const fn era_definitions(calendar: CalendarSystem) -> &'static [EraDefinition] {
174	    match calendar {
175	        CalendarSystem::Gregorian => &GREGORIAN_ERAS,
176	
177	        CalendarSystem::Buddhist => &BUDDHIST_ERAS,
178	
179	        CalendarSystem::Hebrew => &HEBREW_ERAS,
180	
181	        CalendarSystem::IslamicCivil | CalendarSystem::IslamicUmmAlQura => &ISLAMIC_ERAS,
182	
183	        CalendarSystem::Persian => &PERSIAN_ERAS,
184	
185	        CalendarSystem::Indian => &INDIAN_ERAS,
186	
187	        CalendarSystem::Coptic => &COPTIC_ERAS,
188	
189	        CalendarSystem::Ethiopic => &ETHIOPIC_ERAS,
190	
191	        CalendarSystem::EthiopicAmeteAlem => &ETHIOPIC_AMETE_ALEM_ERAS,
192	
193	        CalendarSystem::Roc => &ROC_ERAS,
194	
195	        CalendarSystem::Iso8601
196	        | CalendarSystem::Japanese
197	        | CalendarSystem::Chinese
198	        | CalendarSystem::Dangi => &[],
199	    }
200	}
201	
202	pub(crate) fn era_definition(calendar: CalendarSystem, era_code: &str) -> Option<EraDefinition> {
203	    if calendar == CalendarSystem::Japanese {
204	        return japanese_era_boundary(era_code).map(|boundary| EraDefinition {
205	            code: boundary.code,
206	            display_name: boundary.display_name,
207	            max_year: japanese_era_max_year(boundary),
208	        });
209	    }
210	
211	    era_definitions(calendar)
212	        .iter()
213	        .copied()
214	        .find(|definition| definition.code == era_code)
215	}
216	
217	fn japanese_era_boundary(code: &str) -> Option<&'static EraBoundary> {
218	    JAPANESE_ERA_BOUNDARIES
219	        .iter()
220	        .find(|boundary| boundary.code == code)
221	}
222	
223	fn japanese_era_max_year(boundary: &EraBoundary) -> Option<i32> {
224	    let (end_year, ..) = boundary.end?;
225	
226	    Some(end_year - boundary.start_year + 1)
227	}
228	
229	pub(crate) fn default_era_for(calendar: CalendarSystem) -> Option<Era> {
230	    if calendar == CalendarSystem::Japanese {
231	        return JAPANESE_ERA_BOUNDARIES.last().map(|boundary| Era {
232	            code: String::from(boundary.code),
233	            display_name: String::from(boundary.display_name),
234	        });
235	    }
236	
237	    era_definitions(calendar).last().map(|definition| Era {
238	        code: String::from(definition.code),
239	        display_name: String::from(definition.display_name),
240	    })
241	}
242	
243	pub(crate) fn bounded_months_in_year(
244	    calendar: CalendarSystem,
245	    year: i32,
246	    era: Option<&str>,
247	) -> Option<u8> {
248	    match calendar {
249	        CalendarSystem::Japanese => {
250	            let boundary = japanese_era_boundary(era?)?;
251	
252	            if let Some(max_year) = japanese_era_max_year(boundary)
253	                && year == max_year
254	            {
255	                boundary.end.map(|(_, month, _)| month)
256	            } else {
257	                Some(12)
258	            }
259	        }
260	
261	        _ => None,
262	    }
263	}
264	
265	pub(crate) fn bounded_days_in_month(
266	    calendar: CalendarSystem,
267	    year: i32,
268	    month: u8,
269	    era: Option<&str>,
270	) -> Option<u8> {
271	    match calendar {
272	        CalendarSystem::Japanese => {
273	            let boundary = japanese_era_boundary(era?)?;
274	
275	            // Japanese public dates store `year` as year-in-era, so convert it
276	            // back to the underlying Gregorian year before using Gregorian
277	            // month-length rules.
278	            let absolute_year = boundary.start_year.checked_add(year)?.checked_sub(1)?;
279	
280	            let mut max_day = gregorian_days_in_month(absolute_year, month);
281	
282	            if let Some((end_year, end_month, end_day)) = boundary.end {
283	                // The final month of a historical era may end early, e.g.
284	                // Heisei ends on 2019-04-30 instead of the full month.
285	                let max_year = end_year - boundary.start_year + 1;
286	
287	                if year == max_year && month == end_month {
288	                    max_day = max_day.min(end_day);
289	                }
290	            }
291	
292	            Some(max_day)
293	        }
294	        _ => None,
295	    }
296	}

298	/// Inclusive date range.
299	#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
300	pub struct DateRange {
301	    /// The inclusive range start.
302	    pub start: CalendarDate,
303	
304	    /// The inclusive range end.
305	    pub end: CalendarDate,
306	}
307	
308	impl DateRange {
309	    /// Creates a range when `start <= end` within the same comparable calendar.
310	    #[must_use]
311	    pub fn new(start: CalendarDate, end: CalendarDate) -> Option<Self> {
312	        match start.compare_within_calendar(&end) {
313	            Some(Ordering::Less | Ordering::Equal) => Some(Self { start, end }),
314	            Some(Ordering::Greater) | None => None,
315	        }
316	    }
317	
318	    /// Returns `true` if `date` is within the inclusive range.
319	    #[must_use]
320	    pub fn contains(&self, date: &CalendarDate) -> bool {
321	        date.is_between(&self.start, &self.end)
322	    }
323	
324	    /// Returns the range in `start/end` raw ISO field form.
325	    #[must_use]
326	    pub fn to_iso8601(&self) -> String {
327	        format!("{}/{}", self.start.to_iso8601(), self.end.to_iso8601())
328	    }
329	
330	    /// Creates a valid range by ordering the two comparable inputs.
331	    #[must_use]
332	    pub fn normalized(first_date: CalendarDate, second_date: CalendarDate) -> Option<Self> {
333	        match first_date.compare_within_calendar(&second_date) {
334	            Some(Ordering::Less | Ordering::Equal) => Some(Self {
335	                start: first_date,
336	                end: second_date,
337	            }),
338	
339	            Some(Ordering::Greater) => Some(Self {
340	                start: second_date,
341	                end: first_date,
342	            }),
343	
344	            None => None,
345	        }
346	    }
347	}
348	
349	/// Converts an ISO date to a day count relative to `0001-01-01`.
350	#[must_use]
351	#[cfg(test)]
352	pub(crate) fn iso_to_epoch_days(year: i32, month: u8, day: u8) -> i64 {
353	    // Shift Jan/Feb to the end of the previous year so the leap-day correction
354	    // can be handled uniformly with a March-based year.
355	    let year_adjustment = (14 - i64::from(month)) / 12;
356	
357	    let march_based_year = i64::from(year) + 4800 - year_adjustment;
358	
359	    let march_based_month = i64::from(month) + 12 * year_adjustment - 3;
360	
361	    // The remaining constants are from the standard Gregorian civil-date to
362	    // Julian day transform:
363	    // - `153` groups months into a repeating March-based pattern
364	    // - `32_045` removes the Julian-day epoch bias
365	    // - `GREGORIAN_JDN_OFFSET` rebases onto `0001-01-01 = 0`
366	    i64::from(day)
367	        + (153 * march_based_month + 2) / 5
368	        + 365 * march_based_year
369	        + march_based_year / 4
370	        - march_based_year / 100
371	        + march_based_year / 400
372	        - 32_045
373	        - GREGORIAN_JDN_OFFSET
374	}
375	
376	/// Converts an epoch-day count back to an ISO date.
377	#[must_use]
378	#[cfg(test)]
379	pub(crate) const fn epoch_days_to_iso(epoch_days: i64) -> (i32, u8, u8) {
380	    // First move back into the Julian-day numbering domain expected by the
381	    // inverse civil-date algorithm.
382	    let julian_day = epoch_days + GREGORIAN_JDN_OFFSET + 32_044;
383	
384	    // Split the day number across the Gregorian 400-year cycle (`146_097`
385	    // days), then the 4-year subcycle (`1_461` days).
386	    let century_cycles = (4 * julian_day + 3) / 146_097;
387	
388	    let days_of_century = julian_day - (146_097 * century_cycles) / 4;
389	
390	    let quad_year_cycles = (4 * days_of_century + 3) / 1_461;
391	
392	    let day_of_year_block = days_of_century - (1_461 * quad_year_cycles) / 4;
393	
394	    // `153` again encodes the March-based month pattern used by the forward
395	    // conversion. Converting back from that index yields the ISO month/year.
396	    let month_index = (5 * day_of_year_block + 2) / 153;
397	
398	    let day = (day_of_year_block - (153 * month_index + 2) / 5 + 1) as u8;
399	
400	    let month = (month_index + 3 - 12 * (month_index / 10)) as u8;
401	
402	    let year = (100 * century_cycles + quad_year_cycles - 4800 + month_index / 10) as i32;
403	
404	    (year, month, day)
405	}
406	
407	#[cfg(all(test, feature = "icu4x", feature = "std", not(target_arch = "wasm32")))]
408	pub(crate) fn platform_today_iso() -> Result<(i32, u8, u8), String> {
409	    use std::time::{SystemTime, UNIX_EPOCH};
410	
411	    let now = SystemTime::now()
412	        .duration_since(UNIX_EPOCH)
413	        .map_err(|error| error.to_string())?;
414	
415	    // Convert whole UTC days since 1970-01-01 back into the crate's
416	    // `0001-01-01`-relative epoch used by `epoch_days_to_iso`.
417	    let days = i64::try_from(now.as_secs() / 86_400)
418	        .map_err(|_| String::from("system time exceeds supported range"))?;
419	
420	    Ok(epoch_days_to_iso(days + UNIX_EPOCH_DAYS_FROM_CE))
421	}

430	pub(crate) fn platform_today_iso() -> Result<(i32, u8, u8), String> {
431	    let date = js_sys::Date::new_0();
432	
433	    let year = i32::try_from(date.get_utc_full_year())
434	        .map_err(|_| String::from("browser year out of range"))?;
435	
436	    let month = u8::try_from(date.get_utc_month() + 1)
437	        .map_err(|_| String::from("browser month out of range"))?;
438	
439	    let day =
440	        u8::try_from(date.get_utc_date()).map_err(|_| String::from("browser day out of range"))?;
441	
442	    Ok((year, month, day))
443	}

463	    fn japanese_date(

486	    #[test]
487	    fn month_length_helper_covers_gregorian_rules() {
488	        assert_eq!(gregorian_days_in_month(2024, 2), 29);
489	        assert_eq!(gregorian_days_in_month(2023, 2), 28);
490	        assert_eq!(gregorian_days_in_month(2024, 13), 30);
491	    }

493	    #[test]

495	        assert_eq!(

497	                .expect("Japanese default era")
498	                .code,

500	        );

503	                .expect("Heisei definition")

514	        let heisei_second_year = japanese_date("heisei", "Heisei", 2, 1, 1);

517	
518	        assert_eq!(heisei_start.minimum_month_in_year(), 1);
519	        assert_eq!(heisei_start.minimum_day_in_month(), 8);
520	        assert_eq!(heisei_second_year.minimum_month_in_year(), 1);
521	        assert_eq!(heisei_second_year.minimum_day_in_month(), 1);
522	        assert_eq!(reiwa_start.minimum_month_in_year(), 5);
523	        assert_eq!(reiwa_start.minimum_day_in_month(), 1);

101	/// Props for the `DateField` component.
102	#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, ars_core::HasId)]
103	pub struct Props {
104	    /// The ID of the field.
105	    pub id: String,
106	
107	    /// The externally controlled value of the field.
108	    ///
109	    /// `None` means the field is uncontrolled. `Some(None)` means the parent is
110	    /// controlling the field as empty. `Some(Some(date))` means the parent is
111	    /// controlling a concrete date.
112	    pub value: Option<Option<CalendarDate>>,
113	
114	    /// The initial value used when the field is uncontrolled.
115	    pub default_value: Option<CalendarDate>,
116	
117	    /// The calendar system used to validate and edit date values.
118	    pub calendar: CalendarSystem,
119	
120	    /// The editable date granularity.
121	    pub granularity: DateGranularity,
122	
123	    /// The minimum allowed date value.
124	    pub min_value: Option<CalendarDate>,
125	
126	    /// The maximum allowed date value.
127	    pub max_value: Option<CalendarDate>,
128	
129	    /// Whether the field is disabled.
130	    pub disabled: bool,
131	
132	    /// Whether the field is read-only.
133	    pub readonly: bool,
134	
135	    /// Whether the field is required for form submission.
136	    pub required: bool,
137	
138	    /// Whether the field should receive focus on mount.
139	    pub auto_focus: bool,
140	
141	    /// The visible label text of the field.
142	    pub label: String,
143	
144	    /// The accessible label when no visible label is used.
145	    pub aria_label: Option<String>,
146

=== spec_conformance/date_time.rs head + range_calendar test ===
//! Spec-conformance tests for `crates/ars-components/src/date_time/*`.
//!
//! Asserts each date-time component's `Part` enum matches the spec anatomy.

use ars_components::date_time::{
    calendar, date_field::DateSegmentKind, date_picker, date_time_picker, range_calendar,
    time_field,
};
use ars_i18n::{CalendarDate, Weekday};

use super::helper::assert_anatomy;

fn calendar_date_default_for_part_all() -> CalendarDate {
    // Mirrors the `#[part(default = ...)]` value on `calendar::Part::Cell`
    // and `::CellTrigger`. `Part::all()` constructs these variants with the
    // declared defaults, and the spec-conformance helper compares
    // `Part::all()[i]` against the supplied expected parts via `PartialEq`,
    // so the test must use the same date the derive emits.
    CalendarDate::new_gregorian(1, 1, 1).expect("0001-01-01 is a valid Gregorian date")
}

#[test]
fn calendar_anatomy_matches_spec() {
    let example = calendar_date_default_for_part_all();
    assert_anatomy(
        "calendar",
        &[
            (calendar::Part::Root, "root"),
            (calendar::Part::Header, "header"),
            (calendar::Part::PrevTrigger, "prev-trigger"),
            (calendar::Part::NextTrigger, "next-trigger"),
            (calendar::Part::Heading, "heading"),
            (calendar::Part::Grid, "grid"),
            (calendar::Part::GridGroup, "grid-group"),
            (calendar::Part::HeadRow, "head-row"),
            (
                calendar::Part::HeadCell {
                    day: Weekday::Monday,
                },
                "head-cell",
            ),
            (calendar::Part::Row { week_index: 0 }, "row"),
            (
                calendar::Part::Cell {
                    date: example.clone(),
                    offset: 0,
                },
                "cell",
            ),
            (
                calendar::Part::CellTrigger {
                    date: example.clone(),
                    offset: 0,
                },
                "cell-trigger",
            ),
        ],
    );
}

    type State = State;

760	
761	    /// External configuration passed in by the parent component.
762	    type Props: Clone + PartialEq + HasId;

764	    /// Per-component i18n messages type.
765	    type Messages: ComponentMessages + Clone + Default + 'static;
766	
767	    /// Typed identifier for the named effect intents this machine emits.
768	    ///
769	    /// Each component declares its own enum (e.g. `popover::Effect`,
770	    /// `dialog::Effect`) — every variant is a stable identifier the
771	    /// compiler treats as the single source of truth. Adapters that
772	    /// route on names use exhaustive `match effect.name { … }` so a new
773	    /// variant added in the component forces every adapter to update.
774	    ///
775	    /// Machines that never emit named effects use [`NoEffect`]; the
776	    /// uninhabited type makes `PendingEffect::<_>::named(...)`
777	    /// uncallable for that machine.
778	    ///
779	    /// Prototypes and internal test fixtures can use
780	    /// `type Effect = &'static str` — `&'static str` already implements
781	    /// [`EffectName`] — but production machines should prefer a
782	    /// bespoke enum so authoring code, adapter dispatch, and test
783	    /// fixtures share one typed identifier. See [`EffectName`] for the
784	    /// full bound rationale.
785	    type Effect: EffectName;

795	    /// The adapter resolves `env` (locale, ICU provider) and `messages` from
796	    /// `ArsProvider` context before calling this method. Core code never calls
797	    /// framework hooks — all environment values arrive as parameters.
798	    fn init(
799	        props: &Self::Props,

801	        messages: &Self::Messages,
802	    ) -> (Self::State, Self::Context);

//! Unit and snapshot tests for the `DatePicker` component.
//!
//! Test names that begin with `snapshot_` use `insta::assert_snapshot!` and
//! commit golden output under `snapshots/`. Every other test is a pure
//! state-machine or connect-API assertion that does not depend on `.snap`
//! files.

use alloc::{format, string::String, sync::Arc, vec, vec::Vec};
use core::cell::RefCell;

use ars_core::{AriaAttr, AttrMap, Callback, ComponentPart, Env, HtmlAttr, SendResult, Service};
use ars_i18n::{
    CalendarDate, Locale, StubIntlBackend,
    locales::{de_de, en_gb, en_us, ja_jp},
};
use ars_interactions::{KeyboardEventData, KeyboardKey};
use insta::assert_snapshot;

use super::{calendar, *};

// ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Helpers
// ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

fn date(year: i32, month: u8, day: u8) -> CalendarDate {
    CalendarDate::new_gregorian(year, month, day).expect("valid test date")
}

fn props() -> Props {
    Props {
        id: String::from("date-picker"),
        label: String::from("Date"),
        ..Props::default()
    }
}

fn env(locale: Locale) -> Env {
    Env::new(locale, Arc::new(StubIntlBackend))
}

fn service() -> Service<Machine> {
    Service::<Machine>::new(props(), &env(en_us()), &Messages::default())
}

fn service_with(props: Props, locale: Locale) -> Service<Machine> {
    Service::<Machine>::new(props, &env(locale), &Messages::default())
}

fn snapshot_attrs(attrs: &AttrMap) -> String {
    format!("{attrs:#?}")
}

fn attr(attrs: &AttrMap, key: HtmlAttr) -> Option<String> {
    attrs.get(&key).map(ToString::to_string)
}

fn effects(result: SendResult<Machine>) -> Vec<Effect> {
    result
        .pending_effects
        .into_iter()
        .map(|effect| effect.name)
        .collect()
}

fn initial_effect_names(service: &mut Service<Machine>) -> Vec<Effect> {
    service
        .take_initial_effects()
        .iter()
        .map(|effect| effect.name)
        .collect()
}

    KeyboardEventData {
        key,

I now have a complete picture. I found several spec/code mismatches I'll resolve in-task, but one is a genuine design ambiguity worth confirming: the issue calls for a "validation error on invalid range," while the spec's state machine *normalizes* (swaps) start/end via `DateRange::normalized` so `start > end` can never persist.

> TOOL

id: toolu_01VSgxyXM9X6Tgi8UKD2MzPF
```json
{
  "questions": [
    {
      "question": "The spec machine normalizes start/end (swaps so start<=end always holds), but the issue's test says 'Start date cannot exceed end date (validation error on invalid range)' and spec prose §3.1/§5 call for a `data-ars-invalid` on Root that the spec's Api code never actually emits. How should the agnostic core treat range validity?",
      "header": "Validation",
      "multiSelect": false,
      "options": [
        {
          "label": "Normalize + invalid flag (recommended)",
          "description": "Keep the spec's swap-normalization so start<=end is a structural invariant, AND derive an `invalid` state from min/max bound violations exposed as `data-ars-invalid` on Root + an `is_invalid()` Api getter. The 'invalid range' test asserts (a) start>end input is swapped to a valid ordering, and (b) a range outside [min,max] surfaces data-ars-invalid. Resolves the spec's prose/code gap; keeps spec's DateRange::normalized design."
        },
        {
          "label": "Flag start>end as invalid (no swap)",
          "description": "Change the machine to stop swapping: store the raw start/end and set data-ars-invalid when start>end (and on min/max violations). The 'invalid range' test asserts data-ars-invalid appears when start>end. This contradicts the spec's explicit use of DateRange::normalized and would require rewriting §1.6 transitions away from normalization."
        }
      ]
    }
  ]
}

  "content": "# Task #284 — Implement DateRangeField agnostic core\n\n## Context\n\nGitHub issue #284 (epic #224, depends on #262 DateField which is **closed/merged**) asks for the\nframework-agnostic core of `DateRangeField`: a stateful composition of two `DateField` instances\n(start/end) with shared range coordination, validation, and form integration. No adapter\n(Leptos/Dioxus) work is in scope.\n\nThe component owns: segment value coordination across two child fields, range normalization,\nmin/max coordination between the fields, focus-field tracking (start vs end), ARIA/`data-ars-*`\nattrs (`role=\"group\"` with labelled sub-groups), a visual separator, and hidden form inputs\n(combined ISO interval and/or separate start/end names).\n\nSpec: `spec/components/date-time/date-range-field.md` (tier: stateful).\nImplementation lives in `ars-components`, mirroring the existing `date_field`, `range_calendar`,\nand `date_picker` conventions.\n\n## Spec/code mismatches to resolve in this PR\n\nThe spec's code examples were written against APIs that drifted. Per the spec-sync rule\n(CLAUDE.md) these are fixed in `spec/components/date-time/date-range-field.md` **in this PR**, and\nthe implementation follows the corrected contract:\n\n1. **`date_field::Props` field names** — spec `start_field_props`/`end_field_props` use `min`/`max`;\n   the real [`date_field::Props`](crates/ars-components/src/date_time/date_field/mod.rs#L101) uses\n   `min_value`/`max_value`. Map accordingly.\n2. **`DateRange::normalized` / `DateRange::new` return `Option<Self>`** (see\n   [`helpers.rs`](crates/ars-i18n/src/calendar/helpers.rs#L308)) — spec does\n   `ctx.value.set(Some(DateRange::normalized(..)))` which is a type error. Use\n   `if let Some(range) = DateRange::normalized(start, end) { ctx.value.set(Some(range)) }`.\n3. **`range_description` references nonexistent `self.ctx.format` and a nonexistent free\n   `format_date`** — replace with locale-aware formatting via an `IntlBackend`, mirroring\n   `range_calendar::format_date_label` ([range_calendar/mod.rs:2133](crates/ars-components/src/date_time/range_calendar/mod.rs#L2133)).\n   This requires adding `intl_backend: Arc<dyn IntlBackend>` to `Context` (DateField/RangeCalendar\n   both carry it; resolved from `env.intl_backend` in `init`).\n4. **`format_iso(...)` free fn does not exist** — use `CalendarDate::to_iso8601()` and\n   `DateRange::to_iso8601()` (which already emits `start/end`).\n5. **`ActiveField` does not exist** (DateRangePicker not yet implemented) — define\n   `pub enum ActiveField { Start, End }` in the `date_range_field` module, documented, so a later\n   DateRangePicker task imports it. Update the spec comment accordingly.\n6. **`CalendarDate::new_gregorian` takes `u8` and returns `Result`** (not `NonZeroU8`/`nzu8`) —\n   the spec appendix `nzu8(..)` test snippets are corrected to the `date(y,m,d)` helper pattern\n   used across the date-time test suites.\n7. **Validation gap** — spec §3.1/§5 promise `data-ars-invalid` on Root, but the spec `Context`\n   has no `invalid` field and `root_attrs` never emits it. **Resolution (user-confirmed):\n   normalize + invalid flag.** Keep `DateRange::normalized` (start<=end is a structural invariant),\n   and additionally derive an `invalid` state from min/max bound violations, surfaced as\n   `data-ars-invalid` on Root and an `is_invalid()` Api getter. Add the derivation to spec §1.7.\n8. **Min/max applied to both fields** — refine the child-prop coordination so global bounds always\n   reach both fields while preserving cross-field coordination:\n   - start: `min_value = ctx.min`, `max_value = end-if-range-set else ctx.max`\n   - end:   `min_value = start-if-range-set else ctx.min`, `max_value = ctx.max`\n   Update spec §1.7 to match.\n\n## Module layout (new)\n\n- `crates/ars-components/src/date_time/date_range_field/mod.rs` — `State`, `Event`, `Context`,\n  `Props`, `ActiveField`, `Machine`, `Part`, `Api`, `Messages`. `type Effect = NoEffect`,\n  `type Api<'a> = Api<'a>`.\n- `crates/ars-components/src/date_time/date_range_field/tests.rs` — unit + snapshot tests\n  (`#[cfg(test)] mod tests;` from `mod.rs`).\n- `crates/ars-components/src/date_time/date_range_field/snapshots/` — committed `.snap` insta\n  fixtures.\n- Register `pub mod date_range_field;` in\n  [`date_time/mod.rs`](crates/ars-components/src/date_time/mod.rs).\n\n## Implementation (TDD: tests first)\n\nFollow the `date_field`/`date_picker` test scaffolding exactly (helpers `date()`, `props()`,\n`env()`, `service()`, `snapshot_attrs()`, `attr()` — see\n[date_picker/tests.rs:1-75](crates/ars-components/src/date_time/date_picker/tests.rs)).\n`Env::new(locale, Arc::new(StubIntlBackend))`; `Service::<Machine>::new(props, &env, &Messages::default())`.\n\n### State machine (spec §1.1–1.6)\n- States `Idle | StartFocused | EndFocused`; Events `FocusStart | FocusEnd | BlurAll |\n  SetRange(Option<DateRange>) | StartValueChange(Option<CalendarDate>) | EndValueChange(Option<CalendarDate>)`.\n- `Context`: `value: Bindable<Option<DateRange>>`, `active_field: Option<ActiveField>`,\n  `min`/`max: Option<CalendarDate>`, `locale`, `intl_backend` (added), `messages`, `disabled`,\n  `readonly`, `required`, `name`/`start_name`/`end_name`, `ids: ComponentIds`,\n  `has_description`/`has_error_message`, `force_leading_zeros`. Manual `Debug` (backend is `dyn`).\n- `transition`: guard `is_disabled` returns `None`; focus events set `active_field` + state; blur\n  resets; `SetRange` sets value; `StartValueChange`/`EndValueChange` coordinate + normalize via\n  `DateRange::normalized(..)` (the `Option` is matched, not unwrapped).\n- Document every public item (`missing_docs`). Use `.expect(\"reason\")`, `#[expect(..)]` not\n  `#[allow]`, descriptive names, owned strings into `set()`.\n\n### Connect / Api (spec §1.7)\n- `#[derive(ComponentPart)] #[scope = \"date-range-field\"] enum Part { Root, Label, StartField,\n  Separator, EndField, Description, ErrorMessage, HiddenInput }`.\n- `root_attrs`: scope/part data attrs, id, `role=\"group\"`, `aria-labelledby`→label, `data-ars-state`,\n  `aria-describedby` (description + error-message when present), `data-ars-disabled`,\n  `data-ars-readonly`, `aria-required`, and **`data-ars-invalid` when `is_invalid()`**.\n- `label_attrs`, `separator_attrs` (`aria-hidden`), `separator_text()`, `description_attrs`,\n  `error_message_attrs` (`role=\"alert\"`), `hidden_input_attrs` (combined `start/end` ISO via\n  `DateRange::to_iso8601`), `start_hidden_input_attrs`/`end_hidden_input_attrs` (per\n  `start_name`/`end_name`, value via `CalendarDate::to_iso8601`).\n- `start_field_props()`/`end_field_props()` → `date_field::Props` with corrected `min_value`/\n  `max_value` coordination (mismatch #8), `aria_label` from messages, `force_leading_zeros` passed\n  through. `part_attrs` delegates `StartField`/`EndField` via `.into()` on the child Props.\n- `range_description()` → `Option<String>` using `intl_backend` + a local `format_date_label`-style\n  helper and the `range_description` message.\n- `selected_range()`, `is_focused()`, `is_invalid()`, `state_name()`.\n\n### Messages (spec §4.1)\n`start_label`, `end_label`, `separator_text`, `range_description` with the spec defaults;\n`impl ComponentMessages for Messages`.\n\n## Tests to add (issue \"Tests to add first\" + acceptance criteria)\n\nUnit/connect tests in `tests.rs`:\n- Both start and end field props are produced (`StartField`/`EndField` parts present).\n- Setting start>end normalizes to start<=end (invariant holds); a range outside `[min,max]`\n  surfaces `data-ars-invalid` + `is_invalid()` true.\n- Focus tracking: `FocusStart`→`StartFocused`, `FocusEnd`→`EndFocused`, `BlurAll`→`Idle`\n  (Tab-sequencing is adapter-resolved; assert the focus-state machine here).\n- `root_attrs` → `role=\"group\"`; start/end field props carry their own `role=\"group\"` sub-grouping\n  (via `date_field` group attrs) with distinct ids/aria-labels.\n- Separator part present with `aria-hidden` + `separator_text`.\n- Form: combined hidden input ISO `start/end`; separate `start_name`/`end_name` hidden inputs.\n- Min/max applied to both fields (assert `min_value`/`max_value` on both child props).\n- `disabled` ignores events; required sets `aria-required`.\n\nSnapshot tests (`snapshot_*`, `insta::assert_snapshot!`): `root_attrs` for each output-affecting\nstate/branch (idle, start-focused, end-focused, disabled/readonly, required, invalid,\nwith-description/error), label, separator, description, error-message, hidden-input (empty + set),\nstart/end hidden inputs, and start/end field props. Commit `.snap` fixtures; verify with\n`cargo insta test --unreferenced=reject`.\n\nSpec-conformance: add `date_range_field_anatomy_matches_spec` to\n[tests/spec_conformance/date_time.rs](crates/ars-components/tests/spec_conformance/date_time.rs)\nvia `assert_anatomy`, listing all 8 parts.\n\nProptest: add a state-machine block to\n[tests/proptest_state_machines/date_time.rs](crates/ars-components/tests/proptest_state_machines/date_time.rs)\nasserting invariants (e.g. stored range always has start<=end after arbitrary\nSetRange/StartValueChange/EndValueChange sequences; disabled never transitions). Use\n`super::common::proptest_config()`.\n\n## Post-implementation audit (MANDATORY)\n\nAfter named tests pass, invoke the `post-implementation-audit` skill: (a) spec/impl drift, (b) ≥2\n\"anything missing?\" passes, (c) test-coverage audit. Land every finding in this PR. Confirm the\nspec edits (mismatches #1–#8) are consistent end-to-end (Context, transitions, Api, anatomy table,\n§3/§5 prose, appendix tests).\n\n## Verification\n\n```bash\n# Focused during development\ncargo test -p ars-components date_range_field\ncargo test -p ars-components --test spec_conformance date_range_field\ncargo test -p ars-components --test proptest_state_machines date_time\ncargo insta test -p ars-components --review            # then accept intentional snapshots\ncargo insta test -p ars-components --unreferenced=reject\n\n# Pre-push gate (after user approval), per CLAUDE.md step 9\ncargo xci-fast      # fmt, clippy, unit/integration/adapter, snapshot-count, native coverage\n```\n\nZero warnings required. ## Approved Plan (edited by user):
# Task #284 — Implement DateRangeField agnostic core

## Context

GitHub issue #284 (epic #224, depends on #262 DateField which is **closed/merged**) asks for the
framework-agnostic core of `DateRangeField`: a stateful composition of two `DateField` instances
(start/end) with shared range coordination, validation, and form integration. No adapter
(Leptos/Dioxus) work is in scope.

The component owns: segment value coordination across two child fields, range normalization,
min/max coordination between the fields, focus-field tracking (start vs end), ARIA/`data-ars-*`
attrs (`role="group"` with labelled sub-groups), a visual separator, and hidden form inputs
(combined ISO interval and/or separate start/end names).

Spec: `spec/components/date-time/date-range-field.md` (tier: stateful).
Implementation lives in `ars-components`, mirroring the existing `date_field`, `range_calendar`,
and `date_picker` conventions.

## Spec/code mismatches to resolve in this PR

The spec's code examples were written against APIs that drifted. Per the spec-sync rule
(CLAUDE.md) these are fixed in `spec/components/date-time/date-range-field.md` **in this PR**, and
the implementation follows the corrected contract:

1. **`date_field::Props` field names** — spec `start_field_props`/`end_field_props` use `min`/`max`;
   the real [`date_field::Props`](crates/ars-components/src/date_time/date_field/mod.rs#L101) uses
   `min_value`/`max_value`. Map accordingly.
2. **`DateRange::normalized` / `DateRange::new` return `Option<Self>`** (see
   [`helpers.rs`](crates/ars-i18n/src/calendar/helpers.rs#L308)) — spec does
   `ctx.value.set(Some(DateRange::normalized(..)))` which is a type error. Use
   `if let Some(range) = DateRange::normalized(start, end) { ctx.value.set(Some(range)) }`.
3. **`range_description` references nonexistent `self.ctx.format` and a nonexistent free
   `format_date`** — replace with locale-aware formatting via an `IntlBackend`, mirroring
   `range_calendar::format_date_label` ([range_calendar/mod.rs:2133](crates/ars-components/src/date_time/range_calendar/mod.rs#L2133)).
   This requires adding `intl_backend: Arc<dyn IntlBackend>` to `Context` (DateField/RangeCalendar
   both carry it; resolved from `env.intl_backend` in `init`).
4. **`format_iso(...)` free fn does not exist** — use `CalendarDate::to_iso8601()` and
   `DateRange::to_iso8601()` (which already emits `start/end`).
5. **`ActiveField` does not exist** (DateRangePicker not yet implemented) — define
   `pub enum ActiveField { Start, End }` in the `date_range_field` module, documented, so a later
   DateRangePicker task imports it. Update the spec comment accordingly.
6. **`CalendarDate::new_gregorian` takes `u8` and returns `Result`** (not `NonZeroU8`/`nzu8`) —
   the spec appendix `nzu8(..)` test snippets are corrected to the `date(y,m,d)` helper pattern
   used across the date-time test suites.
7. **Validation gap** — spec §3.1/§5 promise `data-ars-invalid` on Root, but the spec `Context`
   has no `invalid` field and `root_attrs` never emits it. **Resolution (user-confirmed):
   normalize + invalid flag.** Keep `DateRange::normalized` (start<=end is a structural invariant),
   and additionally derive an `invalid` state from min/max bound violations, surfaced as
   `data-ars-invalid` on Root and an `is_invalid()` Api getter. Add the derivation to spec §1.7.
8. **Min/max applied to both fields** — refine the child-prop coordination so global bounds always
   reach both fields while preserving cross-field coordination:
   - start: `min_value = ctx.min`, `max_value = end-if-range-set else ctx.max`
   - end:   `min_value = start-if-range-set else ctx.min`, `max_value = ctx.max`
   Update spec §1.7 to match.

## Module layout (new)

- `crates/ars-components/src/date_time/date_range_field/mod.rs` — `State`, `Event`, `Context`,
  `Props`, `ActiveField`, `Machine`, `Part`, `Api`, `Messages`. `type Effect = NoEffect`,
  `type Api<'a> = Api<'a>`.
- `crates/ars-components/src/date_time/date_range_field/tests.rs` — unit + snapshot tests
  (`#[cfg(test)] mod tests;` from `mod.rs`).
- `crates/ars-components/src/date_time/date_range_field/snapshots/` — committed `.snap` insta
  fixtures.
- Register `pub mod date_range_field;` in
  [`date_time/mod.rs`](crates/ars-components/src/date_time/mod.rs).

## Implementation (TDD: tests first)

Follow the `date_field`/`date_picker` test scaffolding exactly (helpers `date()`, `props()`,
`env()`, `service()`, `snapshot_attrs()`, `attr()` — see
[date_picker/tests.rs:1-75](crates/ars-components/src/date_time/date_picker/tests.rs)).
`Env::new(locale, Arc::new(StubIntlBackend))`; `Service::<Machine>::new(props, &env, &Messages::default())`.

### State machine (spec §1.1–1.6)
- States `Idle | StartFocused | EndFocused`; Events `FocusStart | FocusEnd | BlurAll |
  SetRange(Option<DateRange>) | StartValueChange(Option<CalendarDate>) | EndValueChange(Option<CalendarDate>)`.
- `Context`: `value: Bindable<Option<DateRange>>`, `active_field: Option<ActiveField>`,
  `min`/`max: Option<CalendarDate>`, `locale`, `intl_backend` (added), `messages`, `disabled`,
  `readonly`, `required`, `name`/`start_name`/`end_name`, `ids: ComponentIds`,
  `has_description`/`has_error_message`, `force_leading_zeros`. Manual `Debug` (backend is `dyn`).
- `transition`: guard `is_disabled` returns `None`; focus events set `active_field` + state; blur
  resets; `SetRange` sets value; `StartValueChange`/`EndValueChange` coordinate + normalize via
  `DateRange::normalized(..)` (the `Option` is matched, not unwrapped).
- Document every public item (`missing_docs`). Use `.expect("reason")`, `#[expect(..)]` not
  `#[allow]`, descriptive names, owned strings into `set()`.

### Connect / Api (spec §1.7)
- `#[derive(ComponentPart)] #[scope = "date-range-field"] enum Part { Root, Label, StartField,
  Separator, EndField, Description, ErrorMessage, HiddenInput }`.
- `root_attrs`: scope/part data attrs, id, `role="group"`, `aria-labelledby`→label, `data-ars-state`,
  `aria-describedby` (description + error-message when present), `data-ars-disabled`,
  `data-ars-readonly`, `aria-required`, and **`data-ars-invalid` when `is_invalid()`**.
- `label_attrs`, `separator_attrs` (`aria-hidden`), `separator_text()`, `description_attrs`,
  `error_message_attrs` (`role="alert"`), `hidden_input_attrs` (combined `start/end` ISO via
  `DateRange::to_iso8601`), `start_hidden_input_attrs`/`end_hidden_input_attrs` (per
  `start_name`/`end_name`, value via `CalendarDate::to_iso8601`).
- `start_field_props()`/`end_field_props()` → `date_field::Props` with corrected `min_value`/
  `max_value` coordination (mismatch #8), `aria_label` from messages, `force_leading_zeros` passed
  through. `part_attrs` delegates `StartField`/`EndField` via `.into()` on the child Props.
- `range_description()` → `Option<String>` using `intl_backend` + a local `format_date_label`-style
  helper and the `range_description` message.
- `selected_range()`, `is_focused()`, `is_invalid()`, `state_name()`.

### Messages (spec §4.1)
`start_label`, `end_label`, `separator_text`, `range_description` with the spec defaults;
`impl ComponentMessages for Messages`.

## Tests to add (issue "Tests to add first" + acceptance criteria)

Unit/connect tests in `tests.rs`:
- Both start and end field props are produced (`StartField`/`EndField` parts present).
- Setting start>end normalizes to start<=end (invariant holds); a range outside `[min,max]`
  surfaces `data-ars-invalid` + `is_invalid()` true.
- Focus tracking: `FocusStart`→`StartFocused`, `FocusEnd`→`EndFocused`, `BlurAll`→`Idle`
  (Tab-sequencing is adapter-resolved; assert the focus-state machine here).
- `root_attrs` → `role="group"`; start/end field props carry their own `role="group"` sub-grouping
  (via `date_field` group attrs) with distinct ids/aria-labels.
- Separator part present with `aria-hidden` + `separator_text`.
- Form: combined hidden input ISO `start/end`; separate `start_name`/`end_name` hidden inputs.
- Min/max applied to both fields (assert `min_value`/`max_value` on both child props).
- `disabled` ignores events; required sets `aria-required`.

Snapshot tests (`snapshot_*`, `insta::assert_snapshot!`): `root_attrs` for each output-affecting
state/branch (idle, start-focused, end-focused, disabled/readonly, required, invalid,
with-description/error), label, separator, description, error-message, hidden-input (empty + set),
start/end hidden inputs, and start/end field props. Commit `.snap` fixtures; verify with
`cargo insta test --unreferenced=reject`.

Spec-conformance: add `date_range_field_anatomy_matches_spec` to
[tests/spec_conformance/date_time.rs](crates/ars-components/tests/spec_conformance/date_time.rs)
via `assert_anatomy`, listing all 8 parts.

Proptest: add a state-machine block to
[tests/proptest_state_machines/date_time.rs](crates/ars-components/tests/proptest_state_machines/date_time.rs)
asserting invariants (e.g. stored range always has start<=end after arbitrary
SetRange/StartValueChange/EndValueChange sequences; disabled never transitions). Use
`super::common::proptest_config()`.

## Post-implementation audit (MANDATORY)

After named tests pass, invoke the `post-implementation-audit` skill: (a) spec/impl drift, (b) ≥2
"anything missing?" passes, (c) test-coverage audit. Land every finding in this PR. Confirm the
spec edits (mismatches #1–#8) are consistent end-to-end (Context, transitions, Api, anatomy table,
§3/§5 prose, appendix tests).

## Verification

```bash
# Focused during development
cargo test -p ars-components date_range_field
cargo test -p ars-components --test spec_conformance date_range_field
cargo test -p ars-components --test proptest_state_machines date_time
cargo insta test -p ars-components --review            # then accept intentional snapshots
cargo insta test -p ars-components --unreferenced=reject

# Pre-push gate (after user approval), per CLAUDE.md step 9
cargo xci-fast      # fmt, clippy, unit/integration/adapter, snapshot-count, native coverage
```

Zero warnings required. Then: present for user review → on approval `cargo xci-fast` → commit +
push PR to `main` with `Closes #284` → add `snapshot-reviewed` label (new `.snap` fixtures) →
invoke `waiting-for-codex-review` skill and loop until 👍 → merge → close issue.

```

748	/// A `Machine` declares the component's state type, event type, internal context,
749	/// props, and connect API. It provides pure functions for initialization, transition
750	/// logic, and DOM attribute generation — with no framework dependency.
751	pub trait Machine: Sized + 'static {
752	    /// The state type representing the machine's current configuration.
753	    type State: Clone + Debug + PartialEq;
754	
755	    /// The event type that triggers state transitions.
756	    type Event: Clone + Debug;
757	
758	    /// Internal context accumulated across transitions (e.g. focused index, scroll offset).
759	    type Context: Clone + Debug;
760	
761	    /// External configuration passed in by the parent component.
762	    type Props: Clone + PartialEq + HasId;
```
