  "content": "//! DateRangeField component state machine and connect API.\n//!\n//! This module implements the framework-agnostic two-field date range input\n//! defined in `spec/components/date-time/date-range-field.md`. It composes two\n//! [`date_field`] instances (start and end) with shared range coordination,\n//! min/max validation, focus-field tracking, and form integration. There is no\n//! popover — the two fields render inline with a visual separator between them.\n\n#[cfg(test)]\nmod tests;\n\nuse alloc::{\n    format,\n    string::{String, ToString},\n    sync::Arc,\n    vec::Vec,\n};\nuse core::{\n    cmp::Ordering,\n    fmt::{self, Debug},\n};\n\nuse ars_core::{\n    AriaAttr, AttrMap, Bindable, ComponentIds, ComponentMessages, ComponentPart, ConnectApi, Env,\n    HtmlAttr, IntlBackend, Locale, MessageFn, NoEffect, TransitionPlan,\n};\nuse ars_i18n::{CalendarDate, DateRange};\n\nuse super::date_field;\n\n/// Identifies which of the two child fields is currently active.\n///\n/// Owned by `DateRangeField` as the first range component to require it; later\n/// range components (e.g. `DateRangePicker`) reuse this enum rather than\n/// redefining it.\n#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]\npub enum ActiveField {\n    /// The start field is active.\n    Start,\n\n    /// The end field is active.\n    End,\n}\n\n/// States for the `DateRangeField` component.\n#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]\npub enum State {\n    /// Neither field holds focus.\n    Idle,\n\n    /// The start field holds focus.\n    StartFocused,\n\n    /// The end field holds focus.\n    EndFocused,\n}\n\n/// Events for the `DateRangeField` component.\n#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq)]\npub enum Event {\n    /// Focus moved to the start field.\n    FocusStart,\n\n    /// Focus moved to the end field.\n    FocusEnd,\n\n    /// Focus left the entire component.\n    BlurAll,\n\n    /// Set the full range programmatically.\n    SetRange(Option<DateRange>),\n\n    /// The start field's value changed.\n    StartValueChange(Option<CalendarDate>),\n\n    /// The end field's value changed.\n    EndValueChange(Option<CalendarDate>),\n}\n\n/// Context for the `DateRangeField` component.\n#[derive(Clone)]\npub struct Context {\n    /// Controlled or uncontrolled range value.\n    pub value: Bindable<Option<DateRange>>,\n\n    /// The active field, if either currently holds focus.\n    pub active_field: Option<ActiveField>,\n\n    /// The minimum selectable date applied to both fields.\n    pub min: Option<CalendarDate>,\n\n    /// The maximum selectable date applied to both fields.\n    pub max: Option<CalendarDate>,\n\n    /// The resolved locale.\n    pub locale: Locale,\n\n    /// Backend used for locale-dependent labels in range descriptions.\n    pub intl_backend: Arc<dyn IntlBackend>,\n\n    /// Resolved translatable messages.\n    pub messages: Messages,\n\n    /// Whether the component is disabled.\n    pub disabled: bool,\n\n    /// Whether the component is read-only.\n    pub readonly: bool,\n\n    /// Whether the component is required for form submission.\n    pub required: bool,\n\n    /// Form field name for a single hidden input carrying the range value.\n    pub name: Option<String>,\n\n    /// Form field name for a separate hidden input carrying the start date.\n    pub start_name: Option<String>,\n\n    /// Form field name for a separate hidden input carrying the end date.\n    pub end_name: Option<String>,\n\n    /// Derived component part ids.\n    pub ids: ComponentIds,\n\n    /// Whether the `Description` part is rendered.\n    pub has_description: bool,\n\n    /// Whether the `ErrorMessage` part is rendered.\n    pub has_error_message: bool,\n\n    /// When `true`, numeric segments in both child fields display with leading\n    /// zeros (e.g. \"03\" instead of \"3\").\n    pub force_leading_zeros: bool,\n}\n\nimpl Debug for Context {\n    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> fmt::Result {\n        f.debug_struct(\"Context\")\n            .field(\"value\", &self.value)\n            .field(\"active_field\", &self.active_field)\n            .field(\"min\", &self.min)\n            .field(\"max\", &self.max)\n            .field(\"locale\", &self.locale)\n            .field(\"intl_backend\", &\"<dyn IntlBackend>\")\n            .field(\"messages\", &self.messages)\n            .field(\"disabled\", &self.disabled)\n            .field(\"readonly\", &self.readonly)\n            .field(\"required\", &self.required)\n            .field(\"name\", &self.name)\n            .field(\"start_name\", &self.start_name)\n            .field(\"end_name\", &self.end_name)\n            .field(\"ids\", &self.ids)\n            .field(\"has_description\", &self.has_description)\n            .field(\"has_error_message\", &self.has_error_message)\n            .field(\"force_leading_zeros\", &self.force_leading_zeros)\n            .finish()\n    }\n}\n\nimpl Context {\n    /// Returns `true` when the current range violates the configured min/max\n    /// bounds.\n    ///\n    /// The stored range always satisfies `start <= end` (it is normalized on\n    /// every value change), so the only remaining source of invalidity is a\n    /// start earlier than `min` or an end later than `max`.\n    #[must_use]\n    pub fn is_invalid(&self) -> bool {\n        let Some(range) = self.value.get() else {\n            return false;\n        };\n\n        let below_min = self.min.as_ref().is_some_and(|min| {\n            matches!(range.start.compare_within_calendar(min), Some(Ordering::Less))\n        });\n\n        let above_max = self.max.as_ref().is_some_and(|max| {\n            matches!(range.end.compare_within_calendar(max), Some(Ordering::Greater))\n        });\n\n        below_min || above_max\n    }\n}\n\n/// Props for the `DateRangeField` component.\n#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, ars_core::HasId)]\npub struct Props {\n    /// The stable DOM id for the component.\n    pub id: String,\n\n    /// The externally controlled value of the component.\n    ///\n    /// `None` means uncontrolled. `Some(None)` means the parent controls the\n    /// component as empty. `Some(Some(range))` means a concrete range.\n    pub value: Option<Option<DateRange>>,\n\n    /// The initial value used when the component is uncontrolled.\n    pub default_value: Option<DateRange>,\n\n    /// The minimum selectable date.\n    pub min: Option<CalendarDate>,\n\n    /// The maximum selectable date.\n    pub max: Option<CalendarDate>,\n\n    /// Whether the component is disabled.\n    pub disabled: bool,\n\n    /// Whether the component is read-only.\n    pub readonly: bool,\n\n    /// Whether the component is required for form submission.\n    pub required: bool,\n\n    /// Form field name for a single hidden input carrying the range value.\n    pub name: Option<String>,\n\n    /// Form field name for the start date (alternative to `name`).\n    ///\n    /// When set, the start date submits as a separate form field.\n    pub start_name: Option<String>,\n\n    /// Form field name for the end date (alternative to `name`).\n    ///\n    /// When set, the end date submits as a separate form field.\n    pub end_name: Option<String>,\n\n    /// When `true`, numeric segments in both child fields display with leading\n    /// zeros. Defaults to `false`, which uses locale-aware formatting.\n    pub force_leading_zeros: bool,\n}\n\nimpl Default for Props {\n    fn default() -> Self {\n        Self {\n            id: String::new(),\n            value: None,\n            default_value: None,\n            min: None,\n            max: None,\n            disabled: false,\n            readonly: false,\n            required: false,\n            name: None,\n            start_name: None,\n            end_name: None,\n            force_leading_zeros: false,\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nimpl Props {\n    /// Returns a fresh [`Props`] value with the `DateRangeField` defaults.\n    #[must_use]\n    pub fn new() -> Self {\n        Self::default()\n    }\n\n    /// Sets [`id`](Self::id), the stable DOM id for the component.\n    #[must_use]\n    pub fn id(mut self, id: impl Into<String>) -> Self {\n        self.id = id.into();\n        self\n    }\n\n    /// Sets [`value`](Self::value), the externally controlled range value.\n    #[must_use]\n    pub fn value(mut self, value: Option<DateRange>) -> Self {\n        self.value = Some(value);\n        self\n    }\n\n    /// Sets [`default_value`](Self::default_value), the uncontrolled initial range.\n    #[must_use]\n    pub fn default_value(mut self, value: Option<DateRange>) -> Self {\n        self.default_value = value;\n        self\n    }\n\n    /// Sets [`min`](Self::min), the minimum selectable date.\n    #[must_use]\n    pub fn min(mut self, value: Option<CalendarDate>) -> Self {\n        self.min = value;\n        self\n    }\n\n    /// Sets [`max`](Self::max), the maximum selectable date.\n    #[must_use]\n    pub fn max(mut self, value: Option<CalendarDate>) -> Self {\n        self.max = value;\n        self\n    }\n\n    /// Sets [`disabled`](Self::disabled), whether the component is disabled.\n    #[must_use]\n    pub const fn disabled(mut self, disabled: bool) -> Self {\n        self.disabled = disabled;\n        self\n    }\n\n    /// Sets [`readonly`](Self::readonly), whether the component is read-only.\n    #[must_use]\n    pub const fn readonly(mut self, readonly: bool) -> Self {\n        self.readonly = readonly;\n        self\n    }\n\n    /// Sets [`required`](Self::required), whether the component is required.\n    #[must_use]\n    pub const fn required(mut self, required: bool) -> Self {\n        self.required = required;\n        self\n    }\n\n    /// Sets [`name`](Self::name), the combined hidden input form name.\n    #[must_use]\n    pub fn name(mut self, name: Option<String>) -> Self {\n        self.name = name;\n        self\n    }\n\n    /// Sets [`start_name`](Self::start_name), the separate start-date form name.\n    #[must_use]\n    pub fn start_name(mut self, name: Option<String>) -> Self {\n        self.start_name = name;\n        self\n    }\n\n    /// Sets [`end_name`](Self::end_name), the separate end-date form name.\n    #[must_use]\n    pub fn end_name(mut self, name: Option<String>) -> Self {\n        self.end_name = name;\n        self\n    }\n\n    /// Sets [`force_leading_zeros`](Self::force_leading_zeros).\n    #[must_use]\n    pub const fn force_leading_zeros(mut self, force: bool) -> Self {\n        self.force_leading_zeros = force;\n        self\n    }\n}\n\n/// Translatable messages for the `DateRangeField` component.\n#[derive(Clone, Debug)]\npub struct Messages {\n    /// Accessible label for the start date field.\n    pub start_label: MessageFn<dyn Fn(&Locale) -> String + Send + Sync>,\n\n    /// Accessible label for the end date field.\n    pub end_label: MessageFn<dyn Fn(&Locale) -> String + Send + Sync>,\n\n    /// Text rendered between the start and end fields.\n    pub separator_text: MessageFn<dyn Fn(&Locale) -> String + Send + Sync>,\n\n    /// Screen-reader description of the full range, given formatted start and\n    /// end date strings.\n    pub range_description: MessageFn<dyn Fn(&str, &str, &Locale) -> String + Send + Sync>,\n}\n\nimpl Default for Messages {\n    fn default() -> Self {\n        Self {\n            start_label: MessageFn::static_str(\"Start date\"),\n            end_label: MessageFn::static_str(\"End date\"),\n            separator_text: MessageFn::static_str(\" \\u{2013} \"),\n            range_description: MessageFn::new(|start: &str, end: &str, _locale: &Locale| {\n                format!(\"{start} to {end}\")\n            }),\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nimpl PartialEq for Messages {\n    fn eq(&self, _other: &Self) -> bool {\n        // `MessageFn` wraps function pointers/closures which are not\n        // meaningfully comparable; messages are treated as equal so `Context`\n        // can be cloned and compared structurally elsewhere without depending\n        // on closure identity.\n        true\n    }\n}\n\nimpl ComponentMessages for Messages {}\n\n/// State machine for the `DateRangeField` component.\n#[derive(Debug)]\npub struct Machine;\n\nimpl ars_core::Machine for Machine {\n    type State = State;\n    type Event = Event;\n    type Context = Context;\n    type Props = Props;\n    type Messages = Messages;\n    type Effect = NoEffect;\n    type Api<'a> = Api<'a>;\n\n    fn init(props: &Self::Props, env: &Env, messages: &Self::Messages) -> (Self::State, Self::Context) {\n        let value = if let Some(value) = &props.value {\n            Bindable::controlled(value.clone())\n        } else {\n            Bindable::uncontrolled(props.default_value.clone())\n        };\n\n        let ctx = Context {\n            value,\n            active_field: None,\n            min: props.min.clone(),\n            max: props.max.clone(),\n            locale: env.locale.clone(),\n            intl_backend: Arc::clone(&env.intl_backend),\n            messages: messages.clone(),\n            disabled: props.disabled,\n            readonly: props.readonly,\n            required: props.required,\n            name: props.name.clone(),\n            start_name: props.start_name.clone(),\n            end_name: props.end_name.clone(),\n            ids: ComponentIds::from_id(&props.id),\n            has_description: false,\n            has_error_message: false,\n            force_leading_zeros: props.force_leading_zeros,\n        };\n\n        (State::Idle, ctx)\n    }\n\n    fn transition(\n        state: &Self::State,\n        event: &Self::Event,\n        ctx: &Self::Context,\n        _props: &Self::Props,\n    ) -> Option<TransitionPlan<Self>> {\n        if ctx.disabled {\n            return None;\n        }\n\n        match event {\n            Event::FocusStart => Some(TransitionPlan::to(State::StartFocused).apply(|ctx| {\n                ctx.active_field = Some(ActiveField::Start);\n            })),\n\n            Event::FocusEnd => Some(TransitionPlan::to(State::EndFocused).apply(|ctx| {\n                ctx.active_field = Some(ActiveField::End);\n            })),\n\n            Event::BlurAll => Some(TransitionPlan::to(State::Idle).apply(|ctx| {\n                ctx.active_field = None;\n            })),\n\n            Event::SetRange(range) => {\n                if ctx.readonly {\n                    return None;\n                }\n\n                let range = range.clone();\n                Some(TransitionPlan::context_only(move |ctx| {\n                    ctx.value.set(range);\n                }))\n            }\n\n            Event::StartValueChange(date) => {\n                if ctx.readonly {\n                    return None;\n                }\n\n                let date = date.clone();\n                Some(TransitionPlan::context_only(move |ctx| {\n                    apply_start_change(ctx, date);\n                }))\n            }\n\n            Event::EndValueChange(date) => {\n                if ctx.readonly {\n                    return None;\n                }\n\n                let date = date.clone();\n                Some(TransitionPlan::context_only(move |ctx| {\n                    apply_end_change(ctx, date);\n                }))\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    fn connect<'a>(\n        state: &'a Self::State,\n        ctx: &'a Self::Context,\n        props: &'a Self::Props,\n        send: &'a dyn Fn(Self::Event),\n    ) -> Self::Api<'a> {\n        Api {\n            state,\n            ctx,\n            props,\n            send,\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n/// Applies a start-date change, coordinating with the existing end date.\nfn apply_start_change(ctx: &mut Context, start: Option<CalendarDate>) {\n    let existing_end = ctx.value.get().as_ref().map(|range| range.end.clone());\n\n    match (start, existing_end) {\n        (Some(start), Some(end)) => {\n            if let Some(range) = DateRange::normalized(start, end) {\n                ctx.value.set(Some(range));\n            }\n        }\n        // Only the start is set; no complete range yet. The range completes\n        // when the end is also provided.\n        (Some(_), None) => {}\n        (None, _) => ctx.value.set(None),\n    }\n}\n\n/// Applies an end-date change, coordinating with the existing start date.\nfn apply_end_change(ctx: &mut Context, end: Option<CalendarDate>) {\n    let existing_start = ctx.value.get().as_ref().map(|range| range.start.clone());\n\n    match (existing_start, end) {\n        (Some(start), Some(end)) => {\n            if let Some(range) = DateRange::normalized(start, end) {\n                ctx.value.set(Some(range));\n            }\n        }\n        // Only the end is set; no complete range yet.\n        (None, Some(_)) => {}\n        (_, None) => ctx.value.set(None),\n    }\n}\n\n/// Structural parts exposed by the `DateRangeField` connect API.\n#[derive(ComponentPart)]\n#[scope = \"date-range-field\"]\npub enum Part {\n    /// The root container grouping both fields.\n    Root,\n\n    /// The shared label for the entire range.\n    Label,\n\n    /// The wrapper around the start `DateField`.\n    StartField,\n\n    /// The visual separator between the two fields.\n    Separator,\n\n    /// The wrapper around the end `DateField`.\n    EndField,\n\n    /// The optional description element.\n    Description,\n\n    /// The optional error message element.\n    ErrorMessage,\n\n    /// The combined hidden form input.\n    HiddenInput,\n}\n\n/// API for the `DateRangeField` component.\npub struct Api<'a> {\n    /// The state of the component.\n    state: &'a State,\n\n    /// The context of the component.\n    ctx: &'a Context,\n\n    /// The props of the component.\n    props: &'a Props,\n\n    /// The send function.\n    send: &'a dyn Fn(Event),\n}\n\nimpl Debug for Api<'_> {\n    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> fmt::Result {\n        f.debug_struct(\"Api\")\n            .field(\"state\", self.state)\n            .field(\"ctx\", self.ctx)\n            .field(\"props\", self.props)\n            .finish_non_exhaustive()\n    }\n}\n\nimpl<'a> Api<'a> {\n    /// Creates a `DateRangeField` connect API from machine state.\n    #[must_use]\n    pub const fn new(\n        state: &'a State,\n        ctx: &'a Context,\n        props: &'a Props,\n        send: &'a dyn Fn(Event),\n    ) -> Self {\n        Self {\n            state,\n            ctx,\n            props,\n            send,\n        }\n    }\n\n    // ── AttrMap getters ──────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n    /// Returns attributes for the root element.\n    #[must_use]\n    pub fn root_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {\n        let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();\n        let [(scope_attr, scope_val), (part_attr, part_val)] = Part::Root.data_attrs();\n\n        attrs\n            .set(scope_attr, scope_val)\n            .set(part_attr, part_val)\n            .set(HtmlAttr::Id, self.ctx.ids.id())\n            .set(HtmlAttr::Role, \"group\")\n            .set(HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::LabelledBy), self.ctx.ids.part(\"label\"))\n            .set(HtmlAttr::Data(\"ars-state\"), self.state_name());\n\n        let mut described_by = Vec::new();\n        if self.ctx.has_description {\n            described_by.push(self.ctx.ids.part(\"description\"));\n        }\n        if self.ctx.has_error_message {\n            described_by.push(self.ctx.ids.part(\"error-message\"));\n        }\n        if !described_by.is_empty() {\n            attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::DescribedBy), described_by.join(\" \"));\n        }\n\n        if self.ctx.disabled {\n            attrs.set_bool(HtmlAttr::Data(\"ars-disabled\"), true);\n            attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::Disabled), \"true\");\n        }\n        if self.ctx.readonly {\n            attrs.set_bool(HtmlAttr::Data(\"ars-readonly\"), true);\n            attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::ReadOnly), \"true\");\n        }\n        if self.ctx.required {\n            attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::Required), \"true\");\n        }\n        if self.ctx.is_invalid() {\n            attrs.set_bool(HtmlAttr::Data(\"ars-invalid\"), true);\n        }\n\n        attrs\n    }\n\n    /// Returns attributes for the shared label element.\n    #[must_use]\n    pub fn label_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {\n        let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();\n        let [(scope_attr, scope_val), (part_attr, part_val)] = Part::Label.data_attrs();\n\n        attrs\n            .set(scope_attr, scope_val)\n            .set(part_attr, part_val)\n            .set(HtmlAttr::Id, self.ctx.ids.part(\"label\"));\n\n        attrs\n    }\n\n    /// Returns marker attributes for the start-field wrapper.\n    ///\n    /// The wrapper carries only the scope/part data hooks; the embedded child\n    /// `DateField` is configured through [`Api::start_field_props`].\n    #[must_use]\n    pub fn start_field_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {\n        let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();\n        let [(scope_attr, scope_val), (part_attr, part_val)] = Part::StartField.data_attrs();\n\n        attrs.set(scope_attr, scope_val).set(part_attr, part_val);\n\n        attrs\n    }\n\n    /// Returns marker attributes for the end-field wrapper.\n    ///\n    /// The wrapper carries only the scope/part data hooks; the embedded child\n    /// `DateField` is configured through [`Api::end_field_props`].\n    #[must_use]\n    pub fn end_field_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {\n        let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();\n        let [(scope_attr, scope_val), (part_attr, part_val)] = Part::EndField.data_attrs();\n\n        attrs.set(scope_attr, scope_val).set(part_attr, part_val);\n\n        attrs\n    }\n\n    /// Returns attributes for the separator element.\n    #[must_use]\n    pub fn separator_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {\n        let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();\n        let [(scope_attr, scope_val), (part_attr, part_val)] = Part::Separator.data_attrs();\n\n        attrs\n            .set(scope_attr, scope_val)\n            .set(part_attr, part_val)\n            .set(HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::Hidden), \"true\");\n\n        attrs\n    }\n\n    /// Returns the separator text rendered between the fields (e.g. \" – \").\n    #[must_use]\n    pub fn separator_text(&self) -> String {\n        (self.ctx.messages.separator_text)(&self.ctx.locale)\n    }\n\n    /// Returns a screen-reader description of the full range (e.g. \"March 1,\n    /// 2025 to March 15, 2025\"), or `None` if no complete range is selected.\n    #[must_use]\n    pub fn range_description(&self) -> Option<String> {\n        let range = self.ctx.value.get().as_ref()?;\n        let start = format_date_label(&range.start, self.ctx.intl_backend.as_ref(), &self.ctx.locale);\n        let end = format_date_label(&range.end, self.ctx.intl_backend.as_ref(), &self.ctx.locale);\n\n        Some((self.ctx.messages.range_description)(&start, &end, &self.ctx.locale))\n    }\n\n    /// Builds the child [`date_field::Props`] for the start date input.\n    ///\n    /// The start field's lower bound is the global `min`; its upper bound is the\n    /// current end date when a range is selected, otherwise the global `max`, so\n    /// both global bounds and cross-field coordination apply.\n    #[must_use]\n    pub fn start_field_props(&self) -> date_field::Props {\n        let range = self.ctx.value.get();\n        let max_value = range\n            .as_ref()\n            .map(|range| range.end.clone())\n            .or_else(|| self.ctx.max.clone());\n\n        date_field::Props {\n            id: self.ctx.ids.part(\"start\"),\n            value: Some(range.as_ref().map(|range| range.start.clone())),\n            min_value: self.ctx.min.clone(),\n            max_value,\n            disabled: self.ctx.disabled,\n            readonly: self.ctx.readonly,\n            required: self.ctx.required,\n            aria_label: Some((self.ctx.messages.start_label)(&self.ctx.locale)),\n            force_leading_zeros: self.ctx.force_leading_zeros,\n            ..date_field::Props::default()\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// Builds the child [`date_field::Props`] for the end date input.\n    ///\n    /// The end field's upper bound is the global `max`; its lower bound is the\n    /// current start date when a range is selected, otherwise the global `min`.\n    #[must_use]\n    pub fn end_field_props(&self) -> date_field::Props {\n        let range = self.ctx.value.get();\n        let min_value = range\n            .as_ref()\n            .map(|range| range.start.clone())\n            .or_else(|| self.ctx.min.clone());\n\n        date_field::Props {\n            id: self.ctx.ids.part(\"end\"),\n            value: Some(range.as_ref().map(|range| range.end.clone())),\n            min_value,\n            max_value: self.ctx.max.clone(),\n            disabled: self.ctx.disabled,\n            readonly: self.ctx.readonly,\n            required: self.ctx.required,\n            aria_label: Some((self.ctx.messages.end_label)(&self.ctx.locale)),\n            force_leading_zeros: self.ctx.force_leading_zeros,\n            ..date_field::Props::default()\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// Returns attributes for the description element.\n    #[must_use]\n    pub fn description_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {\n        let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();\n        let [(scope_attr, scope_val), (part_attr, part_val)] = Part::Description.data_attrs();\n\n        attrs\n            .set(scope_attr, scope_val)\n            .set(part_attr, part_val)\n            .set(HtmlAttr::Id, self.ctx.ids.part(\"description\"));\n\n        attrs\n    }\n\n    /// Returns attributes for the error message element.\n    #[must_use]\n    pub fn error_message_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {\n        let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();\n        let [(scope_attr, scope_val), (part_attr, part_val)] = Part::ErrorMessage.data_attrs();\n\n        attrs\n            .set(scope_attr, scope_val)\n            .set(part_attr, part_val)\n            .set(HtmlAttr::Id, self.ctx.ids.part(\"error-message\"))\n            .set(HtmlAttr::Role, \"alert\");\n\n        attrs\n    }\n\n    /// Returns attributes for the combined hidden input element.\n    ///\n    /// The value is the ISO 8601 interval `YYYY-MM-DD/YYYY-MM-DD`, or empty when\n    /// no range is selected.\n    #[must_use]\n    pub fn hidden_input_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {\n        let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();\n        let [(scope_attr, scope_val), (part_attr, part_val)] = Part::HiddenInput.data_attrs();\n\n        attrs\n            .set(scope_attr, scope_val)\n            .set(part_attr, part_val)\n            .set(HtmlAttr::Type, \"hidden\");\n\n        if let Some(name) = &self.ctx.name {\n            attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Name, name);\n        }\n\n        let value = match self.ctx.value.get() {\n            Some(range) => range.to_iso8601(),\n            None => String::new(),\n        };\n        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Value, value);\n\n        attrs\n    }\n\n    /// Returns attributes for the separate hidden input carrying the start date.\n    ///\n    /// Only meaningful when `start_name` is set on `Props`.\n    #[must_use]\n    pub fn start_hidden_input_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {\n        let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();\n        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Type, \"hidden\");\n\n        if let Some(name) = &self.ctx.start_name {\n            attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Name, name);\n        }\n\n        let value = match self.ctx.value.get() {\n            Some(range) => range.start.to_iso8601(),\n            None => String::new(),\n        };\n        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Value, value);\n\n        attrs\n    }\n\n    /// Returns attributes for the separate hidden input carrying the end date.\n    ///\n    /// Only meaningful when `end_name` is set on `Props`.\n    #[must_use]\n    pub fn end_hidden_input_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {\n        let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();\n        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Type, \"hidden\");\n\n        if let Some(name) = &self.ctx.end_name {\n            attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Name, name);\n        }\n\n        let value = match self.ctx.value.get() {\n            Some(range) => range.end.to_iso8601(),\n            None => String::new(),\n        };\n        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Value, value);\n\n        attrs\n    }\n\n    // ── Event dispatch (called by adapters) ──────────────────────────────\n\n    /// Notifies the machine that focus moved to the start field.\n    pub fn focus_start(&self) {\n        (self.send)(Event::FocusStart);\n    }\n\n    /// Notifies the machine that focus moved to the end field.\n    pub fn focus_end(&self) {\n        (self.send)(Event::FocusEnd);\n    }\n\n    /// Notifies the machine that focus left the entire component.\n    pub fn blur(&self) {\n        (self.send)(Event::BlurAll);\n    }\n\n    /// Sets the full range programmatically.\n    pub fn set_range(&self, range: Option<DateRange>) {\n        (self.send)(Event::SetRange(range));\n    }\n\n    /// Reports a start-date change from the start field.\n    pub fn set_start_value(&self, date: Option<CalendarDate>) {\n        (self.send)(Event::StartValueChange(date));\n    }\n\n    /// Reports an end-date change from the end field.\n    pub fn set_end_value(&self, date: Option<CalendarDate>) {\n        (self.send)(Event::EndValueChange(date));\n    }\n\n    // ── Convenience getters ──────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n    /// Returns the currently selected range, if any.\n    #[must_use]\n    pub fn selected_range(&self) -> Option<&DateRange> {\n        self.ctx.value.get().as_ref()\n    }\n\n    /// Returns the active field, if either currently holds focus.\n    #[must_use]\n    pub const fn active_field(&self) -> Option<ActiveField> {\n        self.ctx.active_field\n    }\n\n    /// Returns `true` when either field currently holds focus.\n    #[must_use]\n    pub fn is_focused(&self) -> bool {\n        !matches!(self.state, State::Idle)\n    }\n\n    /// Returns `true` when the current range violates the min/max bounds.\n    #[must_use]\n    pub fn is_invalid(&self) -> bool {\n        self.ctx.is_invalid()\n    }\n\n    /// Returns the stable state token used for `data-ars-state`.\n    #[must_use]\n    pub const fn state_name(&self) -> &'static str {\n        match self.state {\n            State::Idle => \"idle\",\n            State::StartFocused => \"start-focused\",\n            State::EndFocused => \"end-focused\",\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nimpl ConnectApi for Api<'_> {\n    type Part = Part;\n\n    fn part_attrs(&self, part: Part) -> AttrMap {\n        match part {\n            Part::Root => self.root_attrs(),\n            Part::Label => self.label_attrs(),\n            Part::StartField => self.start_field_attrs(),\n            Part::Separator => self.separator_attrs(),\n            Part::EndField => self.end_field_attrs(),\n            Part::Description => self.description_attrs(),\n            Part::ErrorMessage => self.error_message_attrs(),\n            Part::HiddenInput => self.hidden_input_attrs(),\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n/// Formats a single date as a human-readable label for screen-reader range\n/// descriptions (e.g. \"March 1, 2025\").\nfn format_date_label(date: &CalendarDate, backend: &dyn IntlBackend, locale: &Locale) -> String {\n    format!(\n        \"{} {}, {}\",\n        backend.month_long_name(date.month(), locale),\n        date.day(),\n        date.year(),\n    )\n}\n"
}

        self.unmounted

    /// Creates a connect API snapshot for producing DOM attributes.
    ///
    /// The `send` closure is used by the API to wire event handlers. The returned
    /// API borrows from this service and must not outlive it.
    pub fn connect<'a>(&'a self, send: &'a dyn Fn(M::Event)) -> M::Api<'a> {
        M::connect(&self.state, &self.context, &self.props, send)
    }

    /// Test-only: force the service into a specific state.
    ///
    /// Re-derives context from the new state and current props via
    /// `Machine::init`, discarding the init state. Uses default env and
    /// messages. Used by keyboard matrix tests to start from arbitrary states.
    #[cfg(test)]
    pub fn set_state_for_test(&mut self, state: M::State) {
        let (_init_state, context) = M::init(&self.props, &Env::default(), &M::Messages::default());

        self.state = state;
```

  "content": "//! Unit and snapshot tests for the `DateRangeField` component.\n//!\n//! Test names that begin with `snapshot_` use `insta::assert_snapshot!` and\n//! commit golden output under `snapshots/`. Every other test is a pure\n//! state-machine or connect-API assertion that does not depend on `.snap`\n//! files.\n\nuse alloc::{format, string::String, sync::Arc, vec::Vec};\nuse core::cell::RefCell;\n\nuse ars_core::{AriaAttr, AttrMap, ComponentPart, ConnectApi, Env, HtmlAttr, Service};\nuse ars_i18n::{CalendarDate, DateRange, Locale, StubIntlBackend, locales::en_us};\nuse insta::assert_snapshot;\n\nuse super::super::date_field;\nuse super::*;\n\n// ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n// Helpers\n// ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\nfn date(year: i32, month: u8, day: u8) -> CalendarDate {\n    CalendarDate::new_gregorian(year, month, day).expect(\"valid test date\")\n}\n\nfn range(start: CalendarDate, end: CalendarDate) -> DateRange {\n    DateRange::new(start, end).expect(\"ordered test range\")\n}\n\nfn props() -> Props {\n    Props {\n        id: String::from(\"trip\"),\n        ..Props::default()\n    }\n}\n\nfn env(locale: Locale) -> Env {\n    Env::new(locale, Arc::new(StubIntlBackend))\n}\n\nfn service() -> Service<Machine> {\n    Service::<Machine>::new(props(), &env(en_us()), &Messages::default())\n}\n\nfn service_with(props: Props, locale: Locale) -> Service<Machine> {\n    Service::<Machine>::new(props, &env(locale), &Messages::default())\n}\n\nfn snapshot_attrs(attrs: &AttrMap) -> String {\n    format!(\"{attrs:#?}\")\n}\n\nfn attr(attrs: &AttrMap, key: HtmlAttr) -> Option<String> {\n    attrs.get(&key).map(ToString::to_string)\n}\n\n/// Drives a child `DateField` from the given props and returns its field-group\n/// attributes, used to assert the start/end sub-group accessibility.\nfn child_field_group_attrs(child: date_field::Props) -> AttrMap {\n    let service =\n        Service::<date_field::Machine>::new(child, &env(en_us()), &date_field::Messages::default());\n\n    service.connect(&|_| {}).field_group_attrs()\n}\n\n// ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n// Initial state\n// ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n#[test]\nfn initial_state_is_idle() {\n    let svc = service();\n\n    assert_eq!(*svc.state(), State::Idle);\n    assert!(svc.context().active_field.is_none());\n}\n\n#[test]\nfn initial_value_defaults_to_none() {\n    let svc = service();\n\n    assert_eq!(*svc.context().value.get(), None);\n}\n\n#[test]\nfn default_value_seeds_range() {\n    let initial = range(date(2025, 6, 1), date(2025, 6, 15));\n    let svc = service_with(\n        Props {\n            default_value: Some(initial.clone()),\n            ..props()\n        },\n        en_us(),\n    );\n\n    assert_eq!(*svc.context().value.get(), Some(initial));\n}\n\n#[test]\nfn controlled_value_overrides_default() {\n    let controlled = range(date(2025, 1, 1), date(2025, 1, 31));\n    let svc = service_with(\n        Props {\n            value: Some(Some(controlled.clone())),\n            default_value: Some(range(date(2030, 1, 1), date(2030, 1, 2))),\n            ..props()\n        },\n        en_us(),\n    );\n\n    assert_eq!(*svc.context().value.get(), Some(controlled));\n}\n\n// ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n// Focus tracking\n// ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n#[test]\nfn focus_start_enters_start_focused() {\n    let mut svc = service();\n\n    drop(svc.send(Event::FocusStart));\n\n    assert_eq!(*svc.state(), State::StartFocused);\n    assert_eq!(svc.context().active_field, Some(ActiveField::Start));\n}\n\n#[test]\nfn focus_end_enters_end_focused() {\n    let mut svc = service();\n\n    drop(svc.send(Event::FocusEnd));\n\n    assert_eq!(*svc.state(), State::EndFocused);\n    assert_eq!(svc.context().active_field, Some(ActiveField::End));\n}\n\n#[test]\nfn tab_from_start_to_end_then_blur() {\n    let mut svc = service();\n\n    // Tab navigates from the last start segment into the first end segment;\n    // the agnostic core models this as the focus moving between fields.\n    drop(svc.send(Event::FocusStart));\n    assert_eq!(*svc.state(), State::StartFocused);\n\n    drop(svc.send(Event::FocusEnd));\n    assert_eq!(*svc.state(), State::EndFocused);\n\n    drop(svc.send(Event::BlurAll));\n    assert_eq!(*svc.state(), State::Idle);\n    assert!(svc.context().active_field.is_none());\n}\n\n// ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n// Disabled / readonly guards\n// ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n#[test]\nfn disabled_ignores_all_events() {\n    let mut svc = service_with(\n        Props {\n            disabled: true,\n            ..props()\n        },\n        en_us(),\n    );\n\n    drop(svc.send(Event::FocusStart));\n    drop(svc.send(Event::SetRange(Some(range(date(2025, 6, 1), date(2025, 6, 15))))));\n\n    assert_eq!(*svc.state(), State::Idle);\n    assert_eq!(*svc.context().value.get(), None);\n}\n\n#[test]\nfn readonly_ignores_value_changes_but_allows_focus() {\n    let mut svc = service_with(\n        Props {\n            readonly: true,\n            ..props()\n        },\n        en_us(),\n    );\n\n    drop(svc.send(Event::SetRange(Some(range(date(2025, 6, 1), date(2025, 6, 15))))));\n    assert_eq!(*svc.context().value.get(), None);\n\n    drop(svc.send(Event::FocusStart));\n    assert_eq!(*svc.state(), State::StartFocused);\n}\n\n// ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n// Range coordination and normalization\n// ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n#[test]\nfn set_range_updates_value() {\n    let mut svc = service();\n    let selected = range(date(2025, 6, 1), date(2025, 6, 30));\n\n    let result = svc.send(Event::SetRange(Some(selected.clone())));\n    // Value changes are context-only — no state transition.\n    assert!(result.transitioned.is_none());\n\n    assert_eq!(*svc.context().value.get(), Some(selected));\n}\n\n#[test]\nfn start_then_end_completes_range() {\n    let mut svc = service();\n\n    // Setting only the start does not yet produce a complete range.\n    drop(svc.send(Event::StartValueChange(Some(date(2025, 6, 1)))));\n    assert_eq!(*svc.context().value.get(), None);\n\n    drop(svc.send(Event::EndValueChange(Some(date(2025, 6, 15)))));\n    assert_eq!(\n        *svc.context().value.get(),\n        Some(range(date(2025, 6, 1), date(2025, 6, 15)))\n    );\n}\n\n#[test]\nfn start_change_after_end_normalizes_when_out_of_order() {\n    let mut svc = service_with(\n        Props {\n            default_value: Some(range(date(2025, 6, 10), date(2025, 6, 20))),\n            ..props()\n        },\n        en_us(),\n    );\n\n    // Move the start to *after* the existing end; the range must normalize so\n    // start never exceeds end.\n    drop(svc.send(Event::StartValueChange(Some(date(2025, 6, 25)))));\n\n    let stored = svc.context().value.get().clone().expect(\"range present\");\n    assert_eq!(stored.start, date(2025, 6, 20));\n    assert_eq!(stored.end, date(2025, 6, 25));\n    assert!(matches!(\n        stored.start.compare_within_calendar(&stored.end),\n        Some(core::cmp::Ordering::Less | core::cmp::Ordering::Equal)\n    ));\n}\n\n#[test]\nfn end_change_before_start_normalizes() {\n    let mut svc = service_with(\n        Props {\n            default_value: Some(range(date(2025, 6, 10), date(2025, 6, 20))),\n            ..props()\n        },\n        en_us(),\n    );\n\n    drop(svc.send(Event::EndValueChange(Some(date(2025, 6, 1)))));\n\n    let stored = svc.context().value.get().clone().expect(\"range present\");\n    assert_eq!(stored.start, date(2025, 6, 1));\n    assert_eq!(stored.end, date(2025, 6, 10));\n}\n\n#[test]\nfn clearing_start_clears_range() {\n    let mut svc = service_with(\n        Props {\n            default_value: Some(range(date(2025, 6, 1), date(2025, 6, 15))),\n            ..props()\n        },\n        en_us(),\n    );\n\n    drop(svc.send(Event::StartValueChange(None)));\n    assert_eq!(*svc.context().value.get(), None);\n}\n\n// ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n// Validation\n// ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n#[test]\nfn range_within_bounds_is_valid() {\n    let svc = service_with(\n        Props {\n            min: Some(date(2025, 1, 1)),\n            max: Some(date(2025, 12, 31)),\n            default_value: Some(range(date(2025, 6, 1), date(2025, 6, 15))),\n            ..props()\n        },\n        en_us(),\n    );\n\n    assert!(!svc.context().is_invalid());\n    let api = svc.connect(&|_| {});\n    assert!(!api.is_invalid());\n    assert!(attr(&api.root_attrs(), HtmlAttr::Data(\"ars-invalid\")).is_none());\n}\n\n#[test]\nfn range_below_min_is_invalid() {\n    let svc = service_with(\n        Props {\n            min: Some(date(2025, 6, 1)),\n            default_value: Some(range(date(2025, 5, 1), date(2025, 6, 15))),\n            ..props()\n        },\n        en_us(),\n    );\n\n    assert!(svc.context().is_invalid());\n    let api = svc.connect(&|_| {});\n    assert!(api.is_invalid());\n    assert_eq!(\n        attr(&api.root_attrs(), HtmlAttr::Data(\"ars-invalid\")).as_deref(),\n        Some(\"true\")\n    );\n}\n\n#[test]\nfn range_above_max_is_invalid() {\n    let svc = service_with(\n        Props {\n            max: Some(date(2025, 6, 30)),\n            default_value: Some(range(date(2025, 6, 1), date(2025, 7, 15))),\n            ..props()\n        },\n        en_us(),\n    );\n\n    assert!(svc.context().is_invalid());\n}\n\n#[test]\nfn empty_range_is_never_invalid() {\n    let svc = service_with(\n        Props {\n            min: Some(date(2025, 6, 1)),\n            max: Some(date(2025, 6, 30)),\n            ..props()\n        },\n        en_us(),\n    );\n\n    assert!(!svc.context().is_invalid());\n}\n\n// ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n// Connect API — root / group\n// ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n#[test]\nfn root_is_a_group_labelled_by_label() {\n    let svc = service();\n    let api = svc.connect(&|_| {});\n    let attrs = api.root_attrs();\n\n    assert_eq!(attr(&attrs, HtmlAttr::Role).as_deref(), Some(\"group\"));\n    assert_eq!(\n        attr(&attrs, HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::LabelledBy)).as_deref(),\n        Some(\"trip-label\")\n    );\n    assert_eq!(\n        attr(&attrs, HtmlAttr::Data(\"ars-state\")).as_deref(),\n        Some(\"idle\")\n    );\n}\n\n#[test]\nfn required_sets_aria_required() {\n    let svc = service_with(\n        Props {\n            required: true,\n            ..props()\n        },\n        en_us(),\n    );\n    let api = svc.connect(&|_| {});\n\n    assert_eq!(\n        attr(&api.root_attrs(), HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::Required)).as_deref(),\n        Some(\"true\")\n    );\n}\n\n#[test]\nfn describedby_references_present_description_and_error() {\n    let svc = service_with(\n        Props {\n            has_description: true,\n            has_error_message: true,\n            ..props()\n        },\n        en_us(),\n    );\n    let api = svc.connect(&|_| {});\n\n    assert_eq!(\n        attr(&api.root_attrs(), HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::DescribedBy)).as_deref(),\n        Some(\"trip-description trip-error-message\")\n    );\n}\n\n#[test]\nfn two_subgroups_are_groups_with_distinct_labels() {\n    let svc = service();\n    let api = svc.connect(&|_| {});\n\n    let start_group = child_field_group_attrs(api.start_field_props());\n    let end_group = child_field_group_attrs(api.end_field_props());\n\n    assert_eq!(attr(&start_group, HtmlAttr::Role).as_deref(), Some(\"group\"));\n    assert_eq!(\n        attr(&start_group, HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::Label)).as_deref(),\n        Some(\"Start date\")\n    );\n    assert_eq!(attr(&end_group, HtmlAttr::Role).as_deref(), Some(\"group\"));\n    assert_eq!(\n        attr(&end_group, HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::Label)).as_deref(),\n        Some(\"End date\")\n    );\n}\n\n// ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n// Connect API — separator\n// ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n#[test]\nfn separator_is_aria_hidden_with_text() {\n    let svc = service();\n    let api = svc.connect(&|_| {});\n\n    assert_eq!(\n        attr(&api.separator_attrs(), HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::Hidden)).as_deref(),\n        Some(\"true\")\n    );\n    assert_eq!(api.separator_text(), \" \\u{2013} \");\n}\n\n// ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n// Connect API — child field props (min/max coordination)\n// ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n#[test]\nfn child_field_props_carry_distinct_ids_and_force_leading_zeros() {\n    let svc = service_with(\n        Props {\n            force_leading_zeros: true,\n            ..props()\n        },\n        en_us(),\n    );\n    let api = svc.connect(&|_| {});\n\n    let start = api.start_field_props();\n    let end = api.end_field_props();\n\n    assert_eq!(start.id, \"trip-start\");\n    assert_eq!(end.id, \"trip-end\");\n    assert!(start.force_leading_zeros);\n    assert!(end.force_leading_zeros);\n}\n\n#[test]\nfn global_min_max_apply_to_both_fields_when_no_range() {\n    let svc = service_with(\n        Props {\n            min: Some(date(2025, 1, 1)),\n            max: Some(date(2025, 12, 31)),\n            ..props()\n        },\n        en_us(),\n    );\n    let api = svc.connect(&|_| {});\n\n    let start = api.start_field_props();\n    let end = api.end_field_props();\n\n    assert_eq!(start.min_value, Some(date(2025, 1, 1)));\n    assert_eq!(start.max_value, Some(date(2025, 12, 31)));\n    assert_eq!(end.min_value, Some(date(2025, 1, 1)));\n    assert_eq!(end.max_value, Some(date(2025, 12, 31)));\n}\n\n#[test]\nfn selected_range_constrains_cross_field_bounds() {\n    let svc = service_with(\n        Props {\n            min: Some(date(2025, 1, 1)),\n            max: Some(date(2025, 12, 31)),\n            default_value: Some(range(date(2025, 6, 1), date(2025, 6, 30))),\n            ..props()\n        },\n        en_us(),\n    );\n    let api = svc.connect(&|_| {});\n\n    let start = api.start_field_props();\n    let end = api.end_field_props();\n\n    // Start cannot exceed the current end; end cannot precede the current start.\n    assert_eq!(start.min_value, Some(date(2025, 1, 1)));\n    assert_eq!(start.max_value, Some(date(2025, 6, 30)));\n    assert_eq!(end.min_value, Some(date(2025, 6, 1)));\n    assert_eq!(end.max_value, Some(date(2025, 12, 31)));\n    assert_eq!(start.value, Some(Some(date(2025, 6, 1))));\n    assert_eq!(end.value, Some(Some(date(2025, 6, 30))));\n}\n\n#[test]\nfn child_fields_inherit_disabled_readonly_required() {\n    let svc = service_with(\n        Props {\n            disabled: true,\n            readonly: true,\n            required: true,\n {\n            default_value: Some(range(date(2025, 6, 1), date(2025, 6, 15))),\n            ..props()\n        },\n        en_us(),\n    );\n\n    drop(svc.send(Event::FocusEnd));\n    let api = svc.connect(&|_| {});\n\n    assert!(api.is_focused());\n    assert_eq!(api.active_field(), Some(ActiveField::End));\n    assert_eq!(\n        api.selected_range(),\n        Some(&range(date(2025, 6, 1), date(2025, 6, 15)))\n    );\n    assert_eq!(api.state_name(), \"end-focused\");\n}\n\n// ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n// Connect API — event dispatch\n// ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n#[test]\nfn dispatch_methods_send_expected_events() {\n    let svc = service();\n    let recorded: RefCell<Vec<Event>> = RefCell::new(Vec::new());\n    let send = |event: Event| recorded.borrow_mut().push(event);\n    let api = svc.connect(&send);\n\n    api.focus_start();\n    api.focus_end();\n    api.blur();\n    api.set_range(None);\n    api.set_start_value(Some(date(2025, 6, 1)));\n    api.set_end_value(None);\n\n    assert_eq!(\n        *recorded.borrow(),\n        Vec::from([\n            Event::FocusStart,\n            Event::FocusEnd,\n            Event::BlurAll,\n            Event::SetRange(None),\n            Event::StartValueChange(Some(date(2025, 6, 1))),\n            Event::EndValueChange(None),\n        ])\n    );\n}\n\n// ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n// Anatomy / Part coverage\n// ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n#[test]\nfn part_attrs_cover_every_part() {\n    let svc = service();\n    let api = svc.connect(&|_| {});\n\n    for part in Part::all() {\n        let name = part.name();\n        let attrs = api.part_attrs(part);\n        assert_eq!(\n            attr(&attrs, HtmlAttr::Data(\"ars-part\")).as_deref(),\n            Some(name),\n            \"part {name} must carry its data-ars-part marker\"\n        );\n        assert_eq!(\n            attr(&attrs, HtmlAttr::Data(\"ars-scope\")).as_deref(),\n            Some(\"date-range-field\")\n        );\n    }\n}\n\n#[test]\nfn props_builder_sets_every_field() {\n    let built = Props::new()\n        .id(\"trip\")\n        .value(Some(range(date(2025, 6, 1), date(2025, 6, 15))))\n        .default_value(Some(range(date(2025, 1, 1), date(2025, 1, 2))))\n        .min(Some(date(2024, 1, 1)))\n        .max(Some(date(2026, 1, 1)))\n        .disabled(true)\n        .readonly(true)\n        .required(true)\n        .name(Some(String::from(\"range\")))\n        .start_name(Some(String::from(\"start\")))\n        .end_name(Some(String::from(\"end\")))\n        .force_leading_zeros(true)\n        .has_description(true)\n        .has_error_message(true);\n\n    assert_eq!(built.id, \"trip\");\n    assert_eq!(built.value, Some(Some(range(date(2025, 6, 1), date(2025, 6, 15)))));\n    assert_eq!(built.default_value, Some(range(date(2025, 1, 1), date(2025, 1, 2))));\n    assert_eq!(built.min, Some(date(2024, 1, 1)));\n    assert_eq!(built.max, Some(date(2026, 1, 1)));\n    assert!(built.disabled);\n    assert!(built.readonly);\n    assert!(built.required);\n    assert_eq!(built.name.as_deref(), Some(\"range\"));\n    assert_eq!(built.start_name.as_deref(), Some(\"start\"));\n    assert_eq!(built.end_name.as_deref(), Some(\"end\"));\n    assert!(built.force_leading_zeros);\n    assert!(built.has_description);\n    assert!(built.has_error_message);\n}\n\n// ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n// Snapshots\n// ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n#[test]\nfn snapshot_root_idle() {\n    let svc = service();\n    let api = svc.connect(&|_| {});\n\n svc.connect(&|_| {});\n\n    assert_snapshot!(snapshot_attrs(&api.root_attrs()));\n}\n\n#[test]\nfn snapshot_root_end_focused() {\n    let mut svc = service();\n    drop(svc.send(Event::FocusEnd));\n    let api = svc.connect(&|_| {});\n\n    assert_snapshot!(snapshot_attrs(&api.root_attrs()));\n}\n\n#[test]\nfn snapshot_root_disabled() {\n    let svc = service_with(\n        Props {\n > AGENT

Found a real design gap (shared with the spec): the parent can't complete a range from two separate `StartValueChange`/`EndValueChange` events because `Option<DateRange>` can't hold a partial (only-start) value. I'll track each field's value independently in Context and derive the complete range. Let me implement that.

> TOOL

tool_use Edit
id: toolu_01H9V8oNJc5LX9W23aS8rhHW
```json

  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/ericson/Workspace/Rust/ars-ui.worktrees/impl-284/crates/ars-components/src/date_time/date_range_field/mod.rs",
  "old_string": "    /// Controlled or uncontrolled range value.\n    pub value: Bindable<Option<DateRange>>,\n\n    /// The active field, if either currently holds focus.\n    pub active_field: Option<ActiveField>,",
  "new_string": "    /// Controlled or uncontrolled range value.\n    ///\n    /// This is the *derived complete* range: it is `Some` only when both\n    /// [`start_date`](Self::start_date) and [`end_date`](Self::end_date) are\n    /// set, and is always normalized so `start <= end`.\n    pub value: Bindable<Option<DateRange>>,\n\n    /// The start field's current value, tracked independently so a range can be\n    /// assembled incrementally as each field changes.\n    pub start_date: Option<CalendarDate>,\n\n    /// The end field's current value, tracked independently so a range can be\n    /// assembled incrementally as each field changes.\n    pub end_date: Option<CalendarDate>,\n\n    /// The active field, if either currently holds focus.\n    pub active_field: Option<ActiveField>,"
}

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/ericson/Workspace/Rust/ars-ui.worktrees/impl-284/crates/ars-components/src/date_time/date_range_field/mod.rs",
  "old_string": "/// Applies a start-date change, coordinating with the existing end date.\nfn apply_start_change(ctx: &mut Context, start: Option<CalendarDate>) {\n    let existing_end = ctx.value.get().as_ref().map(|range| range.end.clone());\n\n    match (start, existing_end) {\n        (Some(start), Some(end)) => {\n            if let Some(range) = DateRange::normalized(start, end) {\n                ctx.value.set(Some(range));\n            }\n        }\n        // Only the start is set; no complete range yet. The range completes\n        // when the end is also provided.\n        (Some(_), None) => {}\n        (None, _) => ctx.value.set(None),\n    }\n}\n\n/// Applies an end-date change, coordinating with the existing start date.\nfn apply_end_change(ctx: &mut Context, end: Option<CalendarDate>) {\n    let existing_start = ctx.value.get().as_ref().map(|range| range.start.clone());\n\n    match (existing_start, end) {\n        (Some(start), Some(end)) => {\n            if let Some(range) = DateRange::normalized(start, end) {\n                ctx.value.set(Some(range));\n            }\n        }\n        // Only the end is set; no complete range yet.\n        (None, Some(_)) => {}\n        (_, None) => ctx.value.set(None),\n    }\n}",
  "new_string": "/// Applies a start-date change, then recomputes the derived range.\nfn apply_start_change(ctx: &mut Context, start: Option<CalendarDate>) {\n    ctx.start_date = start;\n    recompute_range(ctx);\n}\n\n/// Applies an end-date change, then recomputes the derived range.\nfn apply_end_change(ctx: &mut Context, end: Option<CalendarDate>) {\n    ctx.end_date = end;\n    recompute_range(ctx);\n}\n\n/// Recomputes the derived complete range from the two field values.\n///\n/// When both fields hold a value the range is normalized (swapping if needed so\n/// `start <= end`) and the normalized order is reflected back into the field\n/// values. When either field is empty the range is incomplete and the value is\n/// cleared.\nfn recompute_range(ctx: &mut Context) {\n    match (ctx.start_date.clone(), ctx.end_date.clone()) {\n        (Some(start), Some(end)) => {\n            if let Some(range) = DateRange::normalized(start, end) {\n                ctx.start_date = Some(range.start.clone());\n                ctx.end_date = Some(range.end.clone());\n                ctx.value.set(Some(range));\n            }\n        }\n        _ => ctx.value.set(None),\n    }\n}"
}

#[test]
fn range_calendar_anatomy_matches_spec() {
    let example = calendar_date_default_for_part_all();
    assert_anatomy(
        "range-calendar",
        &[
            (range_calendar::Part::Root, "root"),
            (range_calendar::Part::Header, "header"),
            (range_calendar::Part::PrevTrigger, "prev-trigger"),
            (range_calendar::Part::NextTrigger, "next-trigger"),
            (range_calendar::Part::Heading, "heading"),
            (range_calendar::Part::Grid, "grid"),
            (range_calendar::Part::GridGroup, "grid-group"),
            (range_calendar::Part::HeadRow, "head-row"),
            (
                range_calendar::Part::HeadCell {
                    day: Weekday::Monday,
                },
                "head-cell",
            ),
            (range_calendar::Part::Row { week_index: 0 }, "row"),
            (
                range_calendar::Part::Cell {
                    date: example.clone(),
                    offset: 0,
                },
                "cell",
            ),
            (
                range_calendar::Part::CellTrigger {
                    date: example,
                    offset: 0,
                },
                "cell-trigger",
            ),
        ],
    );
}

#[test]
fn date_picker_anatomy_matches_spec() {
    assert_anatomy(

            (date_picker::Part::Description, "description"),

            (date_picker::Part::HiddenInput, "hidden-input"),

}

use ars_components::date_time::{
    calendar::{self, PageBehavior, SelectionMode},
    date_field::{self, DateSegmentKind},
    date_picker, date_time_picker, range_calendar, time_field,
};
use ars_core::{ComponentPart, ConnectApi, Env, KeyboardKey, Service};
use ars_i18n::{CalendarDate, CalendarDateTime, DateRange, HourCycle, Time, Weekday};
use proptest::prelude::*;

#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
enum DateFieldAction {
    Send(date_field::Event),
    SetControlledValue(Option<CalendarDate>),
    SetDisabled(bool),
    SetReadonly(bool),
    SetInvalid(bool),
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
enum TimeFieldAction {
    Send(time_field::Event),
    SetControlledValue(Option<Time>),
    SetDisabled(bool),
    SetReadonly(bool),
    SetInvalid(bool),
}

fn date(year: i32, month: u8, day: u8) -> CalendarDate {
    CalendarDate::new_gregorian(year, month, day).expect("generated date should be valid")
}

fn date_field_props() -> date_field::Props {
    date_field::Props::new().id("date-field").label("Date")
}

fn time_field_props() -> time_field::Props {
    time_field::Props::new()
        .id("time-field")
        .label("Time")
        .hour_cycle(Some(HourCycle::H12))
        .granularity(time_field::TimeGranularity::Second)
}

fn arb_date() -> impl Strategy<Value = CalendarDate> {
    (1900i32..=2100, 1u8..=12, 1u8..=28).prop_map(|(year, month, day)| date(year, month, day))
}

fn arb_time() -> impl Strategy<Value = Time> {
    (0u8..=23, 0u8..=59, 0u8..=59)
        .prop_map(|(hour, minute, second)| Time::new(hour, minute, second, 0).unwrap())
}

fn arb_editable_kind() -> impl Strategy<Value = DateSegmentKind> {
    prop_oneof![
        Just(DateSegmentKind::Year),
        Just(DateSegmentKind::Month),
        Just(DateSegmentKind::Day),
    ]
}

fn arb_digit() -> impl Strategy<Value = char> {
    (0u8..=9).prop_map(|digit| char::from(b'0' + digit))
}

fn arb_time_editable_kind() -> impl Strategy<Value = DateSegmentKind> {
    prop_oneof![
        Just(DateSegmentKind::Hour),
        Just(DateSegmentKind::Minute),
        Just(DateSegmentKind::Second),
        Just(DateSegmentKind::DayPeriod),

//! Shared configuration for the property-based state-machine tests.
//!
//! Centralises two concerns that every `proptest!` block in this harness
//! cares about:
//!
//! 1. **Case count.** Reads the `PROPTEST_CASES` environment variable so
//!    the nightly `extended-proptest` job (10,000 cases) and local runs
//!    (1,000 cases by default) share one knob.
//! 2. **Failure persistence.** Redirects the `*.proptest-regressions`
//!    seed files to a single per-crate `proptest-regressions/`
//!    directory at the crate root instead of dropping them next to test
//!    sources. Default proptest behaviour clutters the source tree with
//!    a sibling file per test module; this single-file layout is the
//!    same content but lives next to `Cargo.toml` where bookkeeping
//!    files belong. The file is committed (per proptest convention) so
//!    every developer's runs benefit from previously-shrunk failures.
//!
//! Use as `#![proptest_config(super::common::proptest_config())]` inside
//! every `proptest!` block in this harness.

use proptest::test_runner::{Config, FileFailurePersistence};

/// Builds a `ProptestConfig` with the workspace-standard case count and
/// failure-persistence layout.
///
/// The persistence target is a single file at
/// `<crate-root>/proptest-regressions/state-machines.txt` — proptest
/// internally namespaces seeds by test name so multiple modules can
/// safely share one file.
#[must_use]
pub(super) fn proptest_config() -> Config {
    let cases = std::env::var("PROPTEST_CASES")
        .ok()
        .and_then(|v| v.parse().ok())
        .unwrap_or(1000);

    Config {
        cases,
        failure_persistence: Some(Box::new(FileFailurePersistence::Direct(
            "proptest-regressions/state-machines.txt",
=== tail ===

                prop_assert!(
                    !ctx.all_segments()

                );
            }

            if let Some(kind) = ctx.focused_segment {
                prop_assert!(
                    ctx.segment(kind).is_some_and(|segment| segment.is_editable)
                );
            }

            let send = |_event: date_time_picker::Event| {};

            let api = service.connect(&send);

            for part in date_time_picker::Part::all() {
                drop(api.part_attrs(part));
            }
        }
    }
}

───────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\nfn arb_date_range_field_event() -> impl Strategy<Value = date_range_field::Event> {\n    prop_oneof![\n        Just(date_range_field::Event::FocusStart),\n        Just(date_range_field::Event::FocusEnd),\n        Just(date_range_field::Event::BlurAll),\n        proptest::option::of(arb_date()).prop_map(date_range_field::Event::StartValueChange),\n        proptest::option::of(arb_date()).prop_map(date_range_field::Event::EndValueChange),\n        proptest::option::of((arb_date(), arb_date()).prop_map(|(first, second)| {\n            DateRange::normalized(first, second).expect(\"comparable generated dates\")\n        }))\n        .prop_map(date_range_field::Event::SetRange),\n    ]\n}\n\nfn assert_date_range_field_invariants(service: &Service<date_range_field::Machine>) {\n    let ctx = service.context();\n\n    // The derived complete range is `Some` exactly when both fields are set,\n    // and is always normalized so `start <= end`.\n    match ctx.value.get() {\n        Some(range) => {\n            prop_assert!(ctx.start_date.is_some() && ctx.end_date.is_some());\n            prop_assert!(matches!(\n                range.start.compare_within_calendar(&range.end),\n                Some(core::cmp::Ordering::Less | core::cmp::Ordering::Equal)\n            ));\n            prop_assert_eq!(ctx.start_date.as_ref(), Some(&range.start));\n            prop_assert_eq!(ctx.end_date.as_ref(), Some(&range.end));\n        }\n        None => {\n            prop_assert!(ctx.start_date.is_none() || ctx.end_date.is_none());\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Every part produces attributes without panicking.\n    let send = |_event: date_range_field::Event| {};\n    let api = service.connect(&send);\n    for part in date_range_field::Part::all() {\n        drop(api.part_attrs(part));\n    }\n\n    // Child field props mirror the tracked per-field values.\n    prop_assert_eq!(api.start_field_props().value, Some(ctx.start_date.clone()));\n    prop_assert_eq!(api.end_field_props().value, Some(ctx.end_date.clone()));\n\n    Ok(())\n}\n\nproptest! {\n    #![proptest_config(super::common::proptest_config())]\n\n    #[test]\n    fn date_range_field_maintains_normalized_range(\n        events in prop::collection::vec(arb_date_range_field_event(), 0..24),\n    ) {\n        let mut service = Service::<date_range_field::Machine>::new(\n            date_range_field::Props::new().id(\"range\"),\n            &Env::default(),\n            &date_range_field::Messages::default(),\n        );\n\n        for event in events {\n            drop(service.send(event));\n            assert_date_range_field_invariants(&service)?;\n        }\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn date_range_field_disabled_ignores_events(\n        events in prop::collection::vec(arb_date_range_field_event(), 0..24),\n    ) {\n        let mut service = Service::<date_range_field::Machine>::new(\n            date_range_field::Props::new().id(\"range\").disabled(true),\n            &Env::default(),\n            &date_range_field::Messages::default(),\n        );\n\n        for event in events {\n            drop(service.send(event));\n        }\n\n        prop_assert_eq!(*service.state(), date_range_field::State::Idle);\n        prop_assert_eq!(service.context().value.get().as_ref(), None);\n    }\n}"
}

  "new_string": "fn assert_date_range_field_invariants(service: &Service<date_range_field::Machine>) {\n    let ctx = service.context();\n\n    // The derived complete range is `Some` exactly when both fields are set,\n    // and is always normalized so `start <= end`.\n    match ctx.value.get() {\n        Some(range) => {\n            assert!(\n                ctx.start_date.is_some() && ctx.end_date.is_some(),\n                \"complete range requires both fields set\"\n            );\n            assert!(\n                matches!(\n                    range.start.compare_within_calendar(&range.end),\n                    Some(core::cmp::Ordering::Less | core::cmp::Ordering::Equal)\n                ),\n                \"stored range must be normalized so start <= end\"\n            );\n            assert_eq!(ctx.start_date.as_ref(), Some(&range.start));\n            assert_eq!(ctx.end_date.as_ref(), Some(&range.end));\n        }\n        None => {\n            assert!(\n                ctx.start_date.is_none() || ctx.end_date.is_none(),\n                \"an empty range must have at least one unset field\"\n            );\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Every part produces attributes without panicking.\n    let send = |_event: date_range_field::Event| {};\n    let api = service.connect(&send);\n    for part in date_range_field::Part::all() {\n        drop(api.part_attrs(part));\n    }\n\n    // Child field props mirror the tracked per-field values.\n    assert_eq!(api.start_field_props().value, Some(ctx.start_date.clone()));\n    assert_eq!(api.end_field_props().value, Some(ctx.end_date.clone()));\n}"
}

  "old_string": "### 1.3 Context\n\n```rust\n/// Context for the DateRangeField component.\n#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq)]\npub struct Context {\n    pub value: Bindable<Option<DateRange>>,\n    /// The active field.\n    pub active_field: Option<ActiveField>,\n    /// The minimum date.\n    pub min: Option<CalendarDate>,\n    /// The maximum date.\n    pub max: Option<CalendarDate>,\n    /// The locale.\n    pub locale: Locale,\n    /// Resolved translatable messages.\n    pub messages: Messages,\n    /// Whether the component is disabled.\n    pub disabled: bool,\n    /// Whether the component is readonly.\n    pub readonly: bool,\n    /// Whether the component is required.\n    pub required: bool,\n    /// The name of the component.\n    pub name: Option<String>,\n    /// Separate form name for start date.\n    pub start_name: Option<String>,\n    /// Separate form name for end date.\n    pub end_name: Option<String>,\n    /// Component IDs.\n    pub ids: ComponentIds,\n    /// Whether the Description part is rendered.\n    pub has_description: bool,\n    /// Whether the ErrorMessage part is rendered.\n    pub has_error_message: bool,\n    /// When true, all numeric segments in child DateField instances display\n    /// with leading zeros (e.g., \"03\" instead of \"3\").\n    pub force_leading_zeros: bool,\n}\n\n// Reuses ActiveField from DateRangePicker:\n// pub enum ActiveField { Start, End }\n```",
  "new_string": "### 1.3 Context\n\n`Context` derives only `Clone` and implements `Debug` manually, because\n`intl_backend` is an `Arc<dyn IntlBackend>` that cannot derive `Debug`/`PartialEq`\n(the `Machine` trait requires `Context: Clone + Debug` only).\n\nThe public `value` is the *derived complete* range — `Some` only when both\n`start_date` and `end_date` are set, always normalized so `start <= end`. The two\nfields are tracked independently so a range can be assembled incrementally as each\nchild field changes (an `Option<DateRange>` alone cannot hold a partial, one-sided\nvalue).\n\n```rust\n/// Context for the DateRangeField component.\n#[derive(Clone)]\npub struct Context {\n    /// The derived complete range (Some only when both fields are set, always normalized).\n    pub value: Bindable<Option<DateRange>>,\n    /// The start field's current value, tracked independently.\n    pub start_date: Option<CalendarDate>,\n    /// The end field's current value, tracked independently.\n    pub end_date: Option<CalendarDate>,\n    /// The active field.\n    pub active_field: Option<ActiveField>,\n    /// The minimum date.\n    pub min: Option<CalendarDate>,\n    /// The maximum date.\n    pub max: Option<CalendarDate>,\n    /// The locale.\n    pub locale: Locale,\n    /// Backend used for locale-dependent labels in range descriptions.\n    pub intl_backend: Arc<dyn IntlBackend>,\n    /// Resolved translatable messages.\n    pub messages: Messages,\n    /// Whether the component is disabled.\n    pub disabled: bool,\n    /// Whether the component is readonly.\n    pub readonly: bool,\n    /// Whether the component is required.\n    pub required: bool,\n    /// The name of the component.\n    pub name: Option<String>,\n    /// Separate form name for start date.\n    pub start_name: Option<String>,\n    /// Separate form name for end date.\n    pub end_name: Option<String>,\n    /// Component IDs.\n    pub ids: ComponentIds,\n    /// Whether the Description part is rendered.\n    pub has_description: bool,\n    /// Whether the ErrorMessage part is rendered.\n    pub has_error_message: bool,\n    /// When true, all numeric segments in child DateField instances display\n    /// with leading zeros (e.g., \"03\" instead of \"3\").\n    pub force_leading_zeros: bool,\n}\n\nimpl Context {\n    /// Returns `true` when the current range violates the min/max bounds.\n    /// The stored range is always normalized (start <= end), so the only source\n    /// of invalidity is a start earlier than `min` or an end later than `max`.\n    pub fn is_invalid(&self) -> bool {\n        let Some(range) = self.value.get() else { return false };\n        let below_min = self.min.as_ref().is_some_and(|min| {\n            matches!(range.start.compare_within_calendar(min), Some(Ordering::Less))\n        });\n        let above_max = self.max.as_ref().is_some_and(|max| {\n            matches!(range.end.compare_within_calendar(max), Some(Ordering::Greater))\n        });\n        below_min || above_max\n    }\n}\n\n/// Identifies which of the two child fields is currently active.\n///\n/// Owned by `DateRangeField` as the first range component to require it; later\n/// range components (e.g., `DateRangePicker`) reuse this enum.\n#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]\npub enum ActiveField {\n    /// The start field is active.\n    Start,\n    /// The end field is active.\n    End,\n}\n```"
}

Defaults to false,\n    /// which uses locale-aware formatting. Passed through to child DateField\n    /// instances.\n    pub force_leading_zeros: bool,\n}\n\nimpl Default for Props {\n    fn default() -> Self {\n        Self {\n            id: String::new(),\n            value: None,\n            default_value: None,\n            min: None,\n            max: None,\n            disabled: false,\n            readonly: false,\n            required: false,\n            name: None,\n            start_name: None,\n            end_name: None,\n            force_leading_zeros: false,\n        }\n    }\n}\n```",
