{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/ericson/Workspace/Rust/ars-ui.worktrees/impl-284/spec/components/date-time/date-range-field.md",
  "old_string": "    fn init(props: &Self::Props, env: &Env, messages: &Self::Messages) -> (Self::State, Self::Context) {\n        let locale = env.locale.clone();\n        let ctx = Context {\n            value: match &props.value {\n                Some(v) => Bindable::controlled(v.clone()),\n                None => Bindable::uncontrolled(props.default_value.clone()),\n            },\n            active_field: None,\n            min: props.min.clone(),\n            max: props.max.clone(),\n            messages: messages.clone(),\n            locale,\n            disabled: props.disabled,\n            readonly: props.readonly,\n            required: props.required,\n            name: props.name.clone(),\n            start_name: props.start_name.clone(),\n            end_name: props.end_name.clone(),\n            ids: ComponentIds::from_id(&props.id),\n            has_description: false,\n            has_error_message: false,\n            force_leading_zeros: props.force_leading_zeros,\n        };\n        (State::Idle, ctx)\n    }\n\n    fn transition(\n        state: &Self::State,\n        event: &Self::Event,\n        ctx: &Self::Context,\n        _props: &Self::Props,\n    ) -> Option<TransitionPlan<Self>> {\n        if is_disabled(ctx) { return None; }\n\n        match event {\n            Event::FocusStart => {\n                Some(TransitionPlan::to(State::StartFocused).apply(|ctx| {\n                    ctx.active_field = Some(ActiveField::Start);\n                }))\n            }\n\n            Event::FocusEnd => {\n                Some(TransitionPlan::to(State::EndFocused).apply(|ctx| {\n                    ctx.active_field = Some(ActiveField::End);\n                }))\n            }\n\n            Event::BlurAll => {\n                Some(TransitionPlan::to(State::Idle).apply(|ctx| {\n                    ctx.active_field = None;\n                }))\n            }\n\n            Event::SetRange(range) => {\n                let range = range.clone();\n                Some(TransitionPlan::context_only(move |ctx| {\n                    ctx.value.set(range);\n                }))\n            }\n\n            Event::StartValueChange(date) => {\n                let date = date.clone();\n                Some(TransitionPlan::context_only(move |ctx| {\n                    match (&date, ctx.value.get().as_ref().map(|r| &r.end)) {\n                        (Some(start), Some(end)) => {\n                            ctx.value.set(Some(DateRange::normalized(\n                                start.clone(), end.clone(),\n                            )));\n                        }\n                        (Some(start), None) => {\n                            // Only start set; store partial (no complete range yet)\n                            // Range will complete when end is also set.\n                        }\n                        (None, _) => {\n                            ctx.value.set(None);\n                        }\n                    }\n                }))\n            }\n\n            Event::EndValueChange(date) => {\n                let date = date.clone();\n                Some(TransitionPlan::context_only(move |ctx| {\n                    match (ctx.value.get().as_ref().map(|r| &r.start), &date) {\n                        (Some(start), Some(end)) => {\n                            ctx.value.set(Some(DateRange::normalized(\n                                start.clone(), end.clone(),\n                            )));\n                        }\n                        (None, Some(end)) => {\n                            // Only end set; store partial.\n                        }\n                        (_, None) => {\n                            ctx.value.set(None);\n                        }\n                    }\n                }))\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n```",
  "new_string": "    fn init(props: &Self::Props, env: &Env, messages: &Self::Messages) -> (Self::State, Self::Context) {\n        let value = match &props.value {\n            Some(v) => Bindable::controlled(v.clone()),\n            None => Bindable::uncontrolled(props.default_value.clone()),\n        };\n        let initial_range = value.get().clone();\n        let ctx = Context {\n            start_date: initial_range.as_ref().map(|r| r.start.clone()),\n            end_date: initial_range.as_ref().map(|r| r.end.clone()),\n            value,\n            active_field: None,\n            min: props.min.clone(),\n            max: props.max.clone(),\n            locale: env.locale.clone(),\n            intl_backend: Arc::clone(&env.intl_backend),\n            messages: messages.clone(),\n            disabled: props.disabled,\n            readonly: props.readonly,\n            required: props.required,\n            name: props.name.clone(),\n            start_name: props.start_name.clone(),\n            end_name: props.end_name.clone(),\n            ids: ComponentIds::from_id(&props.id),\n            has_description: props.has_description,\n            has_error_message: props.has_error_message,\n            force_leading_zeros: props.force_leading_zeros,\n        };\n        (State::Idle, ctx)\n    }\n\n    fn transition(\n        _state: &Self::State,\n        event: &Self::Event,\n        ctx: &Self::Context,\n        _props: &Self::Props,\n    ) -> Option<TransitionPlan<Self>> {\n        if ctx.disabled { return None; }\n\n        match event {\n            Event::FocusStart => Some(TransitionPlan::to(State::StartFocused)\n                .apply(|ctx: &mut Context| ctx.active_field = Some(ActiveField::Start))),\n\n            Event::FocusEnd => Some(TransitionPlan::to(State::EndFocused)\n                .apply(|ctx: &mut Context| ctx.active_field = Some(ActiveField::End))),\n\n            Event::BlurAll => Some(TransitionPlan::to(State::Idle)\n                .apply(|ctx: &mut Context| ctx.active_field = None)),\n\n            Event::SetRange(range) => {\n                if ctx.readonly { return None; }\n                let range = range.clone();\n                Some(TransitionPlan::context_only(move |ctx: &mut Context| {\n                    ctx.start_date = range.as_ref().map(|r| r.start.clone());\n                    ctx.end_date = range.as_ref().map(|r| r.end.clone());\n                    ctx.value.set(range);\n                }))\n            }\n\n            Event::StartValueChange(date) => {\n                if ctx.readonly { return None; }\n                let date = date.clone();\n                Some(TransitionPlan::context_only(move |ctx: &mut Context| {\n                    ctx.start_date = date;\n                    recompute_range(ctx);\n                }))\n            }\n\n            Event::EndValueChange(date) => {\n                if ctx.readonly { return None; }\n                let date = date.clone();\n                Some(TransitionPlan::context_only(move |ctx: &mut Context| {\n                    ctx.end_date = date;\n                    recompute_range(ctx);\n                }))\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n/// Recomputes the derived complete range from the two field values. When both\n/// fields hold a value the range is normalized (swapping if needed so\n/// `start <= end`) and the normalized order is reflected back into the fields.\n/// When either field is empty the range is incomplete and the value is cleared.\nfn recompute_range(ctx: &mut Context) {\n    match (ctx.start_date.clone(), ctx.end_date.clone()) {\n        (Some(start), Some(end)) => {\n            if let Some(range) = DateRange::normalized(start, end) {\n                ctx.start_date = Some(range.start.clone());\n                ctx.end_date = Some(range.end.clone());\n                ctx.value.set(Some(range));\n            }\n        }\n        _ => ctx.value.set(None),\n    }\n}\n```"
}

{

  "file_path": "/Users/ericson/Workspace/Rust/ars-ui.worktrees/impl-284/spec/components/date-time/date-range-field.md",
  "old_string": "    /// Screen reader description of the full date range (e.g., \"March 1 to March 15\").\n    /// Returns `None` if no complete range is selected.\n    /// The adapter can use this as an `aria-description` on the root or as a live announcement.\n    pub fn range_description(&self) -> Option<String> {\n        let range = self.ctx.value.get().as_ref()?;\n        let start = format_date(&range.start, &self.ctx.format, &self.ctx.locale);\n        let end = format_date(&range.end, &self.ctx.format, &self.ctx.locale);\n        Some((self.ctx.messages.range_description)(&start, &end, &self.ctx.locale))\n    }\n\n    /// Build DateField Props for the start date input.\n    pub fn start_field_props(&self) -> date_field::Props {\n        date_field::Props {\n            id: self.ctx.ids.part(\"start\"),\n            value: self.ctx.value.get().as_ref().map(|r| Some(r.start.clone())),\n            min: self.ctx.min.clone(),\n            max: self.ctx.value.get().as_ref().map(|r| r.end.clone()),\n            disabled: self.ctx.disabled,\n            readonly: self.ctx.readonly,\n            aria_label: Some((self.ctx.messages.start_label)(&self.ctx.locale)),\n            force_leading_zeros: self.ctx.force_leading_zeros,\n            ..Default::default()\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// Build DateField Props for the end date input.\n    pub fn end_field_props(&self) -> date_field::Props {\n        date_field::Props {\n            id: self.ctx.ids.part(\"end\"),\n            value: self.ctx.value.get().as_ref().map(|r| Some(r.end.clone())),\n            min: self.ctx.value.get().as_ref().map(|r| r.start.clone()),\n            max: self.ctx.max.clone(),\n            disabled: self.ctx.disabled,\n            readonly: self.ctx.readonly,\n            aria_label: Some((self.ctx.messages.end_label)(&self.ctx.locale)),\n            force_leading_zeros: self.ctx.force_leading_zeros,\n            ..Default::default()\n        }\n    }",
  "new_string": "    /// Marker attributes (scope + part data hooks) for the start-field wrapper.\n    /// The embedded child DateField is configured via `start_field_props`.\n    pub fn start_field_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {\n        let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();\n        let [(scope_attr, scope_val), (part_attr, part_val)] = Part::StartField.data_attrs();\n        attrs.set(scope_attr, scope_val);\n        attrs.set(part_attr, part_val);\n        attrs\n    }\n\n    /// Marker attributes (scope + part data hooks) for the end-field wrapper.\n    pub fn end_field_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {\n        let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();\n        let [(scope_attr, scope_val), (part_attr, part_val)] = Part::EndField.data_attrs();\n        attrs.set(scope_attr, scope_val);\n        attrs.set(part_attr, part_val);\n        attrs\n    }\n\n    /// Screen reader description of the full date range (e.g., \"March 1, 2025 to\n    /// March 15, 2025\"). Returns `None` if no complete range is selected.\n    /// The adapter can use this as an `aria-description` on the root or as a live announcement.\n    pub fn range_description(&self) -> Option<String> {\n        let range = self.ctx.value.get().as_ref()?;\n        let start = format_date_label(&range.start, self.ctx.intl_backend.as_ref(), &self.ctx.locale);\n        let end = format_date_label(&range.end, self.ctx.intl_backend.as_ref(), &self.ctx.locale);\n        Some((self.ctx.messages.range_description)(&start, &end, &self.ctx.locale))\n    }\n\n    /// Build DateField Props for the start date input. The start field's lower\n    /// bound is the global `min`; its upper bound is the current end date when\n    /// set, otherwise the global `max`, so both global bounds and cross-field\n    /// coordination apply.\n    pub fn start_field_props(&self) -> date_field::Props {\n        date_field::Props {\n            id: self.ctx.ids.part(\"start\"),\n            value: Some(self.ctx.start_date.clone()),\n            min_value: self.ctx.min.clone(),\n            max_value: self.ctx.end_date.clone().or_else(|| self.ctx.max.clone()),\n            disabled: self.ctx.disabled,\n            readonly: self.ctx.readonly,\n            required: self.ctx.required,\n            aria_label: Some((self.ctx.messages.start_label)(&self.ctx.locale)),\n            force_leading_zeros: self.ctx.force_leading_zeros,\n            ..Default::default()\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// Build DateField Props for the end date input. The end field's upper bound\n    /// is the global `max`; its lower bound is the current start date when set,\n    /// otherwise the global `min`.\n    pub fn end_field_props(&self) -> date_field::Props {\n        date_field::Props {\n            id: self.ctx.ids.part(\"end\"),\n            value: Some(self.ctx.end_date.clone()),\n            min_value: self.ctx.start_date.clone().or_else(|| self.ctx.min.clone()),\n            max_value: self.ctx.max.clone(),\n            disabled: self.ctx.disabled,\n            readonly: self.ctx.readonly,\n            required: self.ctx.required,\n            aria_label: Some((self.ctx.messages.end_label)(&self.ctx.locale)),\n            force_leading_zeros: self.ctx.force_leading_zeros,\n            ..Default::default()\n        }\n    }"
}

  "new_string": "        if let Some(ref name) = self.ctx.name {\n            attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Name, name);\n        }\n        // `DateRange::to_iso8601` already emits the `start/end` interval form.\n        let iso_value = match self.ctx.value.get() {\n            Some(range) => range.to_iso8601(),\n            None => String::new(),\n        };\n        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Value, iso_value);\n        attrs\n    }\n\n    /// Attributes for a separate hidden input carrying the start date.\n    /// Only meaningful when `start_name` is set on Props.\n    pub fn start_hidden_input_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {\n        let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();\n        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Type, \"hidden\");\n        if let Some(name) = &self.ctx.start_name {\n            attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Name, name);\n        }\n        let value = match self.ctx.value.get() {\n            Some(range) => range.start.to_iso8601(),\n            None => String::new(),\n        };\n        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Value, value);\n        attrs\n    }\n\n    /// Attributes for a separate hidden input carrying the end date.\n    /// Only meaningful when `end_name` is set on Props.\n    pub fn end_hidden_input_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {\n        let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();\n        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Type, \"hidden\");\n        if let Some(name) = &self.ctx.end_name {\n            attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Name, name);\n        }\n        let value = match self.ctx.value.get() {\n            Some(range) => range.end.to_iso8601(),\n            None => String::new(),\n        };\n        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Value, value);\n        attrs\n    }\n\n    // Event dispatch helpers called by adapters: `focus_start`, `focus_end`,\n    // `blur`, `set_range`, `set_start_value`, `set_end_value` — each forwards\n    // the matching `Event` through `send`."

  "new_string": "    fn part_attrs(&self, part: Part) -> AttrMap {\n        match part {\n            Part::Root => self.root_attrs(),\n            Part::Label => self.label_attrs(),\n            Part::StartField => self.start_field_attrs(), // wrapper markers; child via start_field_props\n            Part::Separator => self.separator_attrs(),\n            Part::EndField => self.end_field_attrs(), // wrapper markers; child via end_field_props\n            Part::Description => self.description_attrs(),\n            Part::ErrorMessage => self.error_message_attrs(),\n            Part::HiddenInput => self.hidden_input_attrs(),\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n/// Formats a single date as a human-readable label for screen-reader range\n/// descriptions (e.g., \"March 1, 2025\").\nfn format_date_label(date: &CalendarDate, backend: &dyn IntlBackend, locale: &Locale) -> String {\n    format!(\"{} {}, {}\", backend.month_long_name(date.month(), locale), date.day(), date.year())\n}\n```\n\nThe `StartField` and `EndField` parts carry only the wrapper scope/part data\nhooks. The embedded child `DateField` components are configured through the\n`start_field_props` / `end_field_props` methods, whose `role=\"group\"` sub-group\nand per-field `aria-label` come from the child machine — keeping semantic data\nseparate from the rendered child view per the API design standards. Live segment\nfocus and caret behavior are resolved by the adapter against native handles keyed\nby logical segment, not by these string ids."
}

normalized should swap\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn disabled_ignores_events() {\n        let props = Props { disabled: true, ..Props::default() };\n        let (state, ctx) = Machine::init(&props, &Env::default(), &Default::default());\n        assert!(Machine::transition(&state, &Event::FocusStart, &ctx, &props).is_none());\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn set_range_updates_value() {\n        let props = Props::default();\n        let (state, ctx) = Machine::init(&props, &Env::default(), &Default::default());\n        let range = DateRange::new(\n            CalendarDate::new_gregorian(2025, nzu8(6), nzu8(1)),\n            CalendarDate::new_gregorian(2025, nzu8(6), nzu8(30)),\n        );\n        let plan = Machine::transition(\n            &state,\n            &Event::SetRange(Some(range)),\n            &ctx,\n            &props,\n        ).unwrap();\n        assert!(plan.target.is_none()); // context-only\n    }\n}\n```",
  "new_string": "`CalendarDate::new_gregorian(year, month, day)` takes plain `u8` month/day and\nreturns `Result`; tests use a `date(y, m, d)` helper that `.expect`s the result.\n`DateRange::new`/`DateRange::normalized` return `Option`. Snapshot tests prefixed\n`snapshot_` use `insta::assert_snapshot!`. The full test suite lives in\n`crates/ars-components/src/date_time/date_range_field/tests.rs` (unit + snapshot),\n`tests/spec_conformance/date_time.rs` (anatomy), and\n`tests/proptest_state_machines/date_time.rs` (normalized-range invariants).\n\n```rust\n#[cfg(test)]\nmod tests {\n    use super::*;\n\n    fn date(year: i32, month: u8, day: u8) -> CalendarDate {\n        CalendarDate::new_gregorian(year, month, day).expect(\"valid test date\")\n    }\n    fn range(start: CalendarDate, end: CalendarDate) -> DateRange {\n        DateRange::new(start, end).expect(\"ordered test range\")\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn focus_tracking() {\n        let props = Props::default();\n        let (state, ctx) = Machine::init(&props, &Env::default(), &Default::default());\n        let plan = Machine::transition(&state, &Event::FocusStart, &ctx, &props).unwrap();\n        assert_eq!(plan.target, Some(State::StartFocused));\n        let plan = Machine::transition(&State::EndFocused, &Event::BlurAll, &ctx, &props).unwrap();\n        assert_eq!(plan.target, Some(State::Idle));\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn out_of_order_change_normalizes() {\n        // Setting start after the existing end swaps so start <= end always holds.\n        let props = Props { default_value: Some(range(date(2025, 6, 10), date(2025, 6, 20))), ..Props::default() };\n        let mut svc = Service::<Machine>::new(props, &Env::default(), &Messages::default());\n        drop(svc.send(Event::StartValueChange(Some(date(2025, 6, 25)))));\n        let stored = svc.context().value.get().clone().unwrap();\n        assert_eq!((stored.start, stored.end), (date(2025, 6, 20), date(2025, 6, 25)));\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn disabled_ignores_events() {\n        let props = Props { disabled: true, ..Props::default() };\n        let (state, ctx) = Machine::init(&props, &Env::default(), &Default::default());\n        assert!(Machine::transition(&state, &Event::FocusStart, &ctx, &props).is_none());\n    }\n}\n```"

451	    fn transition(
452	        _state: &Self::State,
453	        event: &Self::Event,
454	        ctx: &Self::Context,
455	        _props: &Self::Props,
456	    ) -> Option<TransitionPlan<Self>> {
457	        if ctx.disabled {
458	            return None;
459	        }
460	
461	        match event {
462	            Event::FocusStart => Some(TransitionPlan::to(State::StartFocused).apply(
463	                |ctx: &mut Context| {
464	                    ctx.active_field = Some(ActiveField::Start);
465	                },
466	            )),
467	
468	            Event::FocusEnd => Some(TransitionPlan::to(State::EndFocused).apply(
469	                |ctx: &mut Context| {
470	                    ctx.active_field = Some(ActiveField::End);
471	                },
472	            )),
473	
474	            Event::BlurAll => Some(TransitionPlan::to(State::Idle).apply(|ctx: &mut Context| {
475	                ctx.active_field = None;
476	            })),
477	
478	            Event::SetRange(range) => {
479	                if ctx.readonly {
480	                    return None;
481	                }
482	
483	                let range = range.clone();
484	                Some(TransitionPlan::context_only(move |ctx: &mut Context| {
485	                    ctx.start_date = range.as_ref().map(|range| range.start.clone());
486	                    ctx.end_date = range.as_ref().map(|range| range.end.clone());
487	                    ctx.value.set(range);
488	                }))
489	            }
490	
491	            Event::StartValueChange(date) => {
492	                if ctx.readonly {
493	                    return None;
494	                }
495	
496	                let date = date.clone();
497	                Some(TransitionPlan::context_only(move |ctx: &mut Context| {
498	                    apply_start_change(ctx, date);
499	                }))
500	            }
501	
502	            Event::EndValueChange(date) => {
503	                if ctx.readonly {
504	                    return None;
505	                }
506	
507	                let date = date.clone();
508	                Some(TransitionPlan::context_only(move |ctx: &mut Context| {
509	                    apply_end_change(ctx, date);
510	                }))
511	            }
512	        }
513	    }

590	hooks. The embedded child `DateField` components are configured through the
591	`start_field_props` / `end_field_props` methods, whose `role="group"` sub-group
592	and per-field `aria-label` come from the child machine — keeping semantic data
593	separate from the rendered child view per the API design standards. Live segment
594	focus and caret behavior are resolved by the adapter against native handles keyed
595	by logical segment, not by these string ids.
596	
597	## 2. Anatomy
598	
599	```text
600	DateRangeField (en-US)
601	└── Root                                  role="group"  data-ars-scope="date-range-field"
602	    ├── Label                             "Trip dates"
603	    ├── StartField (DateField)            role="group"  aria-label="Start date"
604	    │   ├── Segment (Month)               role="spinbutton"  aria-label="Month"
605	    │   ├── Literal "/"                   aria-hidden="true"
606	    │   ├── Segment (Day)                 role="spinbutton"  aria-label="Day"
607	    │   ├── Literal "/"                   aria-hidden="true"
608	    │   └── Segment (Year)               role="spinbutton"  aria-label="Year"
609	    ├── Separator                         " – "  aria-hidden="true"
610	    ├── EndField (DateField)              role="group"  aria-label="End date"
611	    │   ├── Segment (Month)               role="spinbutton"  aria-label="Month"
612	    │   ├── Literal "/"                   aria-hidden="true"
613	    │   ├── Segment (Day)                 role="spinbutton"  aria-label="Day"
614	    │   ├── Literal "/"                   aria-hidden="true"
615	    │   └── Segment (Year)               role="spinbutton"  aria-label="Year"
616	    ├── Description                       "Select check-in and check-out dates"
617	    ├── ErrorMessage                      role="alert"
618	    └── HiddenInput                       type="hidden"  value="2025-06-01/2025-06-15"
619	```
620	
621	| Part           | Element                 | Description                                                    |
622	| -------------- | ----------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------- |
623	| `Root`         | `<div>`                 | `role="group"` — groups both fields; `aria-labelledby` → Label |
624	| `Label`        | `<label>`               | Shared label for the entire range                              |
625	| `StartField`   | _(DateField)_           | Start date segments (delegated to DateField)                   |
626	| `Separator`    | `<span>`                | `aria-hidden="true"` — visual separator (" -- " or "to")       |
627	| `EndField`     | _(DateField)_           | End date segments (delegated to DateField)                     |
628	| `Description`  | `<div>`                 | Optional help text; referenced by `aria-describedby`           |
629	| `ErrorMessage` | `<div role="alert">`    | Validation error text; announced immediately                   |
630	| `HiddenInput`  | `<input type="hidden">` | ISO 8601 range value for form submission                       |
631	
632	## 3. Accessibility
633	
634	### 3.1 ARIA Roles, States, and Properties
635	
636	| Part       | Role/Attribute     | Details                                                      |
637	| ---------- | ------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------ |
638	| Root       | `role="group"`     | `aria-labelledby` points to Label                            |
639	| StartField | --                 | Delegates to DateField; each segment has `role="spinbutton"` |
640	| EndField   | --                 | Delegates to DateField; each segment has `role="spinbutton"` |
641	| Root       | `aria-disabled`    | Present when disabled                                        |
642	| Root       | `aria-readonly`    | Present when readonly                                        |
643	| Root       | `data-ars-invalid` | Present when validation fails                                |
644	| Root       | `aria-describedby` | Wired to Description and ErrorMessage part IDs               |
645	| Root       | `aria-required`    | Present when required                                        |
646	
647	### 3.2 Keyboard Interaction
648	
649	| Key        | Action                                                          |
650	| ---------- | --------------------------------------------------------------- |
651	| Tab        | Move focus from last segment of StartField to first of EndField |
652	| Shift+Tab  | Move focus from first segment of EndField to last of StartField |
653	| ArrowUp    | Increment focused segment value                                 |
654	| ArrowDown  | Decrement focused segment value                                 |
655	| ArrowLeft  | Move to previous segment (within current field)                 |
656	| ArrowRight | Move to next segment (within current field)                     |
```

All audits and verification pass. Here's the consolidated result for your review.

## Task #284 — DateRangeField agnostic core: complete

### What landed
New module `crates/ars-components/src/date_time/date_range_field/` (mod.rs + tests.rs + 18 `.snap` fixtures), registered in [date_time/mod.rs](crates/ars-components/src/date_time/mod.rs), plus conformance + proptest entries and a fully-synced spec.

A stateful composition of two `DateField` instances with shared range coordination: `Idle/StartFocused/EndFocused` state machine, range normalization, min/max coordination across both fields, `role="group"` root with labelled sub-groups, separator, and combined + separate hidden form inputs.

### Key design decisions (resolved spec/code mismatches, all landed in this PR)
- **Independent field tracking.** The spec's transition logic couldn't complete a range from two separate `StartValueChange`/`EndValueChange` events (`Option<DateRange>` can't hold a one-sided value). Added `start_date`/`end_date` to Context; `value` is the *derived* normalized complete range. This was a real gap shared by the spec — fixed in both.
- **Validation (your call: normalize + invalid flag).** Range is always normalized (`start <= end` structural invariant); `data-ars-invalid` + `is_invalid()` derive from min/max bound violations.
- **8 API-drift fixes:** `min_value`/`max_value` (not `min`/`max`), `DateRange::normalized`→`Option`, `intl_backend` in Context for `range_description`, `to_iso8601` for ISO output, `ActiveField` defined here (DateRangePicker will reuse), `u8`/`Result` date construction, derived `Default`, `has_description`/`has_error_message` props to make the `aria-describedby` branch reachable.
- `StartField`/`EndField` parts emit wrapper markers; child config flows through `start_field_props()`/`end_field_props()` (matches the existing adapter specs).

### Audit (3 phases)
- **Phase 1 (drift):** spec/impl aligned — synced both sides.
- **Phase 2 (3 rounds):** 1 finding (unused `Api::new` → now used by `connect`); rounds 2–3 clean.
- **Phase 3 (coverage):** found+fixed 3 gaps (readonly value-change guards, empty separate hidden inputs, `Debug` impls). **100% line, 100% function, 99.59% region**; the 3 missed regions are the unreachable single-calendar `normalized()→None` guard.

### Verification
| Check | Result |
|---|---|
| Unit + snapshot tests | 56 pass |
| Spec-conformance | pass |
| Proptest (normalized-range invariant, disabled-noop) | 2 pass |
| `clippy --workspace -D warnings` | clean |
| `insta --unreferenced=reject` (`--features i18n`) | no unreferenced |
| `spec validate` | 340 files pass |
| spec snippet compile | pass |
| `cargo doc` | 0 new warnings |
| snapshot-count gate | `date_range_field … OK` |

Adapter (Leptos/Dioxus) components remain out of scope.\n\n## Design\n\n- **Independent field tracking.** Context holds `start_date`/`end_date` per child; the public `value: Bindable<Option<DateRange>>` is the *derived complete* range — `Some` only when both fields are set, always normalized so `start <= end`. This lets a range complete incrementally across separate `StartValueChange`/`EndValueChange` events (an `Option<DateRange>` alone can't hold a one-sided value).\n- **Validation.** Out-of-order entry normalizes (swaps); min/max bound violations surface as `data-ars-invalid` on Root + `Api::is_invalid()`.\n- **Composition.** `StartField`/`EndField` parts emit wrapper scope/part markers; the embedded child `DateField`s are configured via `start_field_props()`/`end_field_props()` with cross-field min/max coordination (start.max = current end ∨ global max; end.min = current start ∨ global min).\n- **Forms.** Combined hidden input (`YYYY-MM-DD/YYYY-MM-DD` via `DateRange::to_iso8601`) plus optional separate `start_name`/`end_name` inputs.\n\n## Tests\n\n- 56 unit + snapshot tests (18 committed `.snap` fixtures covering every part and each output-affecting state/branch)\n- Spec-conformance anatomy test for all 8 parts\n- 2 proptest invariants (normalized-range across arbitrary event sequences; disabled-noop)\n- Module coverage: **100% line / 100% function**\n\n## Spec sync\n\n`spec/components/date-time/date-range-field.md` was synchronized with the implemented contract (the spec's code examples had drifted from current APIs): independent field tracking + `recompute_range`, `intl_backend` in Context for `range_description`, `has_description`/`has_error_message` props, derived `Default`, `ActiveField` owned here (DateRangePicker will reuse), `min_value`/`max_value` child-prop names, `Option`-returning `DateRange::normalized`, and `to_iso8601` output.\n\nPost-implementation audit (3 phases) run; all findings landed in this PR. `cargo xci-fast` green (all 10 gates).\n\nCloses #284\n\n🤖 Generated with [Claude Code](https://claude.com/claude-code)\nEOF\n)\" 2>&1 | tail -5",

===THREAD PRRT_kwDORp4enM6Gg4qV resolved=false===
file=crates/ars-components/src/date_time/date_range_field/mod.rs:402 dbId=3343207687
**<sub><sub>![P1 Badge](https://img.shields.io/badge/P1-orange?style=flat)</sub></sub>  Synchronize reactive prop updates**

Because this `Machine` does not override `on_props_changed`, `Service::set_props()` emits no sync event for this component. In the Leptos/Dioxus adapters, reactive prop changes are applied through `Service::set_props()`, so changing `value`, `min`, `max`, `disabled`, `readonly`, `name`, or the description/error flags after mount leaves `ctx` stale; for example, a controlled parent updating `value` will not update the hidden input or child DateField props.

Useful? React with 👍 / 👎.

===THREAD PRRT_kwDORp4enM6Gg4qb resolved=false===
file=crates/ars-components/src/date_time/date_range_field/mod.rs:552 dbId=3343207696
**<sub><sub>![P2 Badge](https://img.shields.io/badge/P2-yellow?style=flat)</sub></sub>  Clear stale range when normalization fails**

When the two tracked dates are not comparable (for example, a public `set_start_value`/`set_end_value` call supplies a date from a different calendar or era), `DateRange::normalized` returns `None` and this branch leaves the previous `ctx.value` untouched. That makes the hidden input, `selected_range()`, and validation continue reporting an old range even though `start_date`/`end_date` now contain different values; this path should clear or otherwise reconcile the derived range when normalization fails.

Useful? React with 👍 / 👎.

===THREAD PRRT_kwDORp4enM6Gg4qg resolved=false===
file=crates/ars-components/src/date_time/date_range_field/mod.rs:785 dbId=3343207700
**<sub><sub>![P2 Badge](https://img.shields.io/badge/P2-yellow?style=flat)</sub></sub>  Avoid clamping away out-of-order edits**

With an existing end date, this `max_value` is passed to the child `DateField`, whose publish path clamps completed dates to `max_value` before the parent sees them. In the actual composed UI, editing the start field from `2025-06-10` to `2025-06-25` while the end is `2025-06-20` is clamped to `2025-06-20`, so the parent never receives the out-of-order date needed to perform the documented swap/normalization; the symmetric `end_field_props` `min_value` has the same problem.

Useful? React with 👍 / 👎.

===THREAD PRRT_kwDORp4enM6Gg4qk resolved=false===
file=crates/ars-components/src/date_time/date_range_field/mod.rs:890 dbId=3343207704
**<sub><sub>![P2 Badge](https://img.shields.io/badge/P2-yellow?style=flat)</sub></sub>  Submit partial separate date values**

When `start_name`/`end_name` are used, these separate hidden inputs are documented as carrying the individual start/end dates, but their values are derived from the complete range. If a user has entered only a start date, `ctx.start_date` is set while `ctx.value` is still `None`, so the start hidden input submits an empty value instead of the visible start date; the end input has the same issue for a one-sided end value.

Useful? React with 👍 / 👎.

===THREAD PRRT_kwDORp4enM6Gg4qn resolved=false===
file=crates/ars-components/src/date_time/date_range_field/mod.rs:785 dbId=3343207707
**<sub><sub>![P2 Badge](https://img.shields.io/badge/P2-yellow?style=flat)</sub></sub>  Expose invalidity to the child fields**

When the range violates `min`/`max`, only the root gets `data-ars-invalid`; the composed `DateField` props keep their default `invalid: false`, so the focusable child field groups never receive `aria-invalid`. In the invalid-range scenarios already detected by `ctx.is_invalid()`, screen readers have no invalid state on the actual editable controls unless the adapter adds it out of band.

Useful? React with 👍 / 👎.

===THREAD PRRT_kwDORp4enM6Gg4rT resolved=false===
file=crates/ars-components/src/date_time/date_range_field/mod.rs:402 dbId=3343207756
**<sub><sub>![P1 Badge](https://img.shields.io/badge/P1-orange?style=flat)</sub></sub>  Add prop synchronization for DateRangeField**

In any adapter path where the parent re-renders with new props after mount, `Service::set_props` only feeds changes through `Machine::on_props_changed`; this machine does not override that hook, so controlled `value` updates and prop-backed fields like `disabled`, `readonly`, `min`, `max`, names, and description flags never reach `Context`. For example, changing a controlled range from `Some(old)` to `Some(new)` leaves `ctx.value`, `start_date`, `end_date`, and the hidden input on the old range indefinitely, making the controlled component unusable after initialization.

Useful? React with 👍 / 👎.

===THREAD PRRT_kwDORp4enM6Gg4rX resolved=false===
file=crates/ars-components/src/date_time/date_range_field/mod.rs:779 dbId=3343207760
**<sub><sub>![P2 Badge](https://img.shields.io/badge/P2-yellow?style=flat)</sub></sub>  Respect global bounds when deriving child limits**

When the current opposite endpoint lies outside the configured global bound, this `or_else` drops the global limit instead of applying both constraints: with `max = 2025-06-30` and a controlled/default end date of `2025-07-15`, the start `DateField` receives `max_value = 2025-07-15`, allowing edits beyond the component's maximum; the mirrored `end_field_props` path similarly lets an out-of-bounds start date override `min`. Since these props are the child fields' selectable limits, combine the cross-field endpoint with the global min/max rather than choosing the endpoint unconditionally.

Useful? React with 👍 / 👎.

===THREAD PRRT_kwDORp4enM6Gg4rY resolved=false===
file=crates/ars-components/src/date_time/date_range_field/mod.rs:858 dbId=3343207761
**<sub><sub>![P2 Badge](https://img.shields.io/badge/P2-yellow?style=flat)</sub></sub>  Disable hidden inputs when the range field is disabled**

When `Props { disabled: true, name: Some(..), ... }` is rendered with a selected range, this hidden input still has a `name` and value but no `disabled` attribute, so browser form submission includes a value for a disabled DateRangeField. The split `start_hidden_input_attrs`/`end_hidden_input_attrs` helpers below have the same omission; disabled form controls should be omitted from submission, as the existing DatePicker hidden-input helper already does.

Useful? React with 👍 / 👎.

fn maybe_publish(ctx: &mut Context) {
    if !ctx.is_complete() {
        return;
    }

    let Some(date) = ctx.assemble_date() else {
        return;
    };

    let clamped = clamp_date(date, ctx.min_value.as_ref(), ctx.max_value.as_ref());

    ctx.apply_date_to_segments(&clamped);

    ctx.value.set(Some(clamped));
}

=== F6: date_picker hidden_input_attrs disabled handling ===
    pub fn hidden_input_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {
        let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();
        let [(scope_attr, scope_val), (part_attr, part_val)] = Part::HiddenInput.data_attrs();

        attrs
            .set(scope_attr, scope_val)
            .set(part_attr, part_val)
            .set(HtmlAttr::Type, "hidden");

        if let Some(name) = &self.ctx.name {
            attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Name, name);
        }

        // A disabled picker must not submit: a disabled form control is excluded
        // from submission, mirroring the disabled visible input and buttons.
        if self.ctx.disabled {
            attrs.set_bool(HtmlAttr::Disabled, true);
        }

        // Always the canonical ISO 8601 string (`to_iso8601` reads the date's
        // ISO slots, not the display-calendar `year()/month()/day()` fields), so
        // a non-Gregorian value still submits the correct calendar-independent
        // date.
        let value = self
            .ctx
            .value
            .get()
            .as_ref()
            .map(CalendarDate::to_iso8601)
            .unwrap_or_default();

        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Value, value);

        attrs
    }

1957:fn sync_props(ctx: &mut Context, props: &Props) {
--- sync_props body ---
fn sync_props(ctx: &mut Context, props: &Props) {
    let previous_calendar = ctx.calendar;
    let previous_granularity = ctx.granularity;
    let previous_segment_order = ctx.segment_order.clone();
    let previous_force_leading_zeros = ctx.force_leading_zeros;

    let buffered_segment = ctx
        .focused_segment
        .filter(|_| !ctx.type_buffer.is_empty())
        .map(|kind| (kind, ctx.get_segment_value(kind)));

    ctx.calendar = resolve_calendar(&ctx.locale, props.calendar);
    ctx.granularity = props.granularity;
    ctx.disabled = props.disabled;
    ctx.readonly = props.readonly;
    ctx.min_value = props.min_value.clone();
    ctx.max_value = props.max_value.clone();
    ctx.invalid = props.invalid;
    ctx.force_leading_zeros = props.force_leading_zeros;
    ctx.segment_order = props.segment_order.clone();

    let value_application_deferred = if let Some(value) = &props.value {
        !apply_controlled_value_update(ctx, value.clone())
    } else {
        let preserve_buffered_handoff =
            ctx.focused_segment.is_some() && !ctx.type_buffer.is_empty();

        ctx.pending_controlled_value = None;
        ctx.value.sync_controlled(None);

        preserve_buffered_handoff
    };

    let must_rebuild = previous_calendar != ctx.calendar
        || previous_granularity != ctx.granularity
        || previous_segment_order != ctx.segment_order
        || previous_force_leading_zeros != ctx.force_leading_zeros;

    if must_rebuild {
        ctx.rebuild_segments();
    } else {
        ctx.refresh_segment_ranges();
    }

    if value_application_deferred {
        restore_buffered_segment(ctx, buffered_segment);

        return;
    }

    if let Some(value) = ctx.value.get().clone() {
        let clamped = clamp_date(value, ctx.min_value.as_ref(), ctx.max_value.as_ref());

        ctx.apply_date_to_segments(&clamped);

        apply_current_value(ctx, Some(clamped));
    }
}

```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/ericson/Workspace/Rust/ars-ui.worktrees/impl-284/crates/ars-components/src/date_time/date_range_field/mod.rs",
  "old_string": "    fn connect<'a>(\n        state: &'a Self::State,\n        ctx: &'a Self::Context,\n        props: &'a Self::Props,\n        send: &'a dyn Fn(Self::Event),\n    ) -> Self::Api<'a> {\n        Api::new(state, ctx, props, send)\n    }\n}",
  "new_string": "    fn on_props_changed(old: &Self::Props, new: &Self::Props) -> Vec<Self::Event> {\n        debug_assert_eq!(\n            old.id, new.id,\n            \"date_range_field::Props.id must remain stable after init\"\n        );\n\n        if old == new {\n            Vec::new()\n        } else {\n            vec![Event::SyncProps(Box::new(new.clone()))]\n        }\n    }\n\n    fn connect<'a>(\n        state: &'a Self::State,\n        ctx: &'a Self::Context,\n        props: &'a Self::Props,\n        send: &'a dyn Fn(Self::Event),\n    ) -> Self::Api<'a> {\n        Api::new(state, ctx, props, send)\n    }\n}\n\n/// Re-derives mutable context fields from changed props.\n///\n/// `id` is fixed at construction (see [`Machine::on_props_changed`]). For a\n/// controlled component (`props.value` is `Some`) the controlled override and\n/// the per-field working values are reconciled to the new range; an\n/// uncontrolled component keeps its internal working state and merely drops any\n/// stale controlled override.\nfn sync_props(ctx: &mut Context, props: &Props) {\n    ctx.min = props.min.clone();\n    ctx.max = props.max.clone();\n    ctx.disabled = props.disabled;\n    ctx.readonly = props.readonly;\n    ctx.required = props.required;\n    ctx.name = props.name.clone();\n    ctx.start_name = props.start_name.clone();\n    ctx.end_name = props.end_name.clone();\n    ctx.has_description = props.has_description;\n    ctx.has_error_message = props.has_error_message;\n    ctx.force_leading_zeros = props.force_leading_zeros;\n\n    if let Some(controlled) = &props.value {\n        ctx.value.sync_controlled(Some(controlled.clone()));\n        ctx.start_date = controlled.as_ref().map(|range| range.start.clone());\n        ctx.end_date = controlled.as_ref().map(|range| range.end.clone());\n    } else {\n        ctx.value.sync_controlled(None);\n    }\n}"
}

{

  "old_string": "fn recompute_range(ctx: &mut Context) {\n    match (ctx.start_date.clone(), ctx.end_date.clone()) {\n        (Some(start), Some(end)) => {\n            if let Some(range) = DateRange::normalized(start, end) {\n                ctx.start_date = Some(range.start.clone());\n                ctx.end_date = Some(range.end.clone());\n                ctx.value.set(Some(range));\n            }\n        }\n        _ => ctx.value.set(None),\n    }\n}",
  "new_string": "fn recompute_range(ctx: &mut Context) {\n    match (ctx.start_date.clone(), ctx.end_date.clone()) {\n        (Some(start), Some(end)) => match DateRange::normalized(start, end) {\n            Some(range) => {\n                ctx.start_date = Some(range.start.clone());\n                ctx.end_date = Some(range.end.clone());\n                ctx.value.set(Some(range));\n            }\n            // The two dates are not comparable (e.g. different calendars/eras\n            // supplied via the public setters); clear the derived range rather\n            // than leave a stale value behind the new field values.\n            None => ctx.value.set(None),\n        },\n        _ => ctx.value.set(None),\n    }\n}"
}

176	impl Context {
177	    /// Returns `true` when the current range violates the configured min/max
178	    /// bounds.
179	    ///
180	    /// The stored range always satisfies `start <= end` (it is normalized on
181	    /// every value change), so the only remaining source of invalidity is a
182	    /// start earlier than `min` or an end later than `max`.
183	    #[must_use]
184	    pub fn is_invalid(&self) -> bool {
185	        let Some(range) = self.value.get() else {
186	            return false;
187	        };
188	
189	        let below_min = self.min.as_ref().is_some_and(|min| {
190	            matches!(
191	                range.start.compare_within_calendar(min),
192	                Some(Ordering::Less)
193	            )
194	        });
195	
196	        let above_max = self.max.as_ref().is_some_and(|max| {
197	            matches!(
198	                range.end.compare_within_calendar(max),
199	                Some(Ordering::Greater)
200	            )
201	        });
202	
203	        below_min || above_max
204	    }
205	}

Let me refactor `is_invalid` to use a shared helper and add the `date_out_of_bounds` function:

> TOOL

tool_use Edit
id: toolu_016iHyXUWuLMR6XxfSF9Xu2N
```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/ericson/Workspace/Rust/ars-ui.worktrees/impl-284/crates/ars-components/src/date_time/date_range_field/mod.rs",
  "old_string": "    #[must_use]\n    pub fn is_invalid(&self) -> bool {\n        let Some(range) = self.value.get() else {\n            return false;\n        };\n\n        let below_min = self.min.as_ref().is_some_and(|min| {\n            matches!(\n                range.start.compare_within_calendar(min),\n                Some(Ordering::Less)\n            )\n        });\n\n        let above_max = self.max.as_ref().is_some_and(|max| {\n            matches!(\n                range.end.compare_within_calendar(max),\n                Some(Ordering::Greater)\n            )\n        });\n\n        below_min || above_max\n    }\n}",
  "new_string": "    #[must_use]\n    pub fn is_invalid(&self) -> bool {\n        let Some(range) = self.value.get() else {\n            return false;\n        };\n\n        // A normalized range (`start <= end`) lies within `[min, max]` iff\n        // neither endpoint is out of bounds.\n        date_out_of_bounds(Some(&range.start), self.min.as_ref(), self.max.as_ref())\n            || date_out_of_bounds(Some(&range.end), self.min.as_ref(), self.max.as_ref())\n    }\n}\n\n/// Returns `true` when `date` is present and falls outside the inclusive\n/// `[min, max]` bounds (either bound absent means unbounded on that side).\nfn date_out_of_bounds(\n    date: Option<&CalendarDate>,\n    min: Option<&CalendarDate>,\n    max: Option<&CalendarDate>,\n) -> bool {\n    let Some(date) = date else {\n        return false;\n    };\n\n    let below_min =\n        min.is_some_and(|min| matches!(date.compare_within_calendar(min), Some(Ordering::Less)));\n    let above_max =\n        max.is_some_and(|max| matches!(date.compare_within_calendar(max), Some(Ordering::Greater)));\n\n    below_min || above_max\n}"
}

  "old_string": "    /// Builds the child [`date_field::Props`] for the start date input.\n    ///\n    /// The start field's lower bound is the global `min`; its upper bound is the\n    /// current end date when a range is selected, otherwise the global `max`, so\n    /// both global bounds and cross-field coordination apply.\n    #[must_use]\n    pub fn start_field_props(&self) -> date_field::Props {\n        let max_value = self.ctx.end_date.clone().or_else(|| self.ctx.max.clone());\n\n        date_field::Props {\n            id: self.ctx.ids.part(\"start\"),\n            value: Some(self.ctx.start_date.clone()),\n            min_value: self.ctx.min.clone(),\n            max_value,\n            disabled: self.ctx.disabled,\n            readonly: self.ctx.readonly,\n            required: self.ctx.required,\n            aria_label: Some((self.ctx.messages.start_label)(&self.ctx.locale)),\n            force_leading_zeros: self.ctx.force_leading_zeros,\n            ..date_field::Props::default()\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// Builds the child [`date_field::Props`] for the end date input.\n    ///\n    /// The end field's upper bound is the global `max`; its lower bound is the\n    /// current start date when a range is selected, otherwise the global `min`.\n    #[must_use]\n    pub fn end_field_props(&self) -> date_field::Props {\n        let min_value = self.ctx.start_date.clone().or_else(|| self.ctx.min.clone());\n\n        date_field::Props {\n            id: self.ctx.ids.part(\"end\"),\n            value: Some(self.ctx.end_date.clone()),\n            min_value,\n            max_value: self.ctx.max.clone(),\n            disabled: self.ctx.disabled,\n            readonly: self.ctx.readonly,\n            required: self.ctx.required,\n            aria_label: Some((self.ctx.messages.end_label)(&self.ctx.locale)),\n            force_leading_zeros: self.ctx.force_leading_zeros,\n            ..date_field::Props::default()\n        }\n    }",
  "new_string": "    /// Builds the child [`date_field::Props`] for the start date input.\n    ///\n    /// Both child fields are bounded only by the global `min`/`max`, never by\n    /// the opposite endpoint: a child `DateField` clamps completed dates to its\n    /// own `max_value`/`min_value`, so binding the start field to the current\n    /// end date would clamp away an out-of-order edit before the parent can\n    /// perform the documented swap. Cross-field ordering is enforced by\n    /// [`recompute_range`] when the changed value reaches the parent, not by the\n    /// child's clamp. `invalid` reflects whether this field's own value lies\n    /// outside the global bounds.\n    #[must_use]\n    pub fn start_field_props(&self) -> date_field::Props {\n        date_field::Props {\n            id: self.ctx.ids.part(\"start\"),\n            value: Some(self.ctx.start_date.clone()),\n            min_value: self.ctx.min.clone(),\n            max_value: self.ctx.max.clone(),\n            disabled: self.ctx.disabled,\n            readonly: self.ctx.readonly,\n            required: self.ctx.required,\n            invalid: date_out_of_bounds(\n                self.ctx.start_date.as_ref(),\n                self.ctx.min.as_ref(),\n                self.ctx.max.as_ref(),\n            ),\n            aria_label: Some((self.ctx.messages.start_label)(&self.ctx.locale)),\n            force_leading_zeros: self.ctx.force_leading_zeros,\n            ..date_field::Props::default()\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// Builds the child [`date_field::Props`] for the end date input.\n    ///\n    /// Bounded only by the global `min`/`max` for the same reason as\n    /// [`Api::start_field_props`]; `invalid` reflects whether the end field's\n    /// own value lies outside the global bounds.\n    #[must_use]\n    pub fn end_field_props(&self) -> date_field::Props {\n        date_field::Props {\n            id: self.ctx.ids.part(\"end\"),\n            value: Some(self.ctx.end_date.clone()),\n            min_value: self.ctx.min.clone(),\n            max_value: self.ctx.max.clone(),\n            disabled: self.ctx.disabled,\n            readonly: self.ctx.readonly,\n            required: self.ctx.required,\n            invalid: date_out_of_bounds(\n                self.ctx.end_date.as_ref(),\n                self.ctx.min.as_ref(),\n                self.ctx.max.as_ref(),\n            ),\n            aria_label: Some((self.ctx.messages.end_label)(&self.ctx.locale)),\n            force_leading_zeros: self.ctx.force_leading_zeros,\n            ..date_field::Props::default()\n        }\n    }"
}

  "new_string": "        if let Some(name) = &self.ctx.name {\n            attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Name, name);\n        }\n\n        // A disabled control is excluded from form submission; mark it disabled\n        // rather than submit a stale value (mirrors `date_picker`).\n        if self.ctx.disabled {\n            attrs.set_bool(HtmlAttr::Disabled, true);\n        }\n\n        let value = if let Some(range) = self.ctx.value.get() {\n            range.to_iso8601()\n        } else {\n            String::new()\n        };\n\n        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Value, value);\n\n        attrs\n    }\n\n    /// Returns attributes for the separate hidden input carrying the start date.\n    ///\n    /// Only meaningful when `start_name` is set on `Props`. The value is the\n    /// start field's own date (which may be set before a complete range\n    /// exists), so a partially-entered range still submits the start date.\n    #[must_use]\n    pub fn start_hidden_input_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {\n        let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();\n        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Type, \"hidden\");\n\n        if let Some(name) = &self.ctx.start_name {\n            attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Name, name);\n        }\n\n        if self.ctx.disabled {\n            attrs.set_bool(HtmlAttr::Disabled, true);\n        }\n\n        let value = match &self.ctx.start_date {\n            Some(start) => start.to_iso8601(),\n            None => String::new(),\n        };\n\n        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Value, value);\n\n        attrs\n    }\n\n    /// Returns attributes for the separate hidden input carrying the end date.\n    ///\n    /// Only meaningful when `end_name` is set on `Props`. The value is the end\n    /// field's own date, so a partially-entered range still submits the end\n    /// date.\n    #[must_use]\n    pub fn end_hidden_input_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {\n        let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();\n        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Type, \"hidden\");\n\n        if let Some(name) = &self.ctx.end_name {\n            attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Name, name);\n        }\n\n        if self.ctx.disabled {\n            attrs.set_bool(HtmlAttr::Disabled, true);\n        }\n\n        let value = match &self.ctx.end_date {\n            Some(end) => end.to_iso8601(),\n            None => String::new(),\n        };\n\n        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Value, value);\n\n        attrs"

max to the current end (or the end\n    // field's min to the current start) would let the child clamp away an\n    // out-of-order edit before the parent could perform the documented swap.\n    let svc = service_with(\n        Props {\n            min: Some(date(2025, 1, 1)),\n            max: Some(date(2025, 12, 31)),\n            default_value: Some(range(date(2025, 6, 1), date(2025, 6, 30))),\n            ..props()\n        },\n        en_us(),\n    );\n\n    let api = svc.connect(&|_| {});\n\n    let start = api.start_field_props();\n    let end = api.end_field_props();\n\n    assert_eq!(start.min_value, Some(date(2025, 1, 1)));\n    assert_eq!(start.max_value, Some(date(2025, 12, 31)));\n    assert_eq!(end.min_value, Some(date(2025, 1, 1)));\n    assert_eq!(end.max_value, Some(date(2025, 12, 31)));\n    assert_eq!(start.value, Some(Some(date(2025, 6, 1))));\n    assert_eq!(end.value, Some(Some(date(2025, 6, 30))));\n}\n\n#[test]\nfn out_of_bounds_endpoints_mark_their_child_field_invalid() {\n    let svc = service_with(\n        Props {\n            min: Some(date(2025, 6, 1)),\n            max: Some(date(2025, 6, 30)),\n            // start below min, end above max (set programmatically).\n            default_value: Some(range(date(2025, 5, 1), date(2025, 7, 15))),\n            ..props()\n        },\n        en_us(),\n    );\n\n    let api = svc.connect(&|_| {});\n\n    assert!(api.start_field_props().invalid);\n    assert!(api.end_field_props().invalid);\n}\n\n#[test]\nfn in_bounds_endpoints_keep_child_fields_valid() {\n    let svc = service_with(\n        Props {\n            min: Some(date(2025, 1, 1)),\n            max: Some(date(2025, 12, 31)),\n            default_value: Some(range(date(2025, 6, 1), date(2025, 6, 30))),\n            ..props()\n        },\n        en_us(),\n    );\n\n    let api = svc.connect(&|_| {});\n\n    assert!(!api.start_field_props().invalid);\n    assert!(!api.end_field_props().invalid);\n}"

```
    pub fn compare_within_calendar(&self, other: &Self) -> Option<Ordering> {
        if self.calendar != other.calendar {
            return None;
        }

        if self.era.as_ref().map(|value| value.code.as_str())
            != other.era.as_ref().map(|value| value.code.as_str())
        {
            return None;
        }

        Some((self.year, self.month, self.day).cmp(&(other.year, other.month, other.day)))
    }
```

  "new_string": "    drop(svc.send(Event::StartValueChange(None)));\n\n    assert_eq!(*svc.context().value.get(), None);\n}\n\n#[test]\nfn incomparable_dates_clear_the_derived_range() {\n    // Start a valid Gregorian range, then change the start to an ISO-8601 date.\n    // The two dates belong to different calendar systems, so they are not\n    // comparable and `DateRange::normalized` returns `None`; the derived range\n    // must be cleared rather than left stale.\n    let mut svc = service_with(\n        Props {\n            default_value: Some(range(date(2025, 6, 1), date(2025, 6, 15))),\n            ..props()\n        },\n        en_us(),\n    );\n\n    let iso = CalendarDate::new_iso8601(2025, 6, 10).expect(\"valid ISO date\");\n    drop(svc.send(Event::StartValueChange(Some(iso.clone()))));\n\n    assert_eq!(svc.context().start_date, Some(iso));\n    assert_eq!(*svc.context().value.get(), None);\n}\n\n// ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n// Reactive prop synchronization\n// ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n#[test]\nfn controlled_value_update_syncs_after_mount() {\n    let mut svc = service_with(\n        Props {\n            value: Some(Some(range(date(2025, 6, 1), date(2025, 6, 15)))),\n            name: Some(String::from(\"trip-range\")),\n            ..props()\n        },\n        en_us(),\n    );\n\n    drop(svc.set_props(Props {\n        value: Some(Some(range(date(2025, 9, 1), date(2025, 9, 30)))),\n        name: Some(String::from(\"trip-range\")),\n        ..props()\n    }));\n\n    let ctx = svc.context();\n    assert_eq!(ctx.start_date, Some(date(2025, 9, 1)));\n    assert_eq!(ctx.end_date, Some(date(2025, 9, 30)));\n    assert_eq!(\n        *ctx.value.get(),\n        Some(range(date(2025, 9, 1), date(2025, 9, 30)))\n    );\n\n    assert_eq!(\n        attr(&svc.connect(&|_| {}).hidden_input_attrs(), HtmlAttr::Value).as_deref(),\n        Some(\"2025-09-01/2025-09-30\")\n    );\n}\n\n#[test]\nfn prop_updates_sync_bounds_flags_and_names() {\n    let mut svc = service();\n\n    drop(svc.set_props(Props {\n        min: Some(date(2025, 1, 1)),\n        max: Some(date(2025, 12, 31)),\n        readonly: true,\n        required: true,\n        name: Some(String::from(\"range\")),\n        has_description: true,\n        ..props()\n    }));\n\n    let ctx = svc.context();\n    assert_eq!(ctx.min, Some(date(2025, 1, 1)));\n    assert_eq!(ctx.max, Some(date(2025, 12, 31)));\n    assert!(ctx.readonly);\n    assert!(ctx.required);\n    assert_eq!(ctx.name.as_deref(), Some(\"range\"));\n    assert!(ctx.has_description);\n}\n\n#[test]\nfn sync_props_processes_even_when_currently_disabled() {\n    let mut svc = service_with(\n        Props {\n            disabled: true,\n            ..props()\n        },\n        en_us(),\n    );\n\n    // Re-enabling via props must reach the context even though the component\n    // is disabled when the event is processed.\n    drop(svc.set_props(props()));\n\n    assert!(!svc.context().disabled);\n}\n\n// ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n// Validation\n// ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────"

  "old_string": "impl Context {\n    /// Returns `true` when the current range violates the min/max bounds.\n    /// The stored range is always normalized (start <= end), so the only source\n    /// of invalidity is a start earlier than `min` or an end later than `max`.\n    pub fn is_invalid(&self) -> bool {\n        let Some(range) = self.value.get() else { return false };\n        let below_min = self.min.as_ref().is_some_and(|min| {\n            matches!(range.start.compare_within_calendar(min), Some(Ordering::Less))\n        });\n        let above_max = self.max.as_ref().is_some_and(|max| {\n            matches!(range.end.compare_within_calendar(max), Some(Ordering::Greater))\n        });\n        below_min || above_max\n    }\n}",
  "new_string": "impl Context {\n    /// Returns `true` when the current range violates the min/max bounds.\n    /// The stored range is always normalized (start <= end), so it lies within\n    /// `[min, max]` iff neither endpoint is out of bounds.\n    pub fn is_invalid(&self) -> bool {\n        let Some(range) = self.value.get() else { return false };\n        date_out_of_bounds(Some(&range.start), self.min.as_ref(), self.max.as_ref())\n            || date_out_of_bounds(Some(&range.end), self.min.as_ref(), self.max.as_ref())\n    }\n}\n\n/// Returns `true` when `date` is present and outside the inclusive `[min, max]`\n/// bounds (absent bound = unbounded on that side). Shared by `is_invalid` and\n/// the per-field `invalid` flags on the child `DateField` props.\nfn date_out_of_bounds(\n    date: Option<&CalendarDate>,\n    min: Option<&CalendarDate>,\n    max: Option<&CalendarDate>,\n) -> bool {\n    let Some(date) = date else { return false };\n    let below = min.is_some_and(|m| matches!(date.compare_within_calendar(m), Some(Ordering::Less)));\n    let above = max.is_some_and(|m| matches!(date.compare_within_calendar(m), Some(Ordering::Greater)));\n    below || above\n}"
}

251	    fn transition(
252	        _state: &Self::State,
253	        event: &Self::Event,
254	        ctx: &Self::Context,
255	        _props: &Self::Props,
256	    ) -> Option<TransitionPlan<Self>> {
257	        if ctx.disabled { return None; }
258	
259	        match event {
260	            Event::FocusStart => Some(TransitionPlan::to(State::StartFocused)
261	                .apply(|ctx: &mut Context| ctx.active_field = Some(ActiveField::Start))),
262	
263	            Event::FocusEnd => Some(TransitionPlan::to(State::EndFocused)
264	                .apply(|ctx: &mut Context| ctx.active_field = Some(ActiveField::End))),
265	
266	            Event::BlurAll => Some(TransitionPlan::to(State::Idle)
267	                .apply(|ctx: &mut Context| ctx.active_field = None)),
268	
269	            Event::SetRange(range) => {
270	                if ctx.readonly { return None; }
271	                let range = range.clone();
272	                Some(TransitionPlan::context_only(move |ctx: &mut Context| {
273	                    ctx.start_date = range.as_ref().map(|r| r.start.clone());
274	                    ctx.end_date = range.as_ref().map(|r| r.end.clone());
275	                    ctx.value.set(range);
276	                }))
277	            }
278	
279	            Event::StartValueChange(date) => {
280	                if ctx.readonly { return None; }
281	                let date = date.clone();
282	                Some(TransitionPlan::context_only(move |ctx: &mut Context| {
283	                    ctx.start_date = date;
284	                    recompute_range(ctx);
285	                }))
286	            }
287	
288	            Event::EndValueChange(date) => {
289	                if ctx.readonly { return None; }
290	                let date = date.clone();
291	                Some(TransitionPlan::context_only(move |ctx: &mut Context| {
292	                    ctx.end_date = date;
293	                    recompute_range(ctx);
294	                }))
295	            }
296	        }
297	    }
298	}
299	
300	/// Recomputes the derived complete range from the two field values. When both
301	/// fields hold a value the range is normalized (swapping if needed so
302	/// `start <= end`) and the normalized order is reflected back into the fields.
303	/// When either field is empty the range is incomplete and the value is cleared.
304	fn recompute_range(ctx: &mut Context) {
305	    match (ctx.start_date.clone(), ctx.end_date.clone()) {
306	        (Some(start), Some(end)) => {
307	            if let Some(range) = DateRange::normalized(start, end) {
308	                ctx.start_date = Some(range.start.clone());
309	                ctx.end_date = Some(range.end.clone());
310	                ctx.value.set(Some(range));
311	            }
312	        }
313	        _ => ctx.value.set(None),
314	    }
315	}

When both\n/// fields hold a value the range is normalized (swapping if needed so\n/// `start <= end`) and the normalized order is reflected back into the fields.\n/// When either field is empty the range is incomplete and the value is cleared.\nfn recompute_range(ctx: &mut Context) {\n    match (ctx.start_date.clone(), ctx.end_date.clone()) {\n        (Some(start), Some(end)) => {\n            if let Some(range) = DateRange::normalized(start, end) {\n                ctx.start_date = Some(range.start.clone());\n                ctx.end_date = Some(range.end.clone());\n                ctx.value.set(Some(range));\n            }\n        }\n        _ => ctx.value.set(None),\n    }\n}\n```",
  "new_string": "            Event::EndValueChange(date) => {\n                if ctx.readonly { return None; }\n                let date = date.clone();\n                Some(TransitionPlan::context_only(move |ctx: &mut Context| {\n                    ctx.end_date = date;\n                    recompute_range(ctx);\n                }))\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    fn on_props_changed(old: &Self::Props, new: &Self::Props) -> Vec<Self::Event> {\n        debug_assert_eq!(old.id, new.id, \"date_range_field::Props.id must remain stable\");\n        if old == new { Vec::new() } else { vec![Event::SyncProps(Box::new(new.clone()))] }\n    }\n}\n\n/// Recomputes the derived complete range from the two field values. When both\n/// fields hold a value the range is normalized (swapping if needed so\n/// `start <= end`) and the normalized order is reflected back into the fields.\n/// When the two dates are not comparable (different calendars/eras supplied via\n/// the setters) `normalized` returns `None` and the derived range is cleared\n/// rather than left stale. When either field is empty the range is incomplete\n/// and the value is cleared.\nfn recompute_range(ctx: &mut Context) {\n    match (ctx.start_date.clone(), ctx.end_date.clone()) {\n        (Some(start), Some(end)) => match DateRange::normalized(start, end) {\n            Some(range) => {\n                ctx.start_date = Some(range.start.clone());\n                ctx.end_date = Some(range.end.clone());\n                ctx.value.set(Some(range));\n            }\n            None => ctx.value.set(None),\n        },\n        _ => ctx.value.set(None),\n    }\n}\n\n/// Re-derives mutable context fields from changed props. `id` is fixed at\n/// construction. A controlled component (`props.value` is `Some`) reconciles the\n/// controlled override and the per-field working values to the new range; an\n/// uncontrolled component keeps its internal working state and drops any stale\n/// controlled override.\nfn sync_props(ctx: &mut Context, props: &Props) {\n    ctx.min = props.min.clone();\n    ctx.max = props.max.clone();\n    ctx.disabled = props.disabled;\n    ctx.readonly = props.readonly;\n    ctx.required = props.required;\n    ctx.name = props.name.clone();\n    ctx.start_name = props.start_name.clone();\n    ctx.end_name = props.end_name.clone();\n    ctx.has_description = props.has_description;\n    ctx.has_error_message = props.has_error_message;\n    ctx.force_leading_zeros = props.force_leading_zeros;\n\n    if let Some(controlled) = &props.value {\n        ctx.value.sync_controlled(Some(controlled.clone()));\n        ctx.start_date = controlled.as_ref().map(|r| r.start.clone());\n        ctx.end_date = controlled.as_ref().map(|r| r.end.clone());\n    } else {\n        ctx.value.sync_controlled(None);\n    }\n}\n```"

483	    /// bound is the global `min`; its upper bound is the current end date when

The end field's upper bound\n    /// is the global `max`; its lower bound is the current start date when set,\n    /// otherwise the global `min`.\n    pub fn end_field_props(&self) -> date_field::Props {\n        date_field::Props {\n            id: self.ctx.ids.part(\"end\"),\n            value: Some(self.ctx.end_date.clone()),\n            min_value: self.ctx.start_date.clone().or_else(|| self.ctx.min.clone()),\n            max_value: self.ctx.max.clone(),\n            disabled: self.ctx.disabled,\n            readonly: self.ctx.readonly,\n            required: self.ctx.required,\n            aria_label: Some((self.ctx.messages.end_label)(&self.ctx.locale)),\n            force_leading_zeros: self.ctx.force_leading_zeros,\n            ..Default::default()\n        }\n    }",
  "new_string": "    /// Build DateField Props for the start date input. Both child fields are\n    /// bounded only by the global `min`/`max`, never by the opposite endpoint: a\n    /// child `DateField` clamps completed dates to its own bounds, so binding\n    /// the start field's max to the current end would clamp away an out-of-order\n    /// edit before the parent could perform the documented swap. Cross-field\n    /// ordering is enforced by `recompute_range` in the parent. `invalid`\n    /// reflects whether this field's own value lies outside the global bounds.\n    pub fn start_field_props(&self) -> date_field::Props {\n        date_field::Props {\n            id: self.ctx.ids.part(\"start\"),\n            value: Some(self.ctx.start_date.clone()),\n            min_value: self.ctx.min.clone(),\n            max_value: self.ctx.max.clone(),\n            disabled: self.ctx.disabled,\n            readonly: self.ctx.readonly,\n            required: self.ctx.required,\n            invalid: date_out_of_bounds(self.ctx.start_date.as_ref(), self.ctx.min.as_ref(), self.ctx.max.as_ref()),\n            aria_label: Some((self.ctx.messages.start_label)(&self.ctx.locale)),\n            force_leading_zeros: self.ctx.force_leading_zeros,\n            ..Default::default()\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// Build DateField Props for the end date input. Bounded only by the global\n    /// `min`/`max` for the same reason as `start_field_props`; `invalid` reflects\n    /// whether the end field's own value lies outside the global bounds.\n    pub fn end_field_props(&self) -> date_field::Props {\n        date_field::Props {\n            id: self.ctx.ids.part(\"end\"),\n            value: Some(self.ctx.end_date.clone()),\n            min_value: self.ctx.min.clone(),\n            max_value: self.ctx.max.clone(),\n            disabled: self.ctx.disabled,\n            readonly: self.ctx.readonly,\n            required: self.ctx.required,\n            invalid: date_out_of_bounds(self.ctx.end_date.as_ref(), self.ctx.min.as_ref(), self.ctx.max.as_ref()),\n            aria_label: Some((self.ctx.messages.end_label)(&self.ctx.locale)),\n            force_leading_zeros: self.ctx.force_leading_zeros,\n            ..Default::default()\n        }\n    }"
}

  "old_string": "        if let Some(ref name) = self.ctx.name {\n            attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Name, name);\n        }\n        // `DateRange::to_iso8601` already emits the `start/end` interval form.\n        let iso_value = match self.ctx.value.get() {\n            Some(range) => range.to_iso8601(),\n            None => String::new(),\n        };\n        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Value, iso_value);\n        attrs\n    }\n\n    /// Attributes for a separate hidden input carrying the start date.\n    /// Only meaningful when `start_name` is set on Props.\n    pub fn start_hidden_input_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {\n        let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();\n        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Type, \"hidden\");\n        if let Some(name) = &self.ctx.start_name {\n            attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Name, name);\n        }\n        let value = match self.ctx.value.get() {\n            Some(range) => range.start.to_iso8601(),\n            None => String::new(),\n        };\n        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Value, value);\n        attrs\n    }\n\n    /// Attributes for a separate hidden input carrying the end date.\n    /// Only meaningful when `end_name` is set on Props.\n    pub fn end_hidden_input_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {\n        let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();\n        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Type, \"hidden\");\n        if let Some(name) = &self.ctx.end_name {\n            attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Name, name);\n        }\n        let value = match self.ctx.value.get() {\n            Some(range) => range.end.to_iso8601(),\n            None => String::new(),\n        };\n        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Value, value);\n        attrs\n    }",
  "new_string": "        if let Some(ref name) = self.ctx.name {\n            attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Name, name);\n        }\n        // A disabled control is excluded from form submission (mirrors date_picker).\n        if self.ctx.disabled { attrs.set_bool(HtmlAttr::Disabled, true); }\n        // `DateRange::to_iso8601` already emits the `start/end` interval form.\n        let iso_value = match self.ctx.value.get() {\n            Some(range) => range.to_iso8601(),\n            None => String::new(),\n        };\n        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Value, iso_value);\n        attrs\n    }\n\n    /// Attributes for a separate hidden input carrying the start date. Only\n    /// meaningful when `start_name` is set on Props. The value is the start\n    /// field's own date (set even before a complete range exists), so a\n    /// partially-entered range still submits the start date.\n    pub fn start_hidden_input_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {\n        let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();\n        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Type, \"hidden\");\n        if let Some(name) = &self.ctx.start_name {\n            attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Name, name);\n        }\n        if self.ctx.disabled { attrs.set_bool(HtmlAttr::Disabled, true); }\n        let value = match &self.ctx.start_date {\n            Some(start) => start.to_iso8601(),\n            None => String::new(),\n        };\n        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Value, value);\n        attrs\n    }\n\n    /// Attributes for a separate hidden input carrying the end date. Only\n    /// meaningful when `end_name` is set on Props; uses the end field's own date.\n    pub fn end_hidden_input_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {\n        let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();\n        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Type, \"hidden\");\n        if let Some(name) = &self.ctx.end_name {\n            attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Name, name);\n        }\n        if self.ctx.disabled { attrs.set_bool(HtmlAttr::Disabled, true); }\n        let value = match &self.ctx.end_date {\n            Some(end) => end.to_iso8601(),\n            None => String::new(),\n        };\n        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Value, value);\n        attrs\n    }"
}

> AGENT

All 8 Codex threads addressed, replied to, and resolved; `@codex review` re-triggered for pass 2. The polling loop is running in the background and will notify me at the next verdict.

Summary of this round (commit `37b2bdab`, pushed to PR #709):

| Codex finding | Fix |
|---|---|
| **P1** prop sync (×2 threads) | `SyncProps` event + `on_props_changed` re-derive context via `set_props`; processed even when disabled |
| Stale range on normalization failure | `recompute_range` clears `value` on the incomparable-dates `None` arm |
| Cross-field clamp defeats swap | child fields bounded by global `min`/`max` only; parent normalizes |
| Partial separate-input values | start/end hidden inputs derive from each field's own date |
| Invalidity not on child fields | per-endpoint `invalid` via shared `date_out_of_bounds` |
| Disabled inputs still submit | `disabled` attr on all hidden inputs |

+9 tests added, module coverage held at 100% line/function, spec synchronized, all 10 `xci-fast` gates green, all threads replied-to and resolved. Waiting on Codex's pass-2 verdict — I'll handle 👍 (done) or any new threads when the loop fires.
